# Build-input tracer (level 1) Implementation Plan

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** A CLI, `./trace <app> --from A --to B [--task compileJava|fingerprint]`, that lists which commits in a git range changed an app's build inputs, using Gradle task cache keys as the verdict and bazel-diff as an informational second opinion, on a Gradle + Bazel multi-module Java repo built for learning Gradle.

**Architecture:** One source tree, two build descriptions. `build-logic/` (Gradle included build, Java plugins) holds the conventions and a cacheable `fingerprint` task. `tools/tracer/` is a Java CLI that materialises each commit in a throwaway git worktree, runs `gradlew <task> -Dorg.gradle.caching.debug=true` to read the task's cache key and per-input fingerprints, runs `bazel-diff` for the Bazel column, and compares adjacent commits. `tools/scenarios.sh` replays seven teaching commits and asserts the expected verdicts.

**Tech Stack:** Java 21 bytecode on JDK 25, Gradle 9.8.1 (wrapper), Bazel 9.1.0 with rules_java 9.9.0 and rules_jvm_external 7.2, JUnit Jupiter 6.1.3, Gradle TestKit, bazel-diff v60.0.0 (prebuilt binary), commons-lang3 3.18.0 (3.14.0–3.17.x carry CVE-2025-48924), gson 2.10.1.

**Spec:** `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-16-build-input-tracer-design.md`. Read sections 4 (module graph), 5 (CLI), 8 (scenario table) and 14 (verified facts and amendments) before starting. Fifteen tasks, one commit each.

**Conventions for every task:** work on branch `feature/level1-input-tracer`; tests first; run the task's test command before committing; one logical change per commit, ≤ 400 hand-written lines (generated files — `gradlew`, `gradle/wrapper/*`, `*.lockfile`, `maven_install.json` — do not count); docs for a component land in the same commit as the component; no `Co-Authored-By` trailers; commit body says *why*.

---

- [ ] **Step 4: Write the TestKit helper and the failing functional tests**

`build-logic/src/test/java/io/joeyang/tracer/buildlogic/GradleProject.java` (TestKit shares one build cache across every test method by default; three tests compile byte-identical sample code, so without a per-project cache the last one would see `FROM_CACHE` where it asserts `SUCCESS`):
```java
package io.joeyang.tracer.buildlogic;

import java.io.IOException;
import java.nio.file.Files;
import java.nio.file.Path;
import org.gradle.testkit.runner.BuildResult;
import org.gradle.testkit.runner.GradleRunner;

/** Writes a throwaway Gradle project that can apply the convention plugins under test. */
final class GradleProject {
    static final String CATALOG = """
            [versions]
            junit = "6.1.3"
            [libraries]
            junit-bom = { module = "org.junit:junit-bom", version.ref = "junit" }
            junit-jupiter = { module = "org.junit.jupiter:junit-jupiter" }
            junit-platform-launcher = { module = "org.junit.platform:junit-platform-launcher" }
            """;

    final Path dir;
    final Path cacheDir;

    /** Each project gets its own local build cache, so test methods never see each other's FROM-CACHE hits. */
    GradleProject(Path dir) throws IOException {
        this(dir, dir.resolve(".build-cache"));
    }

    /** @param cacheDir local build cache for this project; two projects sharing it can be used to test FROM-CACHE across locations */
    GradleProject(Path dir, Path cacheDir) throws IOException {
        this.dir = dir;
        this.cacheDir = cacheDir;
        Files.createDirectories(dir);
        write("settings.gradle.kts", """
                dependencyResolutionManagement {
                    repositories { mavenCentral() }
                }
                buildCache {
                    local { directory = file("%s") }
                }
                rootProject.name = "sample"
                """.formatted(cacheDir.toString().replace("\\", "/")));
        write("gradle/libs.versions.toml", CATALOG);
        write("gradle.properties", "org.gradle.caching=true\n");
    }

    GradleProject write(String relative, String content) throws IOException {
        Path target = dir.resolve(relative);
        Files.createDirectories(target.getParent());
        Files.writeString(target, content);
        return this;
    }

    BuildResult run(String... args) {
        return runner(args).build();
    }

    BuildResult runAndFail(String... args) {
        return runner(args).buildAndFail();
    }

    private GradleRunner runner(String... args) {
        return GradleRunner.create()
                .withProjectDir(dir.toFile())
                .withPluginClasspath()
                .withArguments(args)
                .forwardOutput();
    }
}
```

`build-logic/src/test/java/io/joeyang/tracer/buildlogic/ConventionPluginsFunctionalTest.java`:
```java
package io.joeyang.tracer.buildlogic;

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertEquals;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertTrue;

import java.io.IOException;
import java.nio.file.Files;
import java.nio.file.Path;
import org.gradle.testkit.runner.BuildResult;
import org.gradle.testkit.runner.TaskOutcome;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.BeforeEach;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.io.TempDir;

class ConventionPluginsFunctionalTest {
    @TempDir Path tmp;
    GradleProject project;

    @BeforeEach
    void setUp() throws IOException {
        project = new GradleProject(tmp);
        project.write("src/main/java/sample/Hello.java", """
                package sample;
                public final class Hello {
                    public static String say() { return "hi"; }
                }
                """);
        project.write("src/test/java/sample/HelloTest.java", """
                package sample;
                import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertEquals;
                import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
                class HelloTest {
                    @Test void says() { assertEquals("hi", Hello.say()); }
                }
                """);
    }

    @Test
    void javaLibraryCompilesToJava21AndRunsJUnitPlatformTests() throws IOException {
        project.write("build.gradle.kts", "plugins { id(\"tracer.java-library\") }\n");

        BuildResult result = project.run("test", "jar");

        assertEquals(TaskOutcome.SUCCESS, result.task(":compileJava").getOutcome());
        assertEquals(TaskOutcome.SUCCESS, result.task(":test").getOutcome());
        byte[] cls = Files.readAllBytes(tmp.resolve("build/classes/java/main/sample/Hello.class"));
        int major = ((cls[6] & 0xff) << 8) | (cls[7] & 0xff);
        assertEquals(65, major, "class file major version must be Java 21 (65)");
    }

    @Test
    void locksAllConfigurationsWhenAskedToWriteLocks() throws IOException {
        project.write("build.gradle.kts", "plugins { id(\"tracer.java-library\") }\n");

        project.run("dependencies", "--write-locks");

        String lock = Files.readString(tmp.resolve("gradle.lockfile"));
        assertTrue(lock.contains("org.junit.jupiter:junit-jupiter:6.1.3="), lock);
    }

    @Test
    void javaApplicationInstallsALauncher() throws IOException {
        project.write("build.gradle.kts", """
                plugins { id("tracer.java-application") }
                application { mainClass.set("sample.Hello") }
                """);

        BuildResult result = project.run("installDist");

        assertEquals(TaskOutcome.SUCCESS, result.task(":installDist").getOutcome());
        assertTrue(Files.isExecutable(tmp.resolve("build/install/sample/bin/sample")));
    }

    @Test
    void jarsAreReproducible() throws IOException {
        project.write("build.gradle.kts", "plugins { id(\"tracer.java-library\") }\n");

        project.run("jar");
        byte[] first = Files.readAllBytes(tmp.resolve("build/libs/sample.jar"));
        project.run("clean", "jar", "--no-build-cache");
        byte[] second = Files.readAllBytes(tmp.resolve("build/libs/sample.jar"));

        assertTrue(java.util.Arrays.equals(first, second), "same sources must produce byte-identical jars");
    }

    @Test
    void failsClearlyWithoutTheVersionCatalog() throws IOException {
        project.write("build.gradle.kts", "plugins { id(\"tracer.java-library\") }\n");
        Files.delete(tmp.resolve("gradle/libs.versions.toml"));

        BuildResult result = project.runAndFail("help");

        assertTrue(result.getOutput().contains("version catalog"), result.getOutput());
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 6: Implement the three plugins**

`build-logic/src/main/java/io/joeyang/tracer/buildlogic/JavaBaseConventionPlugin.java`:
```java
package io.joeyang.tracer.buildlogic;

import org.gradle.api.GradleException;
import org.gradle.api.Plugin;
import org.gradle.api.Project;
import org.gradle.api.artifacts.MinimalExternalModuleDependency;
import org.gradle.api.artifacts.VersionCatalog;
import org.gradle.api.artifacts.VersionCatalogsExtension;
import org.gradle.api.artifacts.dsl.DependencyHandler;
import org.gradle.api.provider.Provider;
import org.gradle.api.tasks.bundling.Jar;
import org.gradle.api.tasks.compile.JavaCompile;
import org.gradle.api.tasks.testing.Test;

/**
 * Conventions shared by every module: Java 21 bytecode, JUnit platform tests,
 * reproducible jars and dependency locking. Library and application plugins build on it.
 */
public final class JavaBaseConventionPlugin implements Plugin<Project> {
    static final int RELEASE = 21;

    @Override
    public void apply(Project project) {
        project.getPluginManager().apply("java");
        project.getTasks().withType(JavaCompile.class).configureEach(task -> {
            task.getOptions().getRelease().set(RELEASE);
            task.getOptions().setEncoding("UTF-8");
        });
        project.getTasks().withType(Jar.class).configureEach(jar -> {
            jar.setPreserveFileTimestamps(false);
            jar.setReproducibleFileOrder(true);
        });
        project.getTasks().withType(Test.class).configureEach(Test::useJUnitPlatform);
        addJUnit(project);
        project.getDependencyLocking().lockAllConfigurations();
    }

    private static void addJUnit(Project project) {
        VersionCatalog libs = catalog(project);
        DependencyHandler deps = project.getDependencies();
        deps.addProvider("testImplementation", deps.platform(library(libs, "junit-bom")));
        deps.addProvider("testImplementation", library(libs, "junit-jupiter"));
        deps.addProvider("testRuntimeOnly", library(libs, "junit-platform-launcher"));
    }

    static VersionCatalog catalog(Project project) {
        VersionCatalogsExtension catalogs = project.getExtensions().findByType(VersionCatalogsExtension.class);
        if (catalogs == null) {
            throw new GradleException("tracer.java-base needs the 'libs' version catalog (gradle/libs.versions.toml)");
        }
        return catalogs.find("libs").orElseThrow(
                () -> new GradleException("version catalog 'libs' not found; expected gradle/libs.versions.toml"));
    }

    private static Provider<MinimalExternalModuleDependency> library(VersionCatalog libs, String alias) {
        return libs.findLibrary(alias).orElseThrow(
                () -> new GradleException("alias '" + alias + "' is missing from gradle/libs.versions.toml"));
    }
}
```

`build-logic/src/main/java/io/joeyang/tracer/buildlogic/JavaLibraryConventionPlugin.java`:
```java
package io.joeyang.tracer.buildlogic;

import org.gradle.api.Plugin;
import org.gradle.api.Project;

/** A library module: base conventions plus the java-library plugin (api vs implementation). */
public final class JavaLibraryConventionPlugin implements Plugin<Project> {
    @Override
    public void apply(Project project) {
        project.getPluginManager().apply(JavaBaseConventionPlugin.class);
        project.getPluginManager().apply("java-library");
    }
}
```

`build-logic/src/main/java/io/joeyang/tracer/buildlogic/JavaApplicationConventionPlugin.java`:
```java
package io.joeyang.tracer.buildlogic;

import org.gradle.api.Plugin;
import org.gradle.api.Project;

/** An application module: base conventions plus the application plugin (run, installDist). */
public final class JavaApplicationConventionPlugin implements Plugin<Project> {
    @Override
    public void apply(Project project) {
        project.getPluginManager().apply(JavaBaseConventionPlugin.class);
        project.getPluginManager().apply("application");
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 9: README stub and docs/gradle-build.md**

`README.md`:
```markdown
# gradle-tracer

A learning repo: the same Java modules built by Gradle and Bazel, plus a tracer that reports which
commits changed an application's build inputs. Design: `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-16-build-input-tracer-design.md`.

## Layout

See `docs/gradle-build.md` (Gradle side) and `docs/bazel-build.md` (Bazel side, added later).

## Build

    ./gradlew build        # all modules, tests included
```

`docs/gradle-build.md`:
```markdown
# Gradle build

## Layout
- `settings.gradle.kts` includes the `build-logic` build (`pluginManagement.includeBuild`) and declares
  Maven Central once for every project (`FAIL_ON_PROJECT_REPOS`).
- `gradle/libs.versions.toml` is the only place a dependency version appears. `build-logic` imports the same
  file so plugins can add JUnit from it.
- `gradle.properties` turns the build cache on (`org.gradle.caching=true`); the tracer depends on that.

## Convention plugins (`build-logic`, written in Java)
| id | applies | adds |
|---|---|---|
| `tracer.java-base` | `java` | `--release 21`, UTF-8, JUnit platform + JUnit from the catalog, reproducible jars, `lockAllConfigurations()` |
| `tracer.java-library` | base + `java-library` | `api` vs `implementation` separation |
| `tracer.java-application` | base + `application` | `run`, `installDist`; module sets `application.mainClass` |

## Dependency locking
Every module locks all configurations. Regenerate after a version change:

    ./gradlew <module>:dependencies --write-locks

## Tests
`./gradlew -p build-logic test` runs the TestKit tests (they need network for JUnit).
```

`utils/src/test/java/io/joeyang/tracer/utils/FormatterTest.java`:
```java
package io.joeyang.tracer.utils;

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertEquals;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertThrows;

import java.util.Locale;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;

class FormatterTest {
    @Test void moneyFormatsDollarsAndCents() { assertEquals("$12.34", Formatter.money(1234)); }
    @Test void moneyPadsSingleDigitCents() { assertEquals("$0.05", Formatter.money(5)); }
    @Test void moneyZero() { assertEquals("$0.00", Formatter.money(0)); }
    @Test void moneyNegativeKeepsSignInFront() { assertEquals("-$7.50", Formatter.money(-750)); }
    @Test void moneyNegativeSingleDigitCents() { assertEquals("-$0.05", Formatter.money(-5)); }
    @Test void moneyLongMaxValue() { assertEquals("$92233720368547758.07", Formatter.money(Long.MAX_VALUE)); }

    @Test void moneyRejectsLongMinValue() {
        IllegalArgumentException ex = assertThrows(IllegalArgumentException.class, () -> Formatter.money(Long.MIN_VALUE));
        assertEquals("cents must be greater than Long.MIN_VALUE", ex.getMessage());
    }

    @Test void moneyIsLocaleIndependent() {
        Locale previous = Locale.getDefault(Locale.Category.FORMAT);
        Locale.setDefault(Locale.Category.FORMAT, Locale.forLanguageTag("ar-EG"));
        try {
            assertEquals("$0.05", Formatter.money(5));
        } finally {
            Locale.setDefault(Locale.Category.FORMAT, previous);
        }
    }

    @Test void padAddsSpacesToWidth() { assertEquals("ab   ", Formatter.pad("ab", 5)); }
    @Test void padLeavesEqualWidthAlone() { assertEquals("abcde", Formatter.pad("abcde", 5)); }
    @Test void padLeavesLongerTextAlone() { assertEquals("abcdef", Formatter.pad("abcdef", 5)); }
    @Test void padZeroWidth() { assertEquals("", Formatter.pad("", 0)); }

    @Test void padRejectsNull() {
        IllegalArgumentException ex = assertThrows(IllegalArgumentException.class, () -> Formatter.pad(null, 3));
        assertEquals("text must not be null", ex.getMessage());
    }

    @Test void padRejectsNegativeWidth() {
        IllegalArgumentException ex = assertThrows(IllegalArgumentException.class, () -> Formatter.pad("a", -1));
        assertEquals("width must be >= 0, got -1", ex.getMessage());
    }
}
```

`utils/src/main/java/io/joeyang/tracer/utils/Formatter.java`:
```java
package io.joeyang.tracer.utils;

import java.util.Locale;

/** Small text helpers shared by every module. */
public final class Formatter {
    private Formatter() {}

    /**
     * Formats cents as dollars: 1234 → "$12.34". The sign goes in front: -750 → "-$7.50".
     *
     * @throws IllegalArgumentException if {@code cents} is {@code Long.MIN_VALUE}, which has no
     *     representable absolute value
     */
    public static String money(long cents) {
        if (cents == Long.MIN_VALUE) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("cents must be greater than Long.MIN_VALUE");
        }
        long abs = Math.abs(cents);
        String sign = cents < 0 ? "-" : "";
        return sign + "$" + (abs / 100) + "." + String.format(Locale.ROOT, "%02d", abs % 100);
    }

    /** Right-pads {@code text} with spaces to {@code width}; longer text is returned unchanged. */
    public static String pad(String text, int width) {
        if (text == null) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("text must not be null");
        }
        if (width < 0) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("width must be >= 0, got " + width);
        }
        if (text.length() >= width) {
            return text;
        }
        return text + " ".repeat(width - text.length());   // SCENARIO S1 edits this line (body only)
    }
}
```

`core/src/main/java/io/joeyang/tracer/core/Greeter.java`:
```java
package io.joeyang.tracer.core;

import io.joeyang.tracer.utils.Formatter;

/** Builds greetings. Used directly by appB and, through lib, by appA. */
public final class Greeter {
    private final int column;

    public Greeter(int column) {
        if (column < 0) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("column must be >= 0, got " + column);
        }
        this.column = column;
    }

    // Greets by name.
    public String greet(String name) {
        if (name == null || name.isBlank()) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("name must not be blank");
        }
        return Formatter.pad("Hello,", column) + name + "!";
    }
}
```
(Scenario S2 appends a `shout` method before the final brace; S5 rewrites the `// Greets by name.` comment.)

`lib/src/main/java/io/joeyang/tracer/lib/Service.java`:
```java
package io.joeyang.tracer.lib;

import io.joeyang.tracer.core.Greeter;
import org.apache.commons.lang3.StringUtils;

/**
 * Facade used by appA. It returns a {@link Greeter}, which is why lib declares core as an
 * {@code api} dependency: appA needs core on its compile classpath to call what it gets back.
 */
public final class Service {
    private final Greeter greeter = new Greeter(8);

    public Greeter greeter() {
        return greeter;
    }

    /** Strips and capitalises the name, then greets it. */
    public String describe(String name) {
        if (name == null) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("name must not be null");
        }
        return greeter.greet(StringUtils.capitalize(name.strip()));
    }
}
```

```java
import java.io.PrintStream;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;

class AppATest {

    }
    @Test void usesFirstArgument() {
        assertEquals("Hello,  Ada!" + System.lineSeparator() + "Hello,  ada!", AppA.run(new String[] {"ada"}));
    }
    @Test void stripsSurroundingWhitespaceConsistently() {
        assertEquals("Hello,  Ada!" + System.lineSeparator() + "Hello,  ada!", AppA.run(new String[] {" ada "}));
    }

                assertThrows(IllegalArgumentException.class, () -> AppA.run(new String[] {"  "})).getMessage());
    }
    @Test void rejectsExtraArguments() {

                assertThrows(IllegalArgumentException.class, () -> AppA.run(new String[] {"a", "b"})).getMessage());
    }

        assertEquals("appA: name must not be blank" + System.lineSeparator(), errBytes.toString());

}
```

```java
package io.joeyang.tracer.app.c;

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertEquals;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertInstanceOf;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertThrows;

import java.io.ByteArrayOutputStream;
import java.io.PrintStream;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;

class AppCTest {
    @Test void defaultsToTwelveThirtyFour() {
        assertEquals("{\"amount\":\"$12.34\",\"label\":\"total   \"}", AppC.run(new String[0]));
    }
    @Test void usesFirstArgumentAsCents() {
        assertEquals("{\"amount\":\"-$0.99\",\"label\":\"total   \"}", AppC.run(new String[] {"-99"}));
    }
    @Test void rejectsNonNumericArgument() {
        IllegalArgumentException e = assertThrows(IllegalArgumentException.class, () -> AppC.run(new String[] {"ten"}));
        assertEquals("expected a whole number of cents, got 'ten'", e.getMessage());
        assertInstanceOf(NumberFormatException.class, e.getCause());

    @Test void rejectsLongMinValue() {

                IllegalArgumentException.class, () -> AppC.run(new String[] {"-9223372036854775808"}));
        assertEquals("cents must be greater than Long.MIN_VALUE", e.getMessage());
    }
    @Test void rejectsOverflowingArgument() {
        IllegalArgumentException e = assertThrows(
                IllegalArgumentException.class, () -> AppC.run(new String[] {"9223372036854775808"}));
        assertEquals("expected a whole number of cents, got '9223372036854775808'", e.getMessage());
    }
    @Test void rejectsEmptyArgument() {
        IllegalArgumentException e = assertThrows(IllegalArgumentException.class, () -> AppC.run(new String[] {""}));
        assertEquals("expected a whole number of cents, got ''", e.getMessage());
    }
    @Test void rejectsExtraArguments() {
        IllegalArgumentException e = assertThrows(
                IllegalArgumentException.class, () -> AppC.run(new String[] {"250", "extra"}));
        assertEquals("expected at most one argument, got 2", e.getMessage());
    }

    @Test void main0PrintsResultAndReturnsZero() {
        ByteArrayOutputStream outBytes = new ByteArrayOutputStream();
        ByteArrayOutputStream errBytes = new ByteArrayOutputStream();
        int code = AppC.main0(new String[] {"250"}, new PrintStream(outBytes), new PrintStream(errBytes));
        assertEquals(0, code);
        assertEquals(
                "{\"amount\":\"$2.50\",\"label\":\"total   \"}" + System.lineSeparator(), outBytes.toString());
        assertEquals("", errBytes.toString());
    }

    @Test void main0ReportsBadArgumentOnStderr() {
        ByteArrayOutputStream outBytes = new ByteArrayOutputStream();
        ByteArrayOutputStream errBytes = new ByteArrayOutputStream();
        int code = AppC.main0(new String[] {"ten"}, new PrintStream(outBytes), new PrintStream(errBytes));
        assertEquals(2, code);
        assertEquals("", outBytes.toString());
        assertEquals(
                "appC: expected a whole number of cents, got 'ten'" + System.lineSeparator(), errBytes.toString());
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Implement the three apps**

`app/appA/src/main/java/io/joeyang/tracer/app/a/AppA.java`:
```java
package io.joeyang.tracer.app.a;

import io.joeyang.tracer.core.Greeter;
import io.joeyang.tracer.lib.Service;
import java.io.PrintStream;

/** appA talks to lib, and to the core type lib hands back. */
public final class AppA {
    private AppA() {}

    public static String run(String[] args) {
        if (args.length > 1) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("expected at most one argument, got " + args.length);
        }
        String name = args.length > 0 ? args[0].strip() : "world";
        Service service = new Service();
        Greeter greeter = service.greeter();          // a core type, reachable only because lib uses api(project(":core"))
        return service.describe(name) + System.lineSeparator() + greeter.greet(name);
    }

    static int main0(String[] args, PrintStream out, PrintStream err) {
        try {
            out.println(run(args));
            return 0;
        } catch (IllegalArgumentException e) {
            err.println("appA: " + e.getMessage());
            return 2;
        }
    }

    public static void main(String[] args) {
        int code = main0(args, System.out, System.err);
        if (code != 0) {
            System.exit(code);
        }
    }
}
```

`app/appB/src/main/java/io/joeyang/tracer/app/b/AppB.java`:

```java
package io.joeyang.tracer.app.b;

import io.joeyang.tracer.core.Greeter;
import java.io.PrintStream;

/** appB depends on core directly and knows nothing about lib or utils. */
public final class AppB {
    private AppB() {}

    public static String run(String[] args) {
        if (args.length > 1) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("expected at most one argument, got " + args.length);
        }
        String name = args.length > 0 ? args[0] : "world";
        return new Greeter(10).greet(name);
    }

    static int main0(String[] args, PrintStream out, PrintStream err) {
        try {
            out.println(run(args));
            return 0;
        } catch (IllegalArgumentException e) {
            err.println("appB: " + e.getMessage());
            return 2;
        }
    }

    public static void main(String[] args) {
        int code = main0(args, System.out, System.err);
        if (code != 0) {
            System.exit(code);
        }
    }
}
```

`app/appC/src/main/java/io/joeyang/tracer/app/c/AppC.java`:
```java
package io.joeyang.tracer.app.c;

import com.google.gson.Gson;
import io.joeyang.tracer.utils.Formatter;
import java.io.PrintStream;
import java.util.LinkedHashMap;
import java.util.Map;

/** appC depends on utils and on gson directly; it never touches core or lib. */
public final class AppC {
    private AppC() {}

    public static String run(String[] args) {
        if (args.length > 1) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("expected at most one argument, got " + args.length);
        }
        long cents = 1234L;
        if (args.length > 0) {
            try {
                cents = Long.parseLong(args[0]);
            } catch (NumberFormatException e) {
                throw new IllegalArgumentException("expected a whole number of cents, got '" + args[0] + "'", e);
            }
        }
        Map<String, String> doc = new LinkedHashMap<>();
        doc.put("amount", Formatter.money(cents));
        doc.put("label", Formatter.pad("total", 8));
        return new Gson().toJson(doc);
    }

    static int main0(String[] args, PrintStream out, PrintStream err) {
        try {
            out.println(run(args));
            return 0;
        } catch (IllegalArgumentException e) {
            err.println("appC: " + e.getMessage());
            return 2;
        }
    }

    public static void main(String[] args) {
        int code = main0(args, System.out, System.err);
        if (code != 0) {
            System.exit(code);
        }
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 8: Document the apps**

In `docs/gradle-build.md`, add three rows to the Modules table:

```markdown
| `app:appA` | `tracer.java-application` | `implementation(project(":lib"))` |
| `app:appB` | `tracer.java-application` | `implementation(project(":core"))` |
| `app:appC` | `tracer.java-application` | `implementation(project(":utils"))`, `implementation(libs.gson)` |
```

Then replace the section's last paragraph (the one ending "arrive in the next commit.") with:

```markdown
Each app depends on its libraries with `implementation`: nothing consumes an app, so no app type
needs to be visible further up the graph. `appA` reaches `core` only through `lib`'s `api` edge,
`appB` depends on `core` directly, and `appC` never touches `core` or `lib`. Each app validates
its argument; a bad one prints `appX: <reason>` to stderr and exits with code 2.

`./gradlew build` runs every module's tests (46 today).
```

```bash
behaviour is unit-tested without capturing stdout."
```

### Task 4: Bazel build for the same modules

**Files:**
- Create: `MODULE.bazel`, `.bazelrc`, `.bazelversion` (one line: `9.1.0`), `BUILD.bazel` (root, empty), `tools/BUILD.bazel` (empty), `tools/defs.bzl`
- Create: `utils/BUILD.bazel`, `core/BUILD.bazel`, `lib/BUILD.bazel`, `app/appA/BUILD.bazel`, `app/appB/BUILD.bazel`, `app/appC/BUILD.bazel`
- Create: `docs/bazel-build.md`
- Modify: `.gitignore` (`bazel-*` → `/bazel-*`; the unanchored pattern also ignored `docs/bazel-build.md`)
- Modify: `README.md` (drop "added later" from the Layout line; add `bazel test //...` to the Build section)
- Generated: `maven_install.json`, `MODULE.bazel.lock`

Verified on this machine (spike, 2026-10-16): Bazel 9.1.0 resolves rules_java ≥ 9.3 fine; rules_jvm_external 7.2 with `lock_file` + `bazel run @maven//:pin` works; without the `--tool_java_runtime_version` flag the build fails with `UnsupportedClassVersionError ... class file version 65.0 ... only recognizes up to 55.0` from rules_jvm_external's jar stamper, because the default tool runtime is Java 11. The flags below fix it.

- [ ] **Step 1: Module, rc and macro files**

`MODULE.bazel`:
```python
module(
    name = "gradle_tracer",
    version = "0.1.0",
)

bazel_dep(name = "rules_java", version = "9.9.0")
bazel_dep(name = "rules_jvm_external", version = "7.2")

maven = use_extension("@rules_jvm_external//:extensions.bzl", "maven")
maven.install(
    artifacts = [
        "org.apache.commons:commons-lang3:3.18.0",
        "com.google.code.gson:gson:2.10.1",
        "org.junit.jupiter:junit-jupiter-api:6.1.3",
        "org.junit.platform:junit-platform-console-standalone:6.1.3",
    ],
    lock_file = "//:maven_install.json",
    repositories = ["https://repo1.maven.org/maven2"],
)
use_repo(maven, "maven")
```
(The versions of commons-lang3 and gson must equal `gradle/libs.versions.toml`; the scenario script bumps both files together.)

`.bazelrc`:
```
# The machine has JDK 25, but Bazel compiles and runs on a managed remote JDK 21 so results are
# reproducible. The tool_* flags matter: rules_jvm_external's jar stamper is compiled for Java 21
# and the default tool runtime is Java 11 (UnsupportedClassVersionError without them).
build --java_language_version=21
build --java_runtime_version=remotejdk_21
build --tool_java_language_version=21
build --tool_java_runtime_version=remotejdk_21
test --test_output=errors
```

`BUILD.bazel` (root) and `tools/BUILD.bazel`: empty files (they make the directories packages so `//:maven_install.json` and `//tools:defs.bzl` resolve).

`tools/defs.bzl`:
```python
"""Repo-wide Bazel macros: shared javacopts and a JUnit 5 runner built on the console launcher."""

load("@rules_java//java:java_binary.bzl", "java_binary")
load("@rules_java//java:java_library.bzl", "java_library")
load("@rules_java//java:java_test.bzl", "java_test")

# The repo forbids deprecated APIs; Gradle has no equivalent flag here, so this is what surfaces them under Bazel.
# SCENARIO S6 appends "-Xlint:all" here (and to the Gradle convention plugin) in the same commit.
JAVACOPTS = ["-Xlint:deprecation"]

_JUNIT_API = ["@maven//:org_junit_jupiter_junit_jupiter_api"]
_JUNIT_RUNTIME = ["@maven//:org_junit_platform_junit_platform_console_standalone"]

def tracer_java_library(name, **kwargs):
    """java_library with the repo-wide javacopts."""
    java_library(name = name, javacopts = JAVACOPTS, **kwargs)

def tracer_java_binary(name, **kwargs):
    """java_binary with the repo-wide javacopts."""
    java_binary(name = name, javacopts = JAVACOPTS, **kwargs)

def junit5_test_suite(name, srcs, deps = [], runtime_deps = [], resources = [], resource_strip_prefix = None, **kwargs):
    """One java_test per *Test.java in srcs, executed through org.junit.platform.console.ConsoleLauncher.

    Sources that are not tests (fakes, helpers) and resources go into <name>_support, a testonly library
    every test depends on. The suite target <name> groups all tests.
    """
    tests = [s for s in srcs if s.endswith("Test.java")]
    helpers = [s for s in srcs if not s.endswith("Test.java")]
    support = []
    if helpers or resources:
        java_library(
            name = name + "_support",
            testonly = True,
            srcs = helpers,
            resources = resources,
            resource_strip_prefix = resource_strip_prefix,
            deps = deps + _JUNIT_API,
            javacopts = JAVACOPTS,
        )
        support = [":" + name + "_support"]
    targets = []
    for src in tests:
        class_name = src.removeprefix("src/test/java/").removesuffix(".java").replace("/", ".")
        target = class_name.rsplit(".", 1)[-1]
        kwargs.setdefault("size", "small")
        java_test(
            name = target,
            srcs = [src],
            deps = deps + support + _JUNIT_API,
            runtime_deps = runtime_deps + _JUNIT_RUNTIME,
            javacopts = JAVACOPTS,
            main_class = "org.junit.platform.console.ConsoleLauncher",
            use_testrunner = False,
            args = ["execute", "--select-class", class_name, "--disable-banner", "--details=summary", "--fail-if-no-tests"],
            **kwargs
        )
        targets.append(":" + target)
    native.test_suite(name = name, tests = targets)
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Docs**

`docs/bazel-build.md`:
```markdown
# Bazel build

The same `src/main/java` and `src/test/java` trees as Gradle. Outputs go to `bazel-*` symlinks (gitignored).

## Files
- `MODULE.bazel` — rules_java 9.9.0, rules_jvm_external 7.2, Maven artifacts pinned in `maven_install.json`
  (`REPIN=1 bazel run @maven//:pin` after any version change — only the very first pin runs without `REPIN`; the scenario script does this for S3/S4).
- `.bazelrc` — remote JDK 21 for targets *and* tools; see the comment in the file for why the tool flags are required.
- `.bazelversion` — pins the Bazel release itself; without it a Bazelisk install would run "latest", which can rewrite `MODULE.bazel.lock` and shift the bazel-diff hashes later tasks rely on.
- `tools/defs.bzl` — `tracer_java_library`/`tracer_java_binary` apply `JAVACOPTS`; `junit5_test_suite` creates one
  `java_test` per `*Test.java` running `org.junit.platform.console.ConsoleLauncher execute --select-class <class>`,
  with helper sources and resources in a `<name>_support` library.

## Edge mapping
| Gradle | Bazel |
|---|---|
| `implementation(project(":x"))` | `deps = ["//x"]` |
| `api(project(":x"))` | `deps = ["//x"], exports = ["//x"]` |
| `implementation(libs.foo)` | `deps = ["@maven//:group_artifact"]` |
| `application { mainClass }` | `java_binary(main_class, runtime_deps = [":app_lib"])` |

## Conventions
- Only files named `*Test.java` under `src/test/java` become test targets (one `java_test` each, run through
  `junit5_test_suite`). Everything else there — fakes, helpers, resources — compiles into the `<name>_support`
  library and never runs on its own, unlike Gradle, which runs anything JUnit discovers on the test classpath.
- `MODULE.bazel`'s `known_contributing_modules = ["protobuf"]` lets protobuf's own Maven artifacts (guava,
  checker-qual, error_prone_annotations) into `maven_install.json` and version resolution, even though nothing
  in this repo depends on them — it is rules_jvm_external quieting a multi-module contribution warning, not a
  new dependency edge.

## Not under Bazel
`build-logic` (Gradle plugins, TestKit tests) and the tracer's `e2eTest` suite are Gradle-only by nature.

## Commands
    bazel build //...   bazel test //...   bazel run //app/appA -- Ada
```

### Task 5: FingerprintTask (runtime-input oracle)

**Files:**
- Create: `build-logic/src/main/java/io/joeyang/tracer/buildlogic/FingerprintTask.java`
- Modify: `build-logic/src/main/java/io/joeyang/tracer/buildlogic/JavaBaseConventionPlugin.java` (register the task)
- Modify: `build-logic/src/test/java/io/joeyang/tracer/buildlogic/GradleProject.java` (local build-cache dir, copy helper)
- Test: `build-logic/src/test/java/io/joeyang/tracer/buildlogic/FingerprintTaskFunctionalTest.java`
- Modify: `docs/gradle-build.md`

Background for the engineer: Gradle computes a *build cache key* for every cacheable task from its implementation hash plus the fingerprints of its inputs. `@Classpath` inputs are fingerprinted by content (jar entries hashed; paths, timestamps and the order of entries inside a jar ignored; the order of classpath entries is significant but deterministic). So a task whose only input is the main runtime classpath has a key that changes exactly when the runtime bytes change. The task's action is irrelevant; the key is the product.

- [ ] **Step 1: Extend the TestKit helper**

Replace `build-logic/src/test/java/io/joeyang/tracer/buildlogic/GradleProject.java` with:
```java
package io.joeyang.tracer.buildlogic;

import java.io.IOException;
import java.nio.file.Files;
import java.nio.file.Path;
import java.util.Set;
import java.util.stream.Stream;
import org.gradle.testkit.runner.BuildResult;
import org.gradle.testkit.runner.GradleRunner;

/** Writes a throwaway Gradle project that can apply the convention plugins under test. */
final class GradleProject {
    static final String CATALOG = """
            [versions]
            junit = "6.1.3"
            [libraries]
            junit-bom = { module = "org.junit:junit-bom", version.ref = "junit" }
            junit-jupiter = { module = "org.junit.jupiter:junit-jupiter" }
            junit-platform-launcher = { module = "org.junit.platform:junit-platform-launcher" }
            """;

    final Path dir;
    final Path cacheDir;

    /** @param cacheDir shared local build cache; pass the same dir to two projects to test FROM-CACHE across locations */
    GradleProject(Path dir, Path cacheDir) throws IOException {
        this(dir, cacheDir, true);
    }

    /** @param writeScaffold false wraps an existing directory (see {@link #copyTo}) without overwriting its files */
    private GradleProject(Path dir, Path cacheDir, boolean writeScaffold) throws IOException {
        this.dir = dir;
        this.cacheDir = cacheDir;
        Files.createDirectories(dir);
        if (!writeScaffold) {
            return;
        }
        write("settings.gradle.kts", """
                dependencyResolutionManagement {
                    repositories { mavenCentral() }
                }
                buildCache {
                    local { directory = file("%s") }
                }
                rootProject.name = "sample"
                """.formatted(cacheDir.toString().replace("\\", "/")));
        write("gradle/libs.versions.toml", CATALOG);
        write("gradle.properties", "org.gradle.caching=true\n");
    }

    /** Each project gets its own local build cache, so test methods never see each other's FROM-CACHE hits. */
    GradleProject(Path dir) throws IOException {
        this(dir, dir.resolve(".build-cache"));
    }

    GradleProject write(String relative, String content) throws IOException {
        Path target = dir.resolve(relative);
        Files.createDirectories(target.getParent());
        Files.writeString(target, content);
        return this;
    }

    private static final Set<String> EXCLUDED_DIRS = Set.of("build", ".gradle", ".build-cache");

    /** Copies sources and build files (not build outputs, at any depth) to another directory sharing the same cache. */
    GradleProject copyTo(Path other) throws IOException {
        try (Stream<Path> paths = Files.walk(dir)) {
            for (Path p : paths.toList()) {
                Path rel = dir.relativize(p);
                boolean excluded = false;
                for (int i = 0; i < rel.getNameCount(); i++) {
                    if (EXCLUDED_DIRS.contains(rel.getName(i).toString())) {
                        excluded = true;
                        break;
                    }
                }
                if (excluded) {
                    continue;
                }
                Path target = other.resolve(rel);
                if (Files.isDirectory(p)) {
                    Files.createDirectories(target);
                } else {
                    Files.createDirectories(target.getParent());
                    Files.copy(p, target);
                }
            }
        }
        return new GradleProject(other, cacheDir, false);
    }

    BuildResult run(String... args) {
        return runner(args).build();
    }

    BuildResult runAndFail(String... args) {
        return runner(args).buildAndFail();
    }

    private GradleRunner runner(String... args) {
        return GradleRunner.create()
                .withProjectDir(dir.toFile())
                .withPluginClasspath()
                .withArguments(args)
                .forwardOutput();
    }
}
```
Compared with Task 1 this adds the private non-writing constructor and `copyTo`; the per-project cache directory already exists. `copyTo` wraps the copied files without rewriting them and keeps the *same* cache directory.

- [ ] **Step 2: Write the failing functional tests**

`build-logic/src/test/java/io/joeyang/tracer/buildlogic/FingerprintTaskFunctionalTest.java`:
```java
package io.joeyang.tracer.buildlogic;

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertEquals;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertTrue;

import java.io.IOException;
import java.nio.file.Files;
import java.nio.file.Path;
import java.util.regex.Pattern;
import org.gradle.testkit.runner.BuildResult;
import org.gradle.testkit.runner.TaskOutcome;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.BeforeEach;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.io.TempDir;

class FingerprintTaskFunctionalTest {
    static final String DEP_SOURCE = """
            package dep;
            /** Comment line one. */
            public final class Dep {
                public static String value() { return "v1"; }
            }
            """;

    @TempDir Path tmp;
    GradleProject project;

    @BeforeEach
    void setUp() throws IOException {
        project = new GradleProject(tmp.resolve("a"), tmp.resolve("cache"));
        project.write("settings.gradle.kts", Files.readString(project.dir.resolve("settings.gradle.kts")) + "include(\"dep\")\n");
        project.write("dep/build.gradle.kts", "plugins { id(\"tracer.java-library\") }\n");
        project.write("dep/src/main/java/dep/Dep.java", DEP_SOURCE);
        project.write("build.gradle.kts", """
                plugins { id("tracer.java-application") }
                dependencies { implementation(project(":dep")) }
                application { mainClass.set("app.Main") }
                """);
        project.write("src/main/java/app/Main.java", """
                package app;
                public final class Main {
                    public static void main(String[] args) { System.out.println(dep.Dep.value()); }
                }
                """);
    }

    @Test
    void runsAndListsTheRuntimeClasspath() throws IOException {
        BuildResult result = project.run(":fingerprint");

        assertEquals(TaskOutcome.SUCCESS, result.task(":fingerprint").getOutcome());
        String marker = Files.readString(project.dir.resolve("build/fingerprint/runtime-classpath.txt"));
        assertTrue(marker.contains("dep.jar"), marker);
    }

    @Test
    void secondRunIsUpToDate() {
        project.run(":fingerprint");
        BuildResult again = project.run(":fingerprint");

        assertEquals(TaskOutcome.UP_TO_DATE, again.task(":fingerprint").getOutcome());
    }

    @Test
    void keyDoesNotDependOnTheProjectLocation() throws IOException {
        project.run(":fingerprint");
        GradleProject moved = project.copyTo(tmp.resolve("b"));

        BuildResult result = moved.run(":fingerprint");

        assertEquals(TaskOutcome.FROM_CACHE, result.task(":fingerprint").getOutcome(),
                "same sources in a different directory must hit the cache");
    }

    @Test
    void keyChangesWhenADependencyBodyChangesButCompileKeyDoesNot() throws IOException {
        project.run(":fingerprint");
        project.write("dep/src/main/java/dep/Dep.java", DEP_SOURCE.replace("\"v1\"", "\"v2\""));

        BuildResult result = project.run(":fingerprint");

        assertEquals(TaskOutcome.SUCCESS, result.task(":fingerprint").getOutcome(), "runtime bytes changed");
        assertEquals(TaskOutcome.UP_TO_DATE, result.task(":compileJava").getOutcome(),
                "ABI unchanged, so the app's compileJava must be avoided");
    }

    @Test
    void keyUnchangedOnCommentOnlyEdit() throws IOException {
        project.run(":fingerprint");
        project.write("dep/src/main/java/dep/Dep.java", DEP_SOURCE.replace("Comment line one.", "Comment line two."));

        BuildResult result = project.run(":fingerprint");

        assertEquals(TaskOutcome.SUCCESS, result.task(":dep:compileJava").getOutcome(), "source changed, so dep recompiles");
        assertEquals(TaskOutcome.UP_TO_DATE, result.task(":fingerprint").getOutcome(), "but the bytecode is identical");
    }

    @Test
    void cacheDebugOutputNamesTheKey() {
        BuildResult result = project.run(":fingerprint", "-Dorg.gradle.caching.debug=true");

        Pattern key = Pattern.compile("Build cache key for task ':fingerprint' is [0-9a-f]{32}");
        assertTrue(key.matcher(result.getOutput()).find(), result.getOutput());
    }

    @Test
    void keyChangesWhenATransitiveDependencyVersionChangesButCompileKeyDoesNot() throws IOException {
        String depWithGson = """
                plugins { id("tracer.java-library") }
                dependencies { implementation("com.google.code.gson:gson:%s") }
                """;
        project.write("dep/build.gradle.kts", depWithGson.formatted("2.10.1"));
        project.run(":fingerprint");
        project.write("dep/build.gradle.kts", depWithGson.formatted("2.11.0"));

        BuildResult result = project.run(":fingerprint");

        assertEquals(TaskOutcome.SUCCESS, result.task(":fingerprint").getOutcome(), "a different gson jar is a different runtime input");
        assertEquals(TaskOutcome.UP_TO_DATE, result.task(":compileJava").getOutcome(),
                "gson is dep's implementation detail, not part of dep's api, so the app's compileJava must be avoided");
    }

    @Test
    void keyChangesWhenAPublicMethodIsAddedToADependency() throws IOException {
        project.run(":fingerprint");
        project.write("dep/src/main/java/dep/Dep.java", DEP_SOURCE.replace(
                "public static String value() { return \"v1\"; }",
                "public static String value() { return \"v1\"; }\n    public static String extra() { return \"x\"; }"));

        BuildResult result = project.run(":fingerprint");

        assertEquals(TaskOutcome.SUCCESS, result.task(":fingerprint").getOutcome(), "runtime bytes changed");
        assertEquals(TaskOutcome.SUCCESS, result.task(":compileJava").getOutcome(),
                "dep's ABI changed, so the app's compileJava must rerun");
    }

    @Test
    void failsClearlyWhenTheClasspathCannotResolve() throws IOException {
        project.write("build.gradle.kts", """
                plugins { id("tracer.java-application") }
                dependencies {
                    implementation(project(":dep"))
                    implementation("com.example:missing:1.0")
                }
                application { mainClass.set("app.Main") }
                """);

        BuildResult result = project.runAndFail(":fingerprint");

        assertTrue(result.getOutput().contains("com.example:missing:1.0"), result.getOutput());
    }
}
```
The `cacheDebugOutputNamesTheKey` pattern is the contract the tracer parses in Task 7; keep the two in sync.

- [ ] **Step 4: Implement the task and register it**

`build-logic/src/main/java/io/joeyang/tracer/buildlogic/FingerprintTask.java`:
```java
package io.joeyang.tracer.buildlogic;

import java.io.File;
import java.io.IOException;
import java.nio.file.Files;
import java.nio.file.Path;
import java.util.List;
import org.gradle.api.DefaultTask;
import org.gradle.api.file.ConfigurableFileCollection;
import org.gradle.api.file.RegularFileProperty;
import org.gradle.api.tasks.CacheableTask;
import org.gradle.api.tasks.Classpath;
import org.gradle.api.tasks.OutputFile;
import org.gradle.api.tasks.TaskAction;

/**
 * A cacheable task whose only input is the main runtime classpath. Its build-cache key is therefore
 * a content hash of everything the application ships: own classes, project jars, third-party jars.
 * {@code @Classpath} normalisation ignores paths and timestamps (and entry order inside a jar) but not contents,
 * so a library bump changes the key and a comment-only edit (identical bytecode) does not. The order of classpath
 * entries themselves is significant, and deterministic for a given build. The marker file exists only so the
 * task has an output and can be cached; the key is the product.
 */
@CacheableTask
public abstract class FingerprintTask extends DefaultTask {

    @Classpath
    public abstract ConfigurableFileCollection getRuntimeClasspath();

    @OutputFile
    public abstract RegularFileProperty getMarker();

    @TaskAction
    public void write() throws IOException {
        List<String> names = getRuntimeClasspath().getFiles().stream()
                .map(File::getName)
                .sorted()
                .toList();
        Path out = getMarker().get().getAsFile().toPath();
        Files.write(out, names);
    }
}
```

In `JavaBaseConventionPlugin.java` add the imports
```java
import org.gradle.api.tasks.SourceSet;
import org.gradle.api.tasks.SourceSetContainer;
```
a constant
```java
    static final String FINGERPRINT_TASK = "fingerprint";
```
and, as the last line of `apply(...)`:
```java
        registerFingerprint(project);
```
with the new method:
```java
    private static void registerFingerprint(Project project) {
        SourceSetContainer sourceSets = project.getExtensions().getByType(SourceSetContainer.class);
        project.getTasks().register(FINGERPRINT_TASK, FingerprintTask.class, task -> {
            task.setGroup("verification");
            task.setDescription("Cacheable no-op whose build-cache key fingerprints the main runtime classpath");
            task.getRuntimeClasspath().from(
                    sourceSets.getByName(SourceSet.MAIN_SOURCE_SET_NAME).getRuntimeClasspath());
            task.getMarker().set(project.getLayout().getBuildDirectory().file("fingerprint/runtime-classpath.txt"));
        });
    }
```
`sourceSets.main.runtimeClasspath` is the compiled output plus the `runtimeClasspath` configuration; Gradle infers the dependency on `compileJava`, `processResources` and every upstream `jar` from the file collection, so no explicit `dependsOn` is needed.

- [ ] **Step 7: Document**

Append to `docs/gradle-build.md`:
```markdown
## The fingerprint task
`tracer.java-base` registers `fingerprint` on every module. It is a `@CacheableTask` whose only input is
`sourceSets.main.runtimeClasspath` declared as `@Classpath`, and whose output is
`build/fingerprint/runtime-classpath.txt`. Nothing reads that file. The point is the task's build-cache
key: a content hash of the whole runtime classpath, path- and timestamp-independent. The key also folds
in `FingerprintTask`'s own implementation hash, so any commit that touches `build-logic` (or bumps the
Gradle wrapper) changes every module's `fingerprint` key — the tracer reports such commits as
build-logic culprits rather than runtime-input changes.

| edit | `compileJava` key of a consumer | `fingerprint` key of a consumer |
|---|---|---|
| method body in a dependency | unchanged (ABI normalisation) | changed |
| public method added in a dependency | changed | changed |
| comment-only edit, same line numbers | unchanged | unchanged (identical bytecode) |
| third-party version bump (implementation dep of a dependency) | unchanged | changed |

Read a key: `./gradlew :app:appA:fingerprint -Dorg.gradle.caching.debug=true --console=plain`.
```

- [ ] **Step 8: Commit**

```bash
git add build-logic docs/gradle-build.md
git commit -m "feat(build-logic): add cacheable fingerprint task for runtime inputs

compileJava's cache key normalises the classpath to its ABI, which is the
right answer for 'would this recompile' but hides library bumps and body
edits. A cacheable task with the runtime classpath as a @Classpath input
gives a second key that tracks the bytes an app ships. TestKit tests pin
the properties the tracer relies on: path independence, change on body
edits, no change on comment-only edits."
```

`tools/tracer/src/test/java/io/joeyang/tracer/cli/FakeProcessRunner.java`:
```java
package io.joeyang.tracer.cli;

import java.nio.file.Path;
import java.time.Duration;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.LinkedHashMap;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;

/** Scripted ProcessRunner: match commands by whole-token prefix, in registration order. */
final class FakeProcessRunner implements ProcessRunner {
    private final Map<String, Object> scripted = new LinkedHashMap<>(); // prefix → Result | ProcessException
    final List<List<String>> calls = new ArrayList<>();
    final List<Path> cwds = new ArrayList<>();
    final List<Duration> timeouts = new ArrayList<>();

    FakeProcessRunner on(String commandPrefix, int exit, String stdout, String stderr) {
        scripted.put(commandPrefix, new Result(exit, stdout, stderr));
        return this;
    }

    FakeProcessRunner failWith(String commandPrefix, ProcessException e) {
        scripted.put(commandPrefix, e);
        return this;
    }

    @Override
    public Result run(Path cwd, Duration timeout, List<String> command) throws ProcessException {
        List<String> commandCopy = List.copyOf(command);
        calls.add(commandCopy);
        cwds.add(cwd);
        timeouts.add(timeout);
        for (Map.Entry<String, Object> e : scripted.entrySet()) {
            if (matchesPrefix(commandCopy, e.getKey())) {
                if (e.getValue() instanceof ProcessException pe) {
                    throw pe;
                }
                return (Result) e.getValue();
            }
        }
        throw new AssertionError("unexpected command: " + String.join(" ", commandCopy));
    }

    boolean ran(String commandPrefix) {
        return calls.stream().anyMatch(c -> matchesPrefix(c, commandPrefix));
    }

    /** True when {@code command} starts with every token of {@code commandPrefix}, split on whitespace. */
    private static boolean matchesPrefix(List<String> command, String commandPrefix) {
        List<String> prefix = List.of(commandPrefix.trim().split("\\s+"));
        return command.size() >= prefix.size() && command.subList(0, prefix.size()).equals(prefix);
    }
}
```

`tools/tracer/src/test/java/io/joeyang/tracer/cli/FakeProcessRunnerTest.java`:
```java
package io.joeyang.tracer.cli;

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertEquals;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertThrows;

import java.nio.file.Path;
import java.time.Duration;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;

class FakeProcessRunnerTest {
    @Test void recordedCallIsAnImmutableCopyOfTheCommand() throws Exception {
        FakeProcessRunner runner = new FakeProcessRunner().on("echo hi", 0, "", "");
        List<String> command = new ArrayList<>(List.of("echo", "hi"));

        runner.run(Path.of("."), Duration.ofSeconds(1), command);
        command.add("mutated");

        assertEquals(List.of("echo", "hi"), runner.calls.get(0));
        assertThrows(UnsupportedOperationException.class, () -> runner.calls.get(0).add("x"));
    }

    @Test void recordsTheTimeoutOfEachCall() throws Exception {
        FakeProcessRunner runner = new FakeProcessRunner().on("echo hi", 0, "", "");

        runner.run(Path.of("."), Duration.ofMinutes(3), List.of("echo", "hi"));

        assertEquals(List.of(Duration.ofMinutes(3)), runner.timeouts);
    }

    @Test void wholeTokenMatchPreventsACharacterLevelPrefixFromShadowing() throws Exception {
        FakeProcessRunner runner = new FakeProcessRunner()
                .on("git log", 0, "wrong match", "")
                .on("git logout", 0, "correct match", "");

        ProcessRunner.Result result = runner.run(Path.of("."), Duration.ofSeconds(1), List.of("git", "logout"));

        assertEquals("correct match", result.stdout());
    }
}
```

`tools/tracer/src/test/java/io/joeyang/tracer/cli/CommitTest.java`:
```java
package io.joeyang.tracer.cli;

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertEquals;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertThrows;

import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;

class CommitTest {
    static final String SHA = "0123456789abcdef0123456789abcdef01234567";

    @Test void shortShaIsSevenChars() { assertEquals("0123456", new Commit(SHA, "x").shortSha()); }

    @Test void rejectsNonHexSha() {
        var e = assertThrows(IllegalArgumentException.class, () -> new Commit("nope", "x"));
        assertEquals("expected a 40-char hex sha, got 'nope'", e.getMessage());
    }

    @Test void rejectsShortSha() {
        var e = assertThrows(IllegalArgumentException.class, () -> new Commit("0123456", "x"));
        assertEquals("expected a 40-char hex sha, got '0123456'", e.getMessage());
    }

    @Test void rejectsNullSha() {
        var e = assertThrows(IllegalArgumentException.class, () -> new Commit(null, "x"));
        assertEquals("expected a 40-char hex sha, got 'null'", e.getMessage());
    }

    @Test void rejectsUppercaseSha() {
        String upper = SHA.toUpperCase(java.util.Locale.ROOT);
        var e = assertThrows(IllegalArgumentException.class, () -> new Commit(upper, "x"));
        assertEquals("expected a 40-char hex sha, got '" + upper + "'", e.getMessage());
    }

    @Test void nullSubjectBecomesEmpty() { assertEquals("", new Commit(SHA, null).subject()); }
}
```

`tools/tracer/src/test/java/io/joeyang/tracer/cli/VerdictTest.java`:
```java
package io.joeyang.tracer.cli;

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertEquals;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertThrows;

import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Optional;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;

class VerdictTest {
    static final Commit COMMIT = new Commit("0123456789abcdef0123456789abcdef01234567", "x");

    @Test void bazelDisagreesOnlyForTheTwoDisagreeingPairs() {
        for (Status status : Status.values()) {
            for (BazelStatus bazel : BazelStatus.values()) {
                Verdict v = new Verdict(COMMIT, status, Optional.empty(), bazel, List.of(), Optional.empty());
                boolean expected = (status == Status.CHANGED && bazel == BazelStatus.CLEAN)
                        || (status == Status.CLEAN && bazel == BazelStatus.IMPACTED);
                assertEquals(expected, v.bazelDisagrees(), status + " vs " + bazel);
            }
        }
    }

    @Test void culpritsListIsACopy() {
        List<String> source = new ArrayList<>(List.of("core.jar"));
        Verdict v = new Verdict(COMMIT, Status.CHANGED, Optional.empty(), BazelStatus.NONE, source, Optional.empty());

        source.add("lib.jar");

        assertEquals(List.of("core.jar"), v.culprits());
        assertThrows(UnsupportedOperationException.class, () -> v.culprits().add("x"));
    }
}
```

```java
package io.joeyang.tracer.cli;

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertEquals;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertThrows;

import java.util.HashMap;
import java.util.Map;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;

class TaskKeyTest {
    @Test void shortKeyShowsFourAndTwo() {
        TaskKey k = new TaskKey(":app:appA:compileJava", "3f9c0a1b2c3d4e5f6a7b8c9d0e1f2a3b", Map.of(), Map.of(), "impl");
        assertEquals("3f9c…3b", k.shortKey());
    }

    @Test void shortKeyOfTinyKeyIsTheKey() {
        TaskKey k = new TaskKey(":t", "abc", Map.of(), Map.of(), "impl");
        assertEquals("abc", k.shortKey());
    }

    @Test void shortKeyBoundaryAtSevenCharsIsUnabbreviated() {
        TaskKey k = new TaskKey(":t", "1234567", Map.of(), Map.of(), "impl");
        assertEquals("1234567", k.shortKey());
    }

    @Test void shortKeyBoundaryAtEightCharsIsAbbreviated() {
        TaskKey k = new TaskKey(":t", "12345678", Map.of(), Map.of(), "impl");
        assertEquals("1234…78", k.shortKey());
    }

    @Test void mapsAreDefensivelyCopied() {
        Map<String, Map<String, String>> files = new HashMap<>();
        files.put("classpath", new HashMap<>(Map.of("/x/core.jar", "aa")));
        TaskKey k = new TaskKey(":t", "k", files, new HashMap<>(), "impl");
        files.get("classpath").put("/x/other.jar", "bb");
        assertEquals(1, k.fileFingerprints().get("classpath").size());
        assertThrows(UnsupportedOperationException.class, () -> k.fileFingerprints().put("x", Map.of()));
    }

    @Test void rejectsBlankKey() {
        var e = assertThrows(IllegalArgumentException.class, () -> new TaskKey(":t", " ", Map.of(), Map.of(), "impl"));
        assertEquals("cache key must not be blank for :t", e.getMessage());
    }

    @Test void rejectsEmptyKey() {
        var e = assertThrows(IllegalArgumentException.class, () -> new TaskKey(":t", "", Map.of(), Map.of(), "impl"));
        assertEquals("cache key must not be blank for :t", e.getMessage());
    }

    @Test void rejectsNullTaskPath() {
        var e = assertThrows(NullPointerException.class, () -> new TaskKey(null, "k", Map.of(), Map.of(), "impl"));
        assertEquals("taskPath must not be null", e.getMessage());
    }

    @Test void rejectsNullFileFingerprints() {
        var e = assertThrows(NullPointerException.class, () -> new TaskKey(":t", "k", null, Map.of(), "impl"));
        assertEquals("fileFingerprints must not be null", e.getMessage());
    }

    @Test void rejectsNullValueFingerprints() {
        var e = assertThrows(NullPointerException.class, () -> new TaskKey(":t", "k", Map.of(), null, "impl"));
        assertEquals("valueFingerprints must not be null", e.getMessage());
    }
}
```

```java
package io.joeyang.tracer.cli;

    }

    @Test void firstErrorLineFallsBackToExitCode() {
        assertEquals("exit code 7", new ProcessRunner.Result(7, "", "").firstErrorLine());
    }
```

`tools/tracer/src/main/java/io/joeyang/tracer/cli/ProcessRunner.java`:
```java
package io.joeyang.tracer.cli;

import java.nio.file.Path;
import java.time.Duration;
import java.util.List;

/** Runs an external command. The single seam that lets tests inject failures without real processes. */
public interface ProcessRunner {

    /** Outcome of a process that started and finished. A non-zero exit is a Result, not an exception. */
    record Result(int exitCode, String stdout, String stderr) {
        public Result {
            stdout = stdout == null ? "" : stdout;
            stderr = stderr == null ? "" : stderr;
        }

        public boolean ok() {
            return exitCode == 0;
        }

        /** Best single line to show a human: first non-blank stderr line, else a stdout line mentioning failure, else the exit code. */
        public String firstErrorLine() {
            return stderr.lines().map(String::strip).filter(l -> !l.isEmpty()).findFirst()
                    .or(() -> stdout.lines().map(String::strip)
                            .filter(l -> l.contains("FAILURE") || l.contains("error") || l.contains("ERROR"))
                            .findFirst())
                    .orElse("exit code " + exitCode);
        }
    }

    /**
     * @throws ProcessException when the process could not be started, timed out, or the thread was interrupted
     */
    Result run(Path cwd, Duration timeout, List<String> command) throws ProcessException;
}
```

`tools/tracer/src/main/java/io/joeyang/tracer/cli/ProcessException.java` (as amended by the Task 11 quality-review fixes — see that task's region for why):
```java
package io.joeyang.tracer.cli;

import java.io.IOException;
import java.time.Duration;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Locale;

/** The process could not run to completion (as opposed to running and failing). */
public final class ProcessException extends Exception {
    private final boolean startFailed;

    private ProcessException(String message, Throwable cause, boolean startFailed) {
        super(message, cause);
        this.startFailed = startFailed;
    }

    public static ProcessException cannotStart(List<String> command, Throwable cause) {
        return new ProcessException("cannot start '" + command.get(0) + "': " + cause.getMessage(), cause, true);
    }

    public static ProcessException timeout(List<String> command, Duration timeout) {
        return new ProcessException("'" + String.join(" ", command) + "' did not finish within " + format(timeout), null, false);
    }

    public static ProcessException interrupted(List<String> command) {
        return new ProcessException("interrupted while waiting for '" + command.get(0) + "'", null, false);
    }

    /** The process ran, but reading its stdout or stderr failed (as opposed to a start failure or a timeout). */
    public static ProcessException ioFailed(List<String> command, IOException cause) {
        return new ProcessException("reading output of '" + command.get(0) + "' failed: " + cause.getMessage(), cause, false);
    }

    /** True when the process never launched (e.g. the binary is missing) — false when it ran but timed out or was interrupted. */
    public boolean startFailed() {
        return startFailed;
    }

    /** Sub-second durations in ms (e.g. "300ms"); otherwise seconds, with one decimal place only when needed. */
    private static String format(Duration d) {
        long millis = d.toMillis();
        if (millis < 1000) {
            return millis + "ms";
        }
        if (millis % 1000 == 0) {
            return (millis / 1000) + "s";
        }
        return String.format(Locale.ROOT, "%.1fs", millis / 1000.0);
    }
}
```
(`startFailed()` was added by a Task 8 re-review round so `BazelDiffProvider` could tell a launch failure from a
timeout/interruption — see that task's region for why. The `ioFailed` factory and the `format` helper were added by
a Task 11 quality-review fix: a failed stdout/stderr read was previously swallowed into an empty string, which made
a broken drain look like a silent success, and the previous `timeout.toSeconds() + "s"` formatting printed
"within 0s" for sub-second timeouts.)

`tools/tracer/src/main/java/io/joeyang/tracer/cli/Commit.java`:
```java
package io.joeyang.tracer.cli;

import java.util.regex.Pattern;

/** One git commit: full sha and first subject line. */
public record Commit(String sha, String subject) {
    private static final Pattern FULL_SHA = Pattern.compile("[0-9a-f]{40}");

    public Commit {
        if (sha == null || !FULL_SHA.matcher(sha).matches()) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("expected a 40-char hex sha, got '" + sha + "'");
        }
        subject = subject == null ? "" : subject.strip();
    }

    public String shortSha() {
        return sha.substring(0, 7);
    }
}
```

`tools/tracer/src/main/java/io/joeyang/tracer/cli/TaskKey.java`:
```java
package io.joeyang.tracer.cli;

import java.util.Collections;
import java.util.LinkedHashMap;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.Objects;

/**
 * A Gradle task's build-cache key and the fingerprints it was built from.
 *
 * @param taskPath e.g. {@code :app:appA:compileJava}
 * @param key the cache key hash as printed by Gradle
 * @param fileFingerprints input property name → (file path → content hash); e.g. {@code classpath → {/x/core.jar → ab12}}
 * @param valueFingerprints input property name → hash of a scalar input, e.g. {@code options.compilerArgs}
 * @param implementation the task implementation hash (changes when build logic changes)
 */
public record TaskKey(String taskPath, String key, Map<String, Map<String, String>> fileFingerprints,
                      Map<String, String> valueFingerprints, String implementation) {

    public TaskKey {
        Objects.requireNonNull(taskPath, "taskPath must not be null");
        Objects.requireNonNull(fileFingerprints, "fileFingerprints must not be null");
        Objects.requireNonNull(valueFingerprints, "valueFingerprints must not be null");
        if (key == null || key.isBlank()) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("cache key must not be blank for " + taskPath);
        }
        Map<String, Map<String, String>> files = new LinkedHashMap<>();
        fileFingerprints.forEach((property, byFile) -> files.put(property, Collections.unmodifiableMap(new LinkedHashMap<>(byFile))));
        fileFingerprints = Collections.unmodifiableMap(files);
        valueFingerprints = Collections.unmodifiableMap(new LinkedHashMap<>(valueFingerprints));
        implementation = implementation == null ? "" : implementation;
    }

    /** {@code 3f9c…3b} style abbreviation for tables. */
    public String shortKey() {
        return key.length() <= 7 ? key : key.substring(0, 4) + "…" + key.substring(key.length() - 2);
    }
}
```

```java
package io.joeyang.tracer.cli;

import java.util.List;
import java.util.Optional;

/**
 * One table row.
 *
 * @param key present unless the build failed
 * @param culprits human-readable names of inputs that changed (jar names, source paths, property names, "build logic")
 * @param error first error line when status is BUILD_FAILED
 */
public record Verdict(Commit commit, Status status, Optional<TaskKey> key, BazelStatus bazel,
                      List<String> culprits, Optional<String> error) {
    public Verdict {
        culprits = List.copyOf(culprits);
    }

    public boolean bazelDisagrees() {
        return (status == Status.CHANGED && bazel == BazelStatus.CLEAN)
                || (status == Status.CLEAN && bazel == BazelStatus.IMPACTED);
    }
}
```

```java
package io.joeyang.tracer.cli;

import java.nio.file.Path;
import java.util.Set;

/** The Bazel side: hash a worktree, then ask which targets differ between two hash files. */
public interface ImpactProvider {
    /** Writes the hashes of every target in {@code worktree} to {@code output} and returns it. */
    Path hashes(Path worktree, Path output) throws ProviderException;

    /** Labels impacted between two hash files, e.g. {@code //app/appA:appA}. */
    Set<String> impactedTargets(Path previousHashes, Path currentHashes) throws ProviderException;
}
```

`tools/tracer/src/main/java/io/joeyang/tracer/cli/Cli.java` (skeleton; Tasks 12 and 13 complete it):
```java
package io.joeyang.tracer.cli;

import java.io.PrintStream;

/** Entry point. Only usage handling exists today; argument parsing and the revision-range walk arrive in later commits. */
public final class Cli {
    public static final int EXIT_OK = 0;
    public static final int EXIT_USAGE = 1;
    public static final int EXIT_TOOL_FAILURE = 2;
    public static final int EXIT_CHANGED = 3;

    static final String USAGE = """
            usage: trace <app> --from <rev> --to <rev> [--task <name>] [--no-bazel] [--offline] [--keep-worktrees] [--tsv]
                   trace <app> --at <rev> [--task <name>]

              <app>       appA | appB | appC   (maps to :app:<app>:<task> and //app/<app>:<app>)
              --task      Gradle task whose cache key is compared; default compileJava, alternative fingerprint
              --no-bazel  skip the bazel-diff column
              --offline   pass --offline to gradle and bazel
              --tsv       machine-readable output (sha, subject, key, bazel, verdict, culprits)

            exit codes: 0 no change, 1 usage error, 2 a tool failed, 3 at least one CHANGED
            """;

    private Cli() {}

    static int usage(PrintStream err, String problem) {
        err.println("trace: " + problem);
        err.print(USAGE);
        return EXIT_USAGE;
    }

    public static void main(String[] args) {
        System.exit(usage(System.err, "not implemented yet"));
    }
}
```

`docs/tracer.md`:
```markdown
# tracer

`tools/tracer` is a Java CLI. Today `Cli.main` ignores its arguments and exits with the usage
text and a "not implemented yet" message; a `./trace` wrapper that builds it with `installDist` and runs the launcher,
and the revision-range walk that produces real verdicts, arrive in later commits.

## Vocabulary (`io.joeyang.tracer.cli`)
| type | meaning |
|---|---|
| `ProcessRunner` / `Result` | runs a command; non-zero exit is a `Result`, "could not run" is a `ProcessException` |
| `Commit` | sha + subject |
| `TaskKey` | a task's cache key plus per-input fingerprints (`property → file → hash`), value fingerprints and implementation hash |
| `Status` | `BASE`, `CHANGED`, `CLEAN`, `BUILD_FAILED` — the Gradle verdict |
| `BazelStatus` | `IMPACTED`, `CLEAN`, `NOT_AVAILABLE`, `NONE` — the informational column |
| `Verdict` | one table row |
| `KeyProvider`, `ImpactProvider` | the two oracles behind interfaces so tests can fake them |
| `UsageException` → exit 1, `ProviderException` → exit 2 | error classes |

## Exit codes
0 no change in range · 1 usage error · 2 a tool failed (some row is BUILD-FAILED) · 3 at least one CHANGED
```

### Task 7: CacheDebugLog parser and GradleKeyProvider

**Files:**
- Create: `tools/tracer/src/main/java/io/joeyang/tracer/cli/CacheDebugLog.java`, `tools/tracer/src/main/java/io/joeyang/tracer/cli/GradleKeyProvider.java`
- Test: `tools/tracer/src/test/java/io/joeyang/tracer/cli/{CacheDebugFixtures,CacheDebugLogTest,GradleKeyProviderTest}.java`
- Test fixtures: `tools/tracer/src/test/resources/cache-debug/appA-compileJava.log`, `tools/tracer/src/test/resources/cache-debug/appA-fingerprint.log`
- Modify: `docs/tracer.md`

The exact line formats, captured from Gradle 9.1 on this machine (paths shortened; the fixtures in Step 1 are the 9.8.1 version):
```
> Task :app:fingerprint
Appending implementation to build cache key: conv.FingerprintTask_Decorated@052c21c2d80b17503620cdc0a8513051
Appending additional implementation to build cache key: conv.FingerprintTask_Decorated@052c21c2d80b17503620cdc0a8513051
Appending input value fingerprint for 'options.release' to build cache key: 91e6b2f4b53f9e7b3eadb03039173cc5
Appending input file fingerprints for 'classpath' to build cache key: b19a517b4bb8b020377c506d479c308b - COMPILE_CLASSPATH{/w/core/build/classes/java/main/demo/core/Greeter.class='demo/core/Greeter.class' / 5042bbb7be5171b8d45ca1e0a8193342}
Appending input file fingerprints for 'runtimeClasspath' to build cache key: 2924fae15aebb9d9dc48b75545f70d84 - CLASSPATH{/w/app/build/classes/java/main/demo/app/Main.class='demo/app/Main.class' / dcbb8c54623ad2dc6ad1ff839471447f, /w/core/build/libs/core.jar=IGNORED / ba11c11731255ecb1b0620818eb3b18d}
Appending input file fingerprints for 'options.annotationProcessorPath' to build cache key: 5fd1e7396e8de4cb5c23dc6aadd7787a - CLASSPATH{EMPTY}
Appending output property name to build cache key: marker
Non-cacheable because Not worth caching [NOT_CACHEABLE]        ← printed instead of a key for e.g. jar tasks
Build cache key for task ':app:fingerprint' is b5a1bb8d226bf944a43ec45392201d50
```
Entries inside `{...}` are `<abs path>=<'normalized path'|IGNORED> / <hash>` separated by `, `. Two facts that shape the design: on the **compile** classpath Gradle feeds project dependencies as class directories (one entry per `.class`), so the culprit for a core change under `compileJava` is `core classes`; on the **runtime** classpath they are jars (`core.jar`). The debug lines print at the default log level. With `--parallel` lines from different tasks interleave, so the provider passes `--no-parallel`.

```bash
./gradlew :app:appA:compileJava --console=plain --no-parallel --build-cache --rerun-tasks -Dorg.gradle.caching.debug=true \
  | sed "s#$PWD#/repo#g; s#$HOME#/home/user#g" > tools/tracer/src/test/resources/cache-debug/appA-compileJava.log
./gradlew :app:appA:fingerprint --console=plain --no-parallel --build-cache --rerun-tasks -Dorg.gradle.caching.debug=true \
  | sed "s#$PWD#/repo#g; s#$HOME#/home/user#g" > tools/tracer/src/test/resources/cache-debug/appA-fingerprint.log
grep -c "Build cache key for task" tools/tracer/src/test/resources/cache-debug/*.log
```
