`tools/tracer/src/test/java/io/joeyang/tracer/cli/CacheDebugLogTest.java`:
```java
package io.joeyang.tracer.cli;

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertEquals;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertFalse;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertThrows;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertTrue;

import java.io.IOException;
import java.io.InputStream;
import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.Optional;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;

class CacheDebugLogTest {
    static final String TASK = ":app:appA:compileJava";
    static final String FIXTURE = CacheDebugFixtures.COMPILE_JAVA_LOG;

    @Test void parsesOnlyTheRequestedTask() {
        TaskKey key = CacheDebugLog.parse(FIXTURE, TASK).orElseThrow();

        assertEquals(TASK, key.taskPath());
        assertEquals("35c0f7cbe157cd2e30090cc5510cec8c", key.key());
        // Fixture repeats the same class in its "additional implementation" line; both are folded in.
        assertEquals("org.gradle.api.tasks.compile.JavaCompile_Decorated@bbbb, org.gradle.api.tasks.compile.JavaCompile_Decorated@bbbb",
                key.implementation());
        assertEquals(Map.of("options.release", "91e6b2f4b53f9e7b3eadb03039173cc5", "options.compilerArgs", "4444"), key.valueFingerprints());
        assertEquals(Map.of(
                "/w/core/build/classes/java/main/io/joeyang/tracer/core/Greeter.class", "5042bbb7be5171b8d45ca1e0a8193342",
                "/w/lib/build/classes/java/main/io/joeyang/tracer/lib/Service.class", "6666"), key.fileFingerprints().get("classpath"));
        assertEquals(Map.of(), key.fileFingerprints().get("options.annotationProcessorPath"));
        assertEquals(Map.of("/w/app/appA/src/main/java/io/joeyang/tracer/app/a/AppA.java", "55e49a9f6bfdc6ea941195b682520e54"),
                key.fileFingerprints().get("stableSources"));
    }

    @Test void otherTasksDoNotLeakIntoTheResult() {
        TaskKey key = CacheDebugLog.parse(FIXTURE, TASK).orElseThrow();
        assertFalse(key.fileFingerprints().get("stableSources").containsKey("/w/core/src/main/java/io/joeyang/tracer/core/Greeter.java"));
    }

    @Test void absentTaskIsEmpty() {
        assertEquals(Optional.empty(), CacheDebugLog.parse(FIXTURE, ":app:appB:compileJava"));
    }

    @Test void nonCacheableTaskIsEmpty() {
        assertEquals(Optional.empty(), CacheDebugLog.parse(FIXTURE, ":core:jar"));
    }

    @Test void truncatedLogIsEmpty() {
        String truncated = FIXTURE.substring(0, FIXTURE.indexOf("Build cache key for task ':app:appA"));
        assertEquals(Optional.empty(), CacheDebugLog.parse(truncated, TASK));
    }

    @Test void ignoredEntriesAndJarsAreParsed() {
        String log = """
                > Task :app:appA:fingerprint
                Appending implementation to build cache key: io.joeyang.tracer.buildlogic.FingerprintTask_Decorated@0001
                Appending input file fingerprints for 'runtimeClasspath' to build cache key: 2924 - CLASSPATH{/w/app/appA/build/classes/java/main/io/joeyang/tracer/app/a/AppA.class='io/joeyang/tracer/app/a/AppA.class' / dcbb, /w/core/build/libs/core.jar=IGNORED / ba11, /home/user/.gradle/caches/modules-2/files-2.1/com.google.code.gson/gson/2.10.1/abc/gson-2.10.1.jar=IGNORED / d632}
                Appending output property name to build cache key: marker
                Build cache key for task ':app:appA:fingerprint' is b5a1bb8d226bf944a43ec45392201d50
                """;
        TaskKey key = CacheDebugLog.parse(log, ":app:appA:fingerprint").orElseThrow();
        Map<String, String> files = key.fileFingerprints().get("runtimeClasspath");
        assertEquals(3, files.size());
        assertEquals("ba11", files.get("/w/core/build/libs/core.jar"));
        assertEquals("d632", files.get("/home/user/.gradle/caches/modules-2/files-2.1/com.google.code.gson/gson/2.10.1/abc/gson-2.10.1.jar"));
        assertEquals(Map.of(), key.valueFingerprints());
    }

    @Test void realCompileJavaFixtureParses() throws IOException {
        TaskKey key = CacheDebugLog.parse(resource("/cache-debug/appA-compileJava.log"), TASK).orElseThrow();
        assertEquals("17035b126dce737b54e2162d950d98e0", key.key());
        assertTrue(key.valueFingerprints().containsKey("options.release"), key.valueFingerprints().keySet().toString());
        assertEquals(2, key.fileFingerprints().get("classpath").size(), "appA's compile classpath has lib and core classes");
        assertTrue(key.fileFingerprints().get("stableSources").keySet().stream().anyMatch(p -> p.endsWith("AppA.java")));
        assertTrue(key.implementation().contains("JavaCompile"), key.implementation());
    }

    @Test void realFingerprintFixtureParses() throws IOException {
        // Read from the fixture itself rather than hand-copied, so a re-capture that changes the key fails loudly.
        String fixture = resource("/cache-debug/appA-fingerprint.log");
        String expectedKey = fixture.lines()
                .filter(l -> l.startsWith("Build cache key for task ':app:appA:fingerprint'"))
                .findFirst().orElseThrow()
                .replaceFirst(".* is ", "");
        TaskKey key = CacheDebugLog.parse(fixture, ":app:appA:fingerprint").orElseThrow();
        assertEquals(expectedKey, key.key());
        assertEquals(5, key.fileFingerprints().get("runtimeClasspath").size());
        assertTrue(key.fileFingerprints().get("runtimeClasspath").keySet().stream().anyMatch(p -> p.endsWith("/core.jar")));
        assertTrue(key.fileFingerprints().get("runtimeClasspath").keySet().stream().anyMatch(p -> p.contains("commons-lang3-3.18.0.jar")));
        assertTrue(key.implementation().contains("FingerprintTask"), key.implementation());
    }

    @Test void keyOnlyBlockParsesWithEmptyInputs() {
        // An UP-TO-DATE task prints the key and no input lines; fresh worktrees never hit this, but the parser must cope.
        String log = "> Task :app:appA:compileJava UP-TO-DATE\nBuild cache key for task ':app:appA:compileJava' is 35c0f7cbe157cd2e30090cc5510cec8c\n";
        TaskKey key = CacheDebugLog.parse(log, TASK).orElseThrow();
        assertEquals("35c0f7cbe157cd2e30090cc5510cec8c", key.key());
        assertTrue(key.fileFingerprints().isEmpty() && key.valueFingerprints().isEmpty());
    }

    @Test void nonCacheableReasonIsReportedForTheRightTaskOnly() {
        assertEquals(Optional.of("Not worth caching [NOT_CACHEABLE]"), CacheDebugLog.nonCacheableReason(FIXTURE, ":core:jar"));
        assertEquals(Optional.empty(), CacheDebugLog.nonCacheableReason(FIXTURE, TASK));
    }

    @Test void dirAndMissingEntriesAreNotGarbageKeys() {
        String log = """
                > Task :app:appA:fingerprint
                Appending implementation to build cache key: io.joeyang.tracer.buildlogic.FingerprintTask_Decorated@0001
                Appending input file fingerprints for 'rootSpec$1' to build cache key: aaaa - RELATIVE_PATH{/w/build/classes/java/main/io='io' / DIR, /w/build/deleted.class='deleted.class' / MISSING, /w/build/classes/java/main/io/Real.class='io/Real.class' / 3333}
                Build cache key for task ':app:appA:fingerprint' is b5a1bb8d226bf944a43ec45392201d50
                """;
        TaskKey key = CacheDebugLog.parse(log, ":app:appA:fingerprint").orElseThrow();
        Map<String, String> entries = key.fileFingerprints().get("rootSpec$1");
        assertEquals(3, entries.size());
        assertEquals("DIR", entries.get("/w/build/classes/java/main/io"));
        assertEquals("MISSING", entries.get("/w/build/deleted.class"));
        assertEquals("3333", entries.get("/w/build/classes/java/main/io/Real.class"));
    }

    @Test void aQuoteInsideANormalizedPathDoesNotMergeTwoEntries() {
        String log = """
                > Task :app:appA:fingerprint
                Appending implementation to build cache key: io.joeyang.tracer.buildlogic.FingerprintTask_Decorated@0001
                Appending input file fingerprints for 'stableSources' to build cache key: aaaa - RELATIVE_PATH{/w/src/io/it's/A.java='io/it's/A.java' / 1111, /w/src/io/B.java='io/B.java' / 2222}
                Build cache key for task ':app:appA:fingerprint' is b5a1bb8d226bf944a43ec45392201d50
                """;
        TaskKey key = CacheDebugLog.parse(log, ":app:appA:fingerprint").orElseThrow();
        Map<String, String> entries = key.fileFingerprints().get("stableSources");
        assertEquals(2, entries.size());
        assertEquals("1111", entries.get("/w/src/io/it's/A.java"));
        assertEquals("2222", entries.get("/w/src/io/B.java"));
    }

    @Test void unknownNormalizationTokenFailsLoudlyInsteadOfDroppingTheProperty() {
        String log = """
                > Task :app:appA:fingerprint
                Appending implementation to build cache key: io.joeyang.tracer.buildlogic.FingerprintTask_Decorated@0001
                Appending input file fingerprints for 'stableSources' to build cache key: aaaa - relativePath{/w/src/A.java='A.java' / 1111}
                Build cache key for task ':app:appA:fingerprint' is b5a1bb8d226bf944a43ec45392201d50
                """;
        IllegalArgumentException e = assertThrows(IllegalArgumentException.class, () -> CacheDebugLog.parse(log, ":app:appA:fingerprint"));
        assertTrue(e.getMessage().contains("unrecognised cache debug output"), e.getMessage());
        assertTrue(e.getMessage().contains("relativePath{/w/src/A.java='A.java' / 1111}"), e.getMessage());
    }

    @Test void unknownValueFingerprintShapeFailsLoudly() {
        String log = """
                > Task :app:appA:fingerprint
                Appending implementation to build cache key: io.joeyang.tracer.buildlogic.FingerprintTask_Decorated@0001
                Appending input value fingerprint for 'options.release' to build cache key: not-a-hex-hash
                Build cache key for task ':app:appA:fingerprint' is b5a1bb8d226bf944a43ec45392201d50
                """;
        IllegalArgumentException e = assertThrows(IllegalArgumentException.class, () -> CacheDebugLog.parse(log, ":app:appA:fingerprint"));
        assertTrue(e.getMessage().contains("unrecognised cache debug output"), e.getMessage());
        assertTrue(e.getMessage().contains("options.release"), e.getMessage());
    }

    @Test void pathsContainingAnEqualsSignAreNotCutAtTheWrongOne() {
        String log = """
                > Task :app:appA:fingerprint
                Appending implementation to build cache key: io.joeyang.tracer.buildlogic.FingerprintTask_Decorated@0001
                Appending input file fingerprints for 'stableSources' to build cache key: aaaa - RELATIVE_PATH{/w/src/io/a=b.java='a=b.java' / 1111, /w/src/io/c=d.java=IGNORED / 2222}
                Build cache key for task ':app:appA:fingerprint' is b5a1bb8d226bf944a43ec45392201d50
                """;
        TaskKey key = CacheDebugLog.parse(log, ":app:appA:fingerprint").orElseThrow();
        Map<String, String> entries = key.fileFingerprints().get("stableSources");
        assertEquals(2, entries.size());
        assertEquals("1111", entries.get("/w/src/io/a=b.java"));
        assertEquals("2222", entries.get("/w/src/io/c=d.java"));
    }

    @Test void additionalImplementationLinesAreFoldedIntoTheImplementationFingerprint() {
        String log = """
                > Task :app:appA:fingerprint
                Appending implementation to build cache key: io.joeyang.tracer.buildlogic.FingerprintTask_Decorated@0001
                Appending additional implementation to build cache key: org.gradle.api.internal.AbstractTask$TaskActionWrapper@0002
                Build cache key for task ':app:appA:fingerprint' is b5a1bb8d226bf944a43ec45392201d50
                """;
        TaskKey key = CacheDebugLog.parse(log, ":app:appA:fingerprint").orElseThrow();
        assertTrue(key.implementation().contains("FingerprintTask_Decorated@0001"), key.implementation());
        assertTrue(key.implementation().contains("TaskActionWrapper@0002"), key.implementation());
    }

    @Test void realFixtureRootSpecHasAllThirteenEntries() throws IOException {
        // rootSpec$1 only ever appears on a Jar task's input, and Jar is non-cacheable, so this property
        // never comes back through parse(); fileFingerprintsFor exercises the same entry parser directly.
        Map<String, Map<String, String>> files = CacheDebugLog.fileFingerprintsFor(resource("/cache-debug/appA-compileJava.log"), ":build-logic:jar");
        assertEquals(13, files.get("rootSpec$1").size());
    }

    private static String resource(String name) throws IOException {
        try (InputStream in = CacheDebugLogTest.class.getResourceAsStream(name)) {
            if (in == null) {
                throw new IOException("missing test resource " + name);
            }
            return new String(in.readAllBytes(), StandardCharsets.UTF_8);
        }
    }
}
```

```java
package io.joeyang.tracer.cli;

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertEquals;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertThrows;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertTrue;

import java.io.IOException;
import java.nio.file.Files;
import java.nio.file.Path;
import java.time.Duration;
import java.util.List;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.BeforeEach;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.io.TempDir;

class GradleKeyProviderTest {
    static final String TASK = ":app:appA:compileJava";
    @TempDir Path worktree;
    String gradlew;

    @BeforeEach
    void setUp() throws IOException {
        Path script = worktree.resolve("gradlew");
        Files.writeString(script, "#!/bin/sh\n");
        script.toFile().setExecutable(true);
        gradlew = script.toString();
    }

    @Test void runsGradlewWithDebugFlagsInTheWorktree() throws Exception {
        FakeProcessRunner runner = new FakeProcessRunner().on(gradlew, 0, CacheDebugFixtures.COMPILE_JAVA_LOG, "");
        Duration timeout = Duration.ofMinutes(1);

        TaskKey key = new GradleKeyProvider(runner, false, timeout).keyFor(worktree, TASK);

        assertEquals("35c0f7cbe157cd2e30090cc5510cec8c", key.key());
        assertEquals(List.of(gradlew, "--console=plain", "--no-parallel", "--build-cache", "-Dorg.gradle.caching.debug=true", TASK), runner.calls.get(0));
        assertEquals(worktree, runner.cwds.get(0));
        assertEquals(timeout, runner.timeouts.get(0));
    }

    @Test void offlineAppendsTheFlag() throws Exception {
        FakeProcessRunner runner = new FakeProcessRunner().on(gradlew, 0, CacheDebugFixtures.COMPILE_JAVA_LOG, "");
        Duration timeout = Duration.ofSeconds(90);

        new GradleKeyProvider(runner, true, timeout).keyFor(worktree, TASK);

        assertEquals(List.of(gradlew, "--console=plain", "--no-parallel", "--build-cache", "-Dorg.gradle.caching.debug=true", TASK, "--offline"),
                runner.calls.get(0));
        assertEquals(timeout, runner.timeouts.get(0));
    }

    @Test void missingGradlewIsAProviderError() throws IOException {
        Files.delete(worktree.resolve("gradlew"));
        FakeProcessRunner runner = new FakeProcessRunner();
        ProviderException e = assertThrows(ProviderException.class,
                () -> new GradleKeyProvider(runner, false, Duration.ofMinutes(1)).keyFor(worktree, TASK));
        assertTrue(e.getMessage().contains("gradlew"), e.getMessage());
        assertTrue(runner.calls.isEmpty());
    }

    @Test void buildFailureCarriesTheFirstErrorLine() {
        FakeProcessRunner runner = new FakeProcessRunner().on(gradlew, 1,
                "> Task :core:compileJava FAILED\n", "Greeter.java:12: error: ';' expected\n");
        ProviderException e = assertThrows(ProviderException.class,
                () -> new GradleKeyProvider(runner, false, Duration.ofMinutes(1)).keyFor(worktree, TASK));
        assertTrue(e.getMessage().contains("';' expected"), e.getMessage());
    }

    @Test void unknownTaskJoinsWhatWentWrongWithItsContinuationLine() {
        // Captured verbatim from `./gradlew :app:appA:nope --console=plain` against Gradle 9.8.1: the cause
        // is not on the "* What went wrong:" line itself but on the indented line right after it.
        String stderr = """

                FAILURE: Build failed with an exception.

                * What went wrong:
                Selection failed
                  Cannot locate tasks that match ':app:appA:nope' as task 'nope' not found in project ':app:appA'.

                * Try:
                > Run gradlew tasks to get a list of available tasks.
                > For more on name expansion, please refer to https://docs.gradle.org/9.8.1/userguide/command_line_interface.html#sec:name_abbreviation in the Gradle documentation.
                > Run with --stacktrace option to get the stack trace.
                > Run with --info or --debug option to get more log output.
                > Run with --scan to get full insights from a Build Scan (powered by Develocity).
                > Get more help at https://help.gradle.org.

                BUILD FAILED in 488ms
                """;
        FakeProcessRunner runner = new FakeProcessRunner().on(gradlew, 1, "", stderr);
        ProviderException e = assertThrows(ProviderException.class,
                () -> new GradleKeyProvider(runner, false, Duration.ofMinutes(1)).keyFor(worktree, TASK));
        assertTrue(e.getMessage().contains("Selection failed"), e.getMessage());
        assertTrue(e.getMessage().contains("Cannot locate tasks that match ':app:appA:nope'"), e.getMessage());
        assertTrue(e.getMessage().contains(TASK), e.getMessage());
    }

    @Test void compileErrorSurfacesTheJavacErrorOverTheGenericWhatWentWrongLine() {
        // Captured verbatim from a real `:app:appA:compileJava` failure against Gradle 9.8.1: "* What went
        // wrong:" only says "Compilation failed; see the compiler output below." — the actual javac error
        // is further down (and also printed, unindented, before the FAILURE banner).
        String stderr = """
                /repo/app/appA/src/main/java/io/joeyang/tracer/app/a/AppA.java:11: error: ';' expected
                    static int broken() { return 1 }
                                                  ^
                1 error

                FAILURE: Build failed with an exception.

                * What went wrong:
                Execution failed for task ':app:appA:compileJava' (registered by plugin class 'org.gradle.api.plugins.JavaBasePlugin').
                > Compilation failed; see the compiler output below.
                  /repo/app/appA/src/main/java/io/joeyang/tracer/app/a/AppA.java:11: error: ';' expected
                      static int broken() { return 1 }
                                                    ^
                  1 error

                * Try:
                > Check your code and dependencies to fix the compilation error(s)
                > Run with --scan to get full insights from a Build Scan (powered by Develocity).

                BUILD FAILED in 452ms
                """;
        FakeProcessRunner runner = new FakeProcessRunner().on(gradlew, 1, "", stderr);
        ProviderException e = assertThrows(ProviderException.class,
                () -> new GradleKeyProvider(runner, false, Duration.ofMinutes(1)).keyFor(worktree, TASK));
        assertTrue(e.getMessage().contains("error: ';' expected"), e.getMessage());
        assertTrue(e.getMessage().contains(TASK), e.getMessage());
    }

    @Test void nullConstructorArgumentsAreRejectedImmediately() {
        NullPointerException runnerNpe = assertThrows(NullPointerException.class,
                () -> new GradleKeyProvider(null, false, Duration.ofMinutes(1)));
        assertTrue(runnerNpe.getMessage().contains("runner"), runnerNpe.getMessage());

        NullPointerException timeoutNpe = assertThrows(NullPointerException.class,
                () -> new GradleKeyProvider(new FakeProcessRunner(), false, null));
        assertTrue(timeoutNpe.getMessage().contains("timeout"), timeoutNpe.getMessage());
    }

    @Test void timeoutIsAProviderError() {
        FakeProcessRunner runner = new FakeProcessRunner()
                .failWith(gradlew, ProcessException.timeout(List.of(gradlew), Duration.ofMinutes(1)));
        ProviderException e = assertThrows(ProviderException.class,
                () -> new GradleKeyProvider(runner, false, Duration.ofMinutes(1)).keyFor(worktree, TASK));
        assertTrue(e.getMessage().contains("did not finish"), e.getMessage());
    }

    @Test void successWithoutAKeyLineIsAProviderErrorNotASilentClean() {
        FakeProcessRunner runner = new FakeProcessRunner().on(gradlew, 0, "> Task :app:appA:compileJava UP-TO-DATE\nBUILD SUCCESSFUL\n", "");
        ProviderException e = assertThrows(ProviderException.class,
                () -> new GradleKeyProvider(runner, false, Duration.ofMinutes(1)).keyFor(worktree, TASK));
        assertTrue(e.getMessage().contains("no cache key"), e.getMessage());
        assertTrue(e.getMessage().contains("unrecognised cache debug output"), e.getMessage());
    }

    @Test void unrecognisedCacheDebugOutputIsAProviderErrorNotAnUncaughtException() {
        String log = """
                > Task :app:appA:compileJava
                Appending implementation to build cache key: org.gradle.api.tasks.compile.JavaCompile_Decorated@bbbb
                Appending input file fingerprints for 'stableSources' to build cache key: aaaa - relativePath{/w/A.java='A.java' / 1111}
                Build cache key for task ':app:appA:compileJava' is 35c0f7cbe157cd2e30090cc5510cec8c
                """;
        FakeProcessRunner runner = new FakeProcessRunner().on(gradlew, 0, log, "");
        ProviderException e = assertThrows(ProviderException.class,
                () -> new GradleKeyProvider(runner, false, Duration.ofMinutes(1)).keyFor(worktree, TASK));
        assertTrue(e.getMessage().contains("unrecognised cache debug output"), e.getMessage());
        assertTrue(e.getMessage().contains("relativePath{/w/A.java='A.java' / 1111}"), e.getMessage());
        assertTrue(e.getMessage().contains(TASK), e.getMessage());
    }

    @Test void nonCacheableTaskIsExplainedNotMisreported() {
        FakeProcessRunner runner = new FakeProcessRunner().on(gradlew, 0, CacheDebugFixtures.COMPILE_JAVA_LOG, "");
        ProviderException e = assertThrows(ProviderException.class,
                () -> new GradleKeyProvider(runner, false, Duration.ofMinutes(1)).keyFor(worktree, ":core:jar"));
        assertTrue(e.getMessage().contains("not cacheable"), e.getMessage());
        assertTrue(e.getMessage().contains("Not worth caching"), e.getMessage());
    }
}
```

`tools/tracer/src/main/java/io/joeyang/tracer/cli/CacheDebugLog.java`:
```java
package io.joeyang.tracer.cli;

import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.LinkedHashMap;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.Optional;
import java.util.regex.Matcher;
import java.util.regex.Pattern;

/**
 * Parses the lines Gradle prints with {@code -Dorg.gradle.caching.debug=true}. Lines belong to the task
 * announced by the preceding {@code > Task <path>} header (hence {@code --no-parallel}); a
 * {@code Build cache key for task '<path>' is <hash>} line, or a {@code Non-cacheable because ...} line,
 * closes the block. Any {@code Appending ...} line inside the target task's block that does not match one
 * of the shapes below is treated as unrecognised rather than silently dropped or mis-split (spec §9): a
 * Gradle upgrade that changes the format must fail loudly instead of producing a wrong, "clean" verdict.
 */
final class CacheDebugLog {
    private static final Pattern TASK_HEADER = Pattern.compile("^> Task (\\S+)");
    private static final Pattern IMPLEMENTATION = Pattern.compile("^Appending implementation to build cache key: (.+)$");
    private static final Pattern ADDITIONAL_IMPLEMENTATION =
            Pattern.compile("^Appending additional implementation to build cache key: (.+)$");
    private static final Pattern VALUE = Pattern.compile("^Appending input value fingerprint for '([^']+)' to build cache key: ([0-9a-f]+)$");
    private static final Pattern FILES = Pattern.compile("^Appending input file fingerprints for '([^']+)' to build cache key: [0-9a-f]+ - [A-Z_]+\\{(.*)\\}$");
    private static final Pattern OUTPUT_PROPERTY = Pattern.compile("^Appending output property name to build cache key: .+$");
    /**
     * A single already-isolated {@code <path>=<'normalized'|IGNORED> / <hash|DIR|MISSING>} entry, with the
     * path captured greedily so a path containing its own {@code '='} does not get cut at the wrong one:
     * the engine backtracks to the rightmost {@code '='} for which the rest of the pattern still matches
     * through end-of-string, which is always the real delimiter.
     */
    private static final Pattern ENTRY = Pattern.compile("^(/.+)=(?:'.*'|IGNORED) / (?:([0-9a-f]+)|DIR|MISSING)$");
    private static final Pattern KEY = Pattern.compile("^Build cache key for task '([^']+)' is ([0-9a-f]+)$");
    private static final Pattern NON_CACHEABLE = Pattern.compile("^Non-cacheable because (.+)$");

    private CacheDebugLog() {}

    static Optional<TaskKey> parse(String output, String taskPath) {
        Block block = blockFor(output, taskPath);
        if (block.key() == null) {
            return Optional.empty();
        }
        return Optional.of(new TaskKey(taskPath, block.key(), block.files(), block.values(), block.implementation()));
    }

    /** The reason Gradle printed instead of a key for {@code taskPath} (e.g. "Not worth caching [NOT_CACHEABLE]" for jar tasks). */
    static Optional<String> nonCacheableReason(String output, String taskPath) {
        return Optional.ofNullable(blockFor(output, taskPath).nonCacheableReason());
    }

    /**
     * Per-file fingerprints for {@code taskPath}, including a task that never prints a key (e.g. a jar
     * task). Exposed so tests can exercise entry parsing directly against properties, such as a {@code Jar}
     * task's {@code rootSpec$1}, that only ever appear on non-cacheable tasks.
     */
    static Map<String, Map<String, String>> fileFingerprintsFor(String output, String taskPath) {
        return blockFor(output, taskPath).files();
    }

    private record Block(String implementation, Map<String, Map<String, String>> files,
                          Map<String, String> values, String key, String nonCacheableReason) {}

    private static Block blockFor(String output, String taskPath) {
        List<String> implementations = new ArrayList<>();
        Map<String, String> values = new LinkedHashMap<>();
        Map<String, Map<String, String>> files = new LinkedHashMap<>();
        boolean inTarget = false;
        for (String raw : output.split("\\R")) {
            String line = raw.strip();
            Matcher header = TASK_HEADER.matcher(line);
            if (header.find()) {
                if (inTarget) {
                    return new Block(implementation(implementations), files, values, null, null);
                }
                inTarget = header.group(1).equals(taskPath);
                continue;
            }
            if (!inTarget) {
                continue;
            }
            Matcher m;
            if ((m = KEY.matcher(line)).matches()) {
                return new Block(implementation(implementations), files, values, m.group(2), null);
            }
            if ((m = NON_CACHEABLE.matcher(line)).matches()) {
                return new Block(implementation(implementations), files, values, null, m.group(1));
            }
            if ((m = IMPLEMENTATION.matcher(line)).matches()) {
                implementations.add(m.group(1));
            } else if ((m = ADDITIONAL_IMPLEMENTATION.matcher(line)).matches()) {
                // A task action / doLast block's own implementation hash; folded in alongside the task
                // class's so a build-logic change made only through a task action is still visible here.
                implementations.add(m.group(1));
            } else if ((m = VALUE.matcher(line)).matches()) {
                values.put(m.group(1), m.group(2));
            } else if ((m = FILES.matcher(line)).matches()) {
                files.put(m.group(1), entries(m.group(2), line));
            } else if (OUTPUT_PROPERTY.matcher(line).matches()) {
                // Does not affect the key material we track.
            } else if (line.startsWith("Appending ")) {
                throw unrecognised(line);
            }
        }
        return new Block(implementation(implementations), files, values, null, null);
    }

    private static String implementation(List<String> implementations) {
        return String.join(", ", implementations);
    }

    private static Map<String, String> entries(String body, String originalLine) {
        Map<String, String> out = new LinkedHashMap<>();
        if (body.isEmpty() || body.equals("EMPTY")) {
            return out;
        }
        // Split before parsing each entry so a "'" inside a normalized path can never merge two entries:
        // every entry boundary is ", " followed by the next entry's absolute path, which always starts with "/".
        for (String entry : body.split(", (?=/)")) {
            Matcher m = ENTRY.matcher(entry);
            if (!m.matches()) {
                throw unrecognised(originalLine);
            }
            String value = m.group(2) != null ? m.group(2) : entry.endsWith("DIR") ? "DIR" : "MISSING";
            out.put(m.group(1), value);
        }
        return out;
    }

    private static IllegalArgumentException unrecognised(String line) {
        return new IllegalArgumentException("unrecognised cache debug output: " + line);
    }
}
```

`tools/tracer/src/main/java/io/joeyang/tracer/cli/GradleKeyProvider.java`:
```java
package io.joeyang.tracer.cli;

import java.nio.file.Files;
import java.nio.file.Path;
import java.time.Duration;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Objects;
import java.util.Optional;
import java.util.regex.Pattern;

/** Runs the worktree's own {@code gradlew} with cache debugging on and parses the task's key out of the output. */
public final class GradleKeyProvider implements KeyProvider {
    private static final String WHAT_WENT_WRONG_HEADER = "* What went wrong:";
    /** A javac error line such as {@code AppA.java:11: error: ';' expected}, with or without a leading path. */
    private static final Pattern JAVAC_ERROR = Pattern.compile("^\\S+\\.java:\\d+: error: .+$");

    private final ProcessRunner runner;
    private final boolean offline;
    private final Duration timeout;

    public GradleKeyProvider(ProcessRunner runner, boolean offline, Duration timeout) {
        this.runner = Objects.requireNonNull(runner, "runner must not be null");
        this.offline = offline;
        this.timeout = Objects.requireNonNull(timeout, "timeout must not be null");
    }

    @Override
    public TaskKey keyFor(Path worktree, String taskPath) throws ProviderException {
        Path gradlew = worktree.resolve("gradlew");
        if (!Files.isExecutable(gradlew)) {
            throw new ProviderException("no executable gradlew in " + worktree + " (does this commit have the wrapper?)");
        }
        List<String> command = new ArrayList<>(List.of(gradlew.toString(), "--console=plain", "--no-parallel",
                "--build-cache", "-Dorg.gradle.caching.debug=true", taskPath));
        if (offline) {
            command.add("--offline");
        }
        ProcessRunner.Result result;
        try {
            result = runner.run(worktree, timeout, command);
        } catch (ProcessException e) {
            throw new ProviderException("gradle: " + e.getMessage(), e);
        }
        if (!result.ok()) {
            String cause = javacError(result).or(() -> whatWentWrong(result)).orElseGet(result::firstErrorLine);
            throw new ProviderException("gradle failed for " + taskPath + ": " + cause);
        }
        String output = result.stdout() + "\n" + result.stderr();
        try {
            return CacheDebugLog.parse(output, taskPath).orElseThrow(() -> new ProviderException(
                    CacheDebugLog.nonCacheableReason(output, taskPath)
                            .map(reason -> taskPath + " is not cacheable (" + reason + "), so it has no cache key; use compileJava or fingerprint")
                            .orElse("gradle printed no cache key for " + taskPath
                                    + " (unrecognised cache debug output, or the task did not print a key)")));
        } catch (IllegalArgumentException e) {
            throw new ProviderException("gradle debug output for " + taskPath + " could not be parsed: " + e.getMessage(), e);
        }
    }

    /** The first javac error line ({@code <file>.java:<line>: error: <message>}) anywhere in the output, if any. */
    private static Optional<String> javacError(ProcessRunner.Result result) {
        return (result.stdout() + "\n" + result.stderr()).lines()
                .map(String::strip)
                .filter(line -> JAVAC_ERROR.matcher(line).matches())
                .findFirst();
    }

    /**
     * The first non-blank line after Gradle's {@code * What went wrong:} header, joined with its first
     * continuation line (the line right after it, when that line is indented and non-blank) — Gradle often
     * puts the actual cause there rather than on the header's own first line, e.g. "Selection failed" /
     * "  Cannot locate tasks that match ...".
     */
    private static Optional<String> whatWentWrong(ProcessRunner.Result result) {
        List<String> lines = (result.stdout() + "\n" + result.stderr()).lines().toList();
        for (int i = 0; i < lines.size(); i++) {
            if (!lines.get(i).strip().equals(WHAT_WENT_WRONG_HEADER)) {
                continue;
            }
            for (int j = i + 1; j < lines.size(); j++) {
                String first = lines.get(j).strip();
                if (first.isEmpty()) {
                    continue;
                }
                if (j + 1 < lines.size()) {
                    String continuationRaw = lines.get(j + 1);
                    String continuation = continuationRaw.strip();
                    if (!continuation.isEmpty() && (continuationRaw.startsWith(" ") || continuationRaw.startsWith("\t"))) {
                        return Optional.of(first + ": " + continuation);
                    }
                }
                return Optional.of(first);
            }
        }
        return Optional.empty();
    }
}
```

```bash
Parses the per-task block Gradle prints with -Dorg.gradle.caching.debug=true:
implementation hash, scalar input fingerprints and per-file fingerprints,
```

Append to `docs/tracer.md`, then make **Commit B** — `GradleKeyProvider`, `GradleKeyProviderTest`, and this doc change:
```markdown
## Gradle oracle
`GradleKeyProvider` runs `<worktree>/gradlew --console=plain --no-parallel --build-cache -Dorg.gradle.caching.debug=true <task>`
(`--offline` when asked) and `CacheDebugLog` parses, per task block:
- `Appending implementation to build cache key: <class>@<hash>` → implementation hash (changes with build logic)
- `Appending input value fingerprint for '<property>' ...: <hash>` → scalar inputs such as `options.compilerArgs`
- `Appending input file fingerprints for '<property>' ...: <hash> - <NORMALIZATION>{<path>=<'normalized'|IGNORED> / <hash|DIR|MISSING>, ...}` → per-file hashes
  (a directory or a deleted file prints `DIR`/`MISSING` in place of a hash; project dependencies otherwise appear as class
  directories on the compile classpath and as jars on the runtime classpath, which later commits use to name a culprit like
  `core classes` under `compileJava` versus `core.jar` under `fingerprint`)
- `Build cache key for task '<path>' is <hash>` → the key, which closes the block
- `Non-cacheable because <reason>` (jar, installDist, …) also closes the block with no key; the provider reports the reason
  instead of a silent `clean`
Any other `Appending ...` line inside the task's own block — a normalization token outside the known set, a malformed
`{...}` entry, anything a future Gradle version prints differently — fails parsing immediately with "unrecognised cache
debug output" naming the line, per spec §9, rather than silently dropping or mis-splitting it into a wrong key. An
UP-TO-DATE task prints the key line without input lines; the tracer always runs in a fresh worktree, so the inputs are
present whenever the key changed.

When `gradlew` exits non-zero, the provider first looks for a javac error line (`<file>.java:<line>: error: <message>`)
anywhere in the output, since Gradle's `* What went wrong:` header for a compile failure only says "Compilation failed;
see the compiler output below." — not the actual error. Failing that, it looks for the header and joins its first
non-blank line with the following line when that one is indented (e.g. "Selection failed" / "Cannot locate tasks that
match ..." for an unknown task, where the real cause is the indented continuation, not the header's own first line).
Failing that, it falls back to the first non-blank stderr/stdout line. The task path is named in the message either way.
A line inside the target task's own cache-debug block that CacheDebugLog cannot recognise is reported the same way, as a
`ProviderException` naming the task and the offending line, instead of escaping uncaught. Computing *which* culprit
changed between two commits' `TaskKey`s, and the `./trace` revision-range walk that drives this provider, arrive in
later commits.
```

```bash
git add tools/tracer/src/main/java/io/joeyang/tracer/cli/GradleKeyProvider.java \
        tools/tracer/src/test/java/io/joeyang/tracer/cli/GradleKeyProviderTest.java \
        docs/tracer.md
git commit -m "feat(tracer): resolve compileJava and fingerprint keys through Gradle

GradleKeyProvider runs the worktree's own gradlew with cache debugging
flags and hands the output to CacheDebugLog. Treats a non-zero exit, a
timeout, and a successful run that printed no key (e.g. a non-cacheable
task) as distinct ProviderException cases instead of a silent clean,
since each needs a different fix from whoever is reading the report."
```

### Task 8: BazelDiffProvider and the bazel-diff fetch script

**Files:**
- Create: `tools/fetch-bazel-diff.sh` (executable), `tools/tracer/src/main/java/io/joeyang/tracer/cli/BazelDiffProvider.java`
- Modify: `.gitignore` (add `tools/bin/`)
- Test: `tools/tracer/src/test/java/io/joeyang/tracer/cli/BazelDiffProviderTest.java`
- Modify: `docs/bazel-build.md`, `docs/tracer.md`

A later review round (see the fixup commits below) added one package-private overload to the shared test helper
`tools/tracer/src/test/java/io/joeyang/tracer/cli/FakeProcessRunner.java` — defined for Task 6, not reproduced here —
so a scripted command can run a `Runnable` side effect (e.g. writing the output file a real tool would) when it
"executes": `FakeProcessRunner on(String commandPrefix, int exit, String stdout, String stderr, Runnable onRun)`.
Purely additive; the existing 4-arg `on(...)` is unchanged.

Verified in the spike: bazel-diff is now a single static binary (Rust); `generate-hashes -w <ws> -b bazel --fineGrainedHashExternalRepos=@maven <out.json>` hashes each `@maven//...` target separately (51 of them in the spike; the bare spelling `maven` fails with `ERROR: invalid package name 'maven/'`); `get-impacted-targets -w <ws> --startingHashes a.json --finalHashes b.json -o out.txt` writes one label per line. Building it from the Bazel Central Registry module would compile Rust via rules_rust, so we download the pinned release binary instead. Each worktree gets its own Bazel output base; the provider runs `bazel clean --expunge` in the worktree afterwards so disk does not leak.

- [ ] **Step 1: Fetch script and ignore rule**

Append to `.gitignore`:
```
# downloaded tools
tools/bin/
```

`tools/fetch-bazel-diff.sh`:
```bash
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# Downloads the pinned bazel-diff release binary into tools/bin/ (gitignored) and verifies its checksum.
set -euo pipefail
VERSION="v60.0.0"
SHA256="4c1e70c955a7a950fc0ce15d921e976078a5fd1571e423c05016cb6e7c433dac"
URL="https://github.com/Tinder/bazel-diff/releases/download/$VERSION/bazel-diff-rust-linux-amd64"
ROOT="$(cd "$(dirname "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}")/.." && pwd)"
DEST="$ROOT/tools/bin/bazel-diff"

OS="$(uname -s)"
ARCH="$(uname -m)"
if [[ "$OS" != "Linux" || "$ARCH" != "x86_64" ]]; then
  echo "error: only the linux-amd64 bazel-diff release is pinned, but this host is $OS/$ARCH;" \
       "set BAZEL_DIFF=/path/to/binary to point at one you built or downloaded yourself." >&2
  exit 1
fi

if [[ -x "$DEST" ]] && echo "$SHA256  $DEST" | sha256sum -c --quiet - 2>/dev/null; then
  echo "bazel-diff $VERSION already present at $DEST"
  exit 0
fi
mkdir -p "$ROOT/tools/bin"
TMP="$(mktemp "$ROOT/tools/bin/.bazel-diff.XXXXXX")"
trap 'rm -f "$TMP"' EXIT
curl -fsSL --connect-timeout 20 --max-time 300 --retry 3 -o "$TMP" "$URL"
echo "$SHA256  $TMP" | sha256sum -c --quiet -
chmod 0755 "$TMP"
mv "$TMP" "$DEST"
trap - EXIT
"$DEST" --version
```
Run: `chmod +x tools/fetch-bazel-diff.sh && tools/fetch-bazel-diff.sh`
Expected: `bazel-diff 60.0.0`. (Only linux-amd64 is pinned; other platforms set `BAZEL_DIFF=/path/to/binary`.)

```java
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertEquals;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertFalse;

import java.io.IOException;
import java.io.PrintStream;

import java.time.Duration;

import java.util.function.Function;

import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.io.TempDir;

class BazelDiffProviderTest {
    @TempDir Path tmp;
    Path repo, worktree, binary, out;

    @BeforeEach
    void setUp() throws IOException {
        repo = Files.createDirectories(tmp.resolve("repo"));
        worktree = Files.createDirectories(tmp.resolve("wt-aaaaaaa"));
        binary = tmp.resolve("bazel-diff");
        out = tmp.resolve("hashes-aaaaaaa.json");
    }

    BazelDiffProvider provider(FakeProcessRunner runner, boolean offline) {
        return new BazelDiffProvider(runner, repo, offline, Duration.ofMinutes(1), binary);
    }

    BazelDiffProvider provider(FakeProcessRunner runner, boolean offline, PrintStream warnings) {
        return new BazelDiffProvider(runner, repo, offline, Duration.ofMinutes(1), binary, warnings);
    }

    String generate() { return binary + " generate-hashes -w " + worktree + " -b bazel --fineGrainedHashExternalRepos=@maven"; }

    private static void writeQuietly(Path path, String content) {
        try {
            Files.writeString(path, content);
        } catch (IOException e) {
            throw new UncheckedIOException(e);
        }
    }

    @Test void hashesRunsGenerateHashesFromTheRepoAndCleansTheWorktreeServer() throws Exception {
        FakeProcessRunner runner = new FakeProcessRunner()
                .on(generate(), 0, "", "", () -> writeQuietly(out, "{}"))
                .on("bazel clean --expunge", 0, "", "");

        Path result = provider(runner, false).hashes(worktree, out);

        assertEquals(out, result);
        assertEquals(List.of(binary.toString(), "generate-hashes", "-w", worktree.toString(), "-b", "bazel",
                "--fineGrainedHashExternalRepos=@maven", out.toString()), runner.calls.get(0));
        assertEquals(repo, runner.cwds.get(0));
        assertEquals(Duration.ofMinutes(1), runner.timeouts.get(0));
        assertEquals(List.of("bazel", "clean", "--expunge"), runner.calls.get(1));
        assertEquals(worktree, runner.cwds.get(1));
        assertEquals(Duration.ofMinutes(1), runner.timeouts.get(1));
    }

    @Test void offlineForbidsRepositoryDownloads() throws Exception {
        FakeProcessRunner runner = new FakeProcessRunner()
                .on(binary.toString(), 0, "", "", () -> writeQuietly(out, "{}"))
                .on("bazel clean", 0, "", "");
        provider(runner, true).hashes(worktree, out);
        assertEquals(List.of(binary.toString(), "generate-hashes", "-w", worktree.toString(), "-b", "bazel",
                "--fineGrainedHashExternalRepos=@maven", "--bazelCommandOptions=--repository_disable_download", out.toString()),
                runner.calls.get(0));
    }

    @Test void missingOutputIsAProviderError() {
        FakeProcessRunner runner = new FakeProcessRunner().on(generate(), 0, "", "").on("bazel clean", 0, "", "");
        ProviderException e = assertThrows(ProviderException.class, () -> provider(runner, false).hashes(worktree, out));
        assertTrue(e.getMessage().contains("no hashes file"), e.getMessage());
    }

    @Test void staleOutputFileDoesNotMaskABrokenRun() throws Exception {
        Files.writeString(out, "{\"stale\":true}");
        FakeProcessRunner runner = new FakeProcessRunner().on(generate(), 0, "", "").on("bazel clean", 0, "", "");
        ProviderException e = assertThrows(ProviderException.class, () -> provider(runner, false).hashes(worktree, out));
        assertTrue(e.getMessage().contains("no hashes file"), e.getMessage());
    }

    @Test void nonZeroExitCarriesTheFirstErrorLine() {
        FakeProcessRunner runner = new FakeProcessRunner().on(generate(), 1, "", "ERROR: no such package '@maven//'\n").on("bazel clean", 0, "", "");
        ProviderException e = assertThrows(ProviderException.class, () -> provider(runner, false).hashes(worktree, out));
        assertTrue(e.getMessage().contains("no such package"), e.getMessage());

    /**
     * Real stderr captured from {@code bazel-diff generate-hashes} against a worktree with a syntax error
     * injected into {@code utils/BUILD.bazel} (see commit message for how it was reproduced). bazel-diff's own
     * wrapper line ("[Error] Bazel query failed...") comes first; Bazel's real {@code ERROR:} lines come after.
     */
    private static final String BROKEN_PACKAGE_STDERR = ""
            + "\n"
            + "[Error] Bazel query failed, exit code 7: Loading: 0 packages loaded\n"
            + "ERROR: /repo/utils/BUILD.bazel:14:6: 'is' not supported, use '==' instead\n"
            + "ERROR: /repo/utils/BUILD.bazel:14:6: syntax error at 'is': expected newline\n"
            + "ERROR: package contains errors: utils\n"
            + "ERROR: package contains errors: utils: 'is' not supported, use '==' instead\n"
            + "ERROR: Error evaluating '//...:all-targets': error loading package 'utils': Package 'utils' contains errors\n";

    /**
     * Real stderr captured from a cold (freshly created) Bazel output base with {@code --repository_disable_download}:
     * bazel-diff's wrapper line comes first, then Bazel's repository-fetch failure, several lines in.
     */
    private static final String COLD_CACHE_OFFLINE_STDERR = ""
            + "\n"
            + "[Error] Bazel query failed, exit code 7: Loading: 0 packages loaded\n"
            + "INFO: Repo package_metadata+ defined by rule http_archive in @@bazel_tools//tools/build_defs/repo:http.bzl\n"
            + "INFO: Repo rules_license+ defined by rule http_archive in @@bazel_tools//tools/build_defs/repo:http.bzl\n"
            + "ERROR: /base/external/bazel_tools/tools/build_defs/repo/http.bzl:200:45: An error occurred during the fetch of repository 'package_metadata+':\n"
            + "   Traceback (most recent call last):\n"
            + "\tFile \"/base/external/bazel_tools/tools/build_defs/repo/http.bzl\", line 200, column 45, in _http_archive_impl\n"
            + "\t\tdownload_info = ctx.download_and_extract(\n"
            + "Error in download_and_extract: java.io.IOException: Failed to download repository @@package_metadata+: download is disabled.\n"
            + "ERROR: error loading package under directory '': no such package '@@package_metadata+//rules': java.io.IOException: Failed to download repository @@package_metadata+: download is disabled.\n";

    @Test void bazelErrorPrefersTheDownloadDisabledLineOverBazelDiffsOwnWrapperLine() {
        FakeProcessRunner runner = new FakeProcessRunner().on(generate(), 1, "", COLD_CACHE_OFFLINE_STDERR).on("bazel clean", 0, "", "");
        ProviderException e = assertThrows(ProviderException.class, () -> provider(runner, true).hashes(worktree, out));
        assertTrue(e.getMessage().contains("download is disabled"), e.getMessage());
        assertTrue(e.getMessage().contains(worktree.toString()), "must name the worktree: " + e.getMessage());
    }

    @Test void bazelErrorPrefersTheRealBazelErrorLineOverBazelDiffsOwnWrapperLine() {
        FakeProcessRunner runner = new FakeProcessRunner().on(generate(), 1, "", BROKEN_PACKAGE_STDERR).on("bazel clean", 0, "", "");
        ProviderException e = assertThrows(ProviderException.class, () -> provider(runner, false).hashes(worktree, out));
        assertTrue(e.getMessage().contains("'is' not supported"), e.getMessage());
        assertTrue(!e.getMessage().contains("[Error] Bazel query failed"), e.getMessage());

    @Test void bazelErrorFallsBackToFirstErrorLineWhenNeitherPatternMatches() {
        FakeProcessRunner runner = new FakeProcessRunner().on(generate(), 1, "", "some other failure\n").on("bazel clean", 0, "", "");
        ProviderException e = assertThrows(ProviderException.class, () -> provider(runner, false).hashes(worktree, out));
        assertTrue(e.getMessage().contains("some other failure"), e.getMessage());

    @Test void missingBinaryIsAProviderError() {

        ProviderException e = assertThrows(ProviderException.class, () -> provider(runner, false).hashes(worktree, out));

        assertTrue(e.getMessage().contains("fetch-bazel-diff"), "must tell the user how to get it: " + e.getMessage());

    @Test void cleanupFailureIsIgnored() throws Exception {
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Implement**

`tools/tracer/src/main/java/io/joeyang/tracer/cli/BazelDiffProvider.java`:
```java
package io.joeyang.tracer.cli;

import java.io.IOException;
import java.io.PrintStream;
import java.nio.file.Files;
import java.nio.file.Path;
import java.time.Duration;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.Collections;
import java.util.LinkedHashSet;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Objects;
import java.util.Set;
import java.util.function.Function;
import java.util.regex.Pattern;

/**
 * The Bazel oracle: {@code bazel-diff generate-hashes} per worktree and {@code get-impacted-targets} per adjacent pair.
 * External Maven artifacts are hashed individually ({@code --fineGrainedHashExternalRepos=@maven}) so one library bump
 * impacts only its dependants. Every worktree gets its own Bazel output base, so the provider runs
 * {@code bazel clean --expunge} there afterwards to stop the server and free the disk.
 */
public final class BazelDiffProvider implements ImpactProvider {
    static final String FINE_GRAINED_REPOS = "@maven";
    private static final String HOW_TO_GET = " (run tools/fetch-bazel-diff.sh or set BAZEL_DIFF=/path/to/binary)";
    /** A Bazel label such as {@code //app/appA:appA}, {@code //lib}, or {@code @maven//:guava}. */
    private static final Pattern BAZEL_LABEL = Pattern.compile("^(@[^/]*)?//[^:]*(:[^:]+)?$");

    private final ProcessRunner runner;
    private final Path repo;
    private final boolean offline;
    private final Duration timeout;
    private final Path binary;
    private final PrintStream warnings;

    public BazelDiffProvider(ProcessRunner runner, Path repo, boolean offline, Duration timeout) {
        this(runner, repo, offline, timeout, defaultBinary(repo));
    }

    BazelDiffProvider(ProcessRunner runner, Path repo, boolean offline, Duration timeout, Path binary) {
        this(runner, repo, offline, timeout, binary, System.err);
    }

    BazelDiffProvider(ProcessRunner runner, Path repo, boolean offline, Duration timeout, Path binary, PrintStream warnings) {
        this.runner = Objects.requireNonNull(runner, "runner must not be null");
        this.repo = Objects.requireNonNull(repo, "repo must not be null");
        this.offline = offline;
        this.timeout = Objects.requireNonNull(timeout, "timeout must not be null");
        this.binary = Objects.requireNonNull(binary, "binary must not be null");
        this.warnings = Objects.requireNonNull(warnings, "warnings must not be null");
    }

    /** {@code $BAZEL_DIFF} if set, else the fetched copy under tools/bin, else whatever {@code bazel-diff} is on the PATH. */
    static Path defaultBinary(Path repo) {
        return defaultBinary(repo, System::getenv);
    }

    /** Same resolution, with the environment lookup injectable so tests do not depend on the real {@code $BAZEL_DIFF}. */
    static Path defaultBinary(Path repo, Function<String, String> getenv) {
        String env = getenv.apply("BAZEL_DIFF");
        if (env != null && !env.isBlank()) {
            return Path.of(env);
        }
        Path fetched = repo.resolve("tools/bin/bazel-diff");
        return Files.isExecutable(fetched) ? fetched : Path.of("bazel-diff");
    }

    @Override
    public Path hashes(Path worktree, Path output) throws ProviderException {
        List<String> command = new ArrayList<>(List.of(binary.toString(), "generate-hashes", "-w", worktree.toString(),
                "-b", "bazel", "--fineGrainedHashExternalRepos=" + FINE_GRAINED_REPOS));
        if (offline) {
            command.add("--bazelCommandOptions=--repository_disable_download");
        }
        command.add(output.toString());
        // A leftover file from a previous run must not be mistaken for this run's output if bazel-diff fails to write one.
        try {
            Files.deleteIfExists(output);
        } catch (IOException e) {
            throw new ProviderException("cannot remove the stale hashes file " + output + ": " + e.getMessage(), e);
        }
        boolean skipCleanup = false;
        try {
            ProcessRunner.Result result;
            try {
                result = run(repo, command);
            } catch (ProviderException e) {
                // Nothing to clean up only when bazel-diff never launched (e.g. the binary is missing): no Bazel server ran
                // in the worktree. A timeout or interruption may well have started one, so cleanup must still run then.
                if (e.getCause() instanceof ProcessException pe && pe.startFailed()) {
                    skipCleanup = true;
                }
                throw e;
            }
            if (!result.ok()) {
                throw new ProviderException("bazel-diff generate-hashes failed for " + worktree + ": " + bazelError(result));
            }
            if (!Files.isRegularFile(output)) {
                throw new ProviderException("bazel-diff produced no hashes file at " + output);
            }
            return output;
        } finally {
            if (!skipCleanup) {
                cleanUpServer(worktree);
            }
        }
    }

    @Override
    public Set<String> impactedTargets(Path previousHashes, Path currentHashes) throws ProviderException {
        Path impacted = currentHashes.resolveSibling(currentHashes.getFileName() + ".impacted.txt");
        List<String> command = List.of(binary.toString(), "get-impacted-targets", "-w", repo.toString(), "-b", "bazel",
                "--startingHashes", previousHashes.toString(), "--finalHashes", currentHashes.toString(), "-o", impacted.toString());
        ProcessRunner.Result result = run(repo, command);
        if (!result.ok()) {
            throw new ProviderException("bazel-diff get-impacted-targets failed for " + repo + ": " + bazelError(result));
        }
        List<String> lines;
        try {
            lines = Files.readAllLines(impacted);
        } catch (IOException e) {
            String reason = e.getMessage() == null || e.getMessage().equals(impacted.toString())
                    ? e.getClass().getSimpleName() : e.getMessage();
            throw new ProviderException("cannot read " + impacted + ": " + reason, e);
        }
        Set<String> targets = new LinkedHashSet<>();
        for (String rawLine : lines) {
            String line = rawLine.strip();
            if (line.isEmpty()) {
                continue;
            }
            if (!BAZEL_LABEL.matcher(line).matches()) {
                throw new ProviderException("bazel-diff wrote a line that is not a Bazel label ('" + line + "') in " + impacted);
            }
            targets.add(line);
        }
        return Collections.unmodifiableSet(targets);
    }

    private ProcessRunner.Result run(Path cwd, List<String> command) throws ProviderException {
        try {
            return runner.run(cwd, timeout, command);
        } catch (ProcessException e) {
            throw new ProviderException("bazel-diff: " + e.getMessage() + HOW_TO_GET, e);
        }
    }

    /**
     * The real Bazel error, not bazel-diff's own wrapper line ({@code "[Error] Bazel query failed, exit code N: ..."}),
     * which never names the actual cause. Prefers a line mentioning "download is disabled" (a cold, offline output base),
     * else the first line Bazel itself prefixes with {@code ERROR:}, else the generic {@link ProcessRunner.Result#firstErrorLine()}.
     */
    private static String bazelError(ProcessRunner.Result result) {
        List<String> lines = (result.stdout() + "\n" + result.stderr()).lines().map(String::strip).filter(l -> !l.isEmpty()).toList();
        return lines.stream().filter(l -> l.contains("download is disabled")).findFirst()
                .or(() -> lines.stream().filter(l -> l.startsWith("ERROR:")).findFirst())
                .orElseGet(result::firstErrorLine);
    }

    private void cleanUpServer(Path worktree) {
        try {
            ProcessRunner.Result result = runner.run(worktree, timeout, List.of("bazel", "clean", "--expunge"));
            if (!result.ok()) {
                warnings.println("trace: bazel clean --expunge failed in " + worktree + ": " + result.firstErrorLine());
            }
        } catch (ProcessException e) {
            warnings.println("trace: could not clean the Bazel output base of " + worktree + " (" + e.getMessage() + ")");
        }
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 7: Docs and commit**

Append to `docs/bazel-build.md`:
```markdown
## bazel-diff
`tools/fetch-bazel-diff.sh` downloads the pinned release binary (v60.0.0, sha256-checked) to `tools/bin/` (gitignored).
`--offline` maps to `--repository_disable_download` for the Bazel queries: it works on a fresh output base once the repository
cache is warm, and fails with a clear "download is disabled" error when it is cold.
Override with `BAZEL_DIFF=/path/to/binary`. The tracer runs `generate-hashes -w <worktree> -b bazel --fineGrainedHashExternalRepos=@maven`
per commit and `get-impacted-targets` per adjacent pair, then `bazel clean --expunge` in the worktree so each throwaway
output base is removed. Building bazel-diff from the Bazel Central Registry module was rejected: it compiles Rust via rules_rust.
```

Append to `docs/tracer.md`:
```markdown
## Bazel oracle
`BazelDiffProvider` — see `docs/bazel-build.md`. Failure modes: binary missing → the error names `tools/fetch-bazel-diff.sh`;
non-zero exit → the real Bazel `ERROR:` line (not bazel-diff's own wrapper line); no output file → explicit error;
a stray non-label line in `get-impacted-targets`' output → explicit error naming the line. Mapping these into the
`BazelStatus` column (`NOT_AVAILABLE` rather than failing the whole row) arrives in a later commit.
```
(The sentence above reflects the review-round fixes landed after the commits below; it replaced an earlier draft
that claimed the Cli already mapped these to an `n/a` column, which it does not yet do.)

Land this as two commits at the fetch-script/provider seam, each leaving the build green (the fetch script and the
provider are independently reviewable, and the script has no code dependency on the provider):

```bash
chmod +x tools/fetch-bazel-diff.sh
git add .gitignore tools/fetch-bazel-diff.sh docs/bazel-build.md
git commit -m "build(tools): fetch a pinned bazel-diff binary

bazel-diff ships as a single static binary; fetching the pinned, checksummed
release into tools/bin/ (gitignored) is simpler and faster than building the
Bazel Central Registry module, which compiles Rust via rules_rust."

git add tools/tracer/src/main/java/io/joeyang/tracer/cli/BazelDiffProvider.java \
        tools/tracer/src/test/java/io/joeyang/tracer/cli/BazelDiffProviderTest.java \
        docs/tracer.md
git commit -m "feat(tracer): detect impacted Bazel targets with bazel-diff

Maven artifacts are hashed individually (--fineGrainedHashExternalRepos=@maven)
so one library bump impacts only its dependants, not everything. Each worktree
gets its own Bazel output base, so the provider runs bazel clean --expunge
there afterwards to stop the server and free the disk; that cleanup failure is
swallowed rather than masking the real hashing result."
```

A later review round added five more fixup commits (bazelError, a testable binary override, impacted-target
validation, a hardened fetch script, a doc correction) plus one extra commit, `build(bazel): record the module
extensions bazel-diff's mod graph evaluates`, committing the `MODULE.bazel.lock` growth (`bazel mod graph`
evaluating `rules_android`'s `apksig_extension`/`com_android_dex_extension`, ~36 lines) that bazel-diff's first
real run triggers — see `docs/bazel-build.md`'s bazel-diff section for why.

A re-review round found that a timed-out or interrupted `generate-hashes` left `started` false, skipping
`bazel clean --expunge` in exactly the case most likely to leave a Bazel server running. The fix adds an
additive `startFailed()` accessor on `ProcessException` (defined for Task 6 — see that task's region for the
current block) distinguishing "could not launch" (`cannotStart`, cleanup skipped — there is nothing to clean
up) from "ran but did not finish" (`timeout`/`interrupted`, cleanup still runs). The
same round also added two `impactedTargets` failure-path tests that had no coverage: a non-zero bazel-diff
exit (asserting the message names the repo) and a `ProcessException` (asserting it carries the fetch hint).
It also corrected `docs/bazel-build.md`'s bazel-diff section: the measured hashes are identical with or without
the extra `MODULE.bazel.lock` entries, so the real effect of letting the lockfile drift is a dirty `git status`,
not drifting hashes as an earlier draft claimed; and `get-impacted-targets`, not just `generate-hashes`, also
runs `bazel mod graph` — `generate-hashes` in its `-w` workspace (the worktree being hashed), `get-impacted-targets`
in the repo checkout, not both in the repo checkout as a later draft of this note first said.
