`tools/tracer/src/test/java/io/joeyang/tracer/cli/RevisionRangeTest.java`:
```java
package io.joeyang.tracer.cli;

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertEquals;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertThrows;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertTrue;

import java.nio.file.Path;
import java.util.List;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;

class RevisionRangeTest {
    static final String A = "a".repeat(40), B = "b".repeat(40), C = "c".repeat(40), D = "d".repeat(40);
    final Path repo = Path.of("/repo");

    @Test void returnsBaseThenCommitsOldestFirst() throws Exception {
        FakeProcessRunner git = new FakeProcessRunner()
                .on("git rev-parse --verify --quiet v1^{commit}", 0, A + "\n", "")
                .on("git rev-parse --verify --quiet HEAD^{commit}", 0, C + "\n", "")
                .on("git log -1 --format=%H%x1f%P%x1f%s " + A, 0, A + "\u001f\u001fbaseline\n", "")
                .on("git log --reverse --first-parent --format=%H%x1f%P%x1f%s " + A + ".." + C, 0,
                        B + "\u001f" + A + "\u001fcore: add shout\n" + C + "\u001f" + B + "\u001fdeps: bump gson\n", "");

        List<Commit> commits = new RevisionRange(git, repo).resolve("v1", "HEAD");

        assertEquals(List.of(new Commit(A, "baseline"), new Commit(B, "core: add shout"), new Commit(C, "deps: bump gson")), commits);
        assertEquals(repo, git.cwds.get(0));
    }

    // Real git, under --quiet, exits 1 with no stderr for an unresolvable revision (verified against a real repo).
    @Test void unknownRevisionIsAUsageError() {
        FakeProcessRunner git = new FakeProcessRunner()
                .on("git rev-parse --verify --quiet nope^{commit}", 1, "", "");

        UsageException e = assertThrows(UsageException.class, () -> new RevisionRange(git, repo).resolve("nope", "HEAD"));
        assertTrue(e.getMessage().contains("nope"), e.getMessage());
    }

    // Exit 128 ("fatal: not a git repository", a broken worktree, ...) is a tool failure, not a usage error: the
    // revision itself may well be fine, so this must not be reported (or exit-coded) as if it were unknown.
    @Test void revParseFailureOtherThanExitOneIsAProviderError() {
        FakeProcessRunner git = new FakeProcessRunner()
                .on("git rev-parse --verify --quiet HEAD^{commit}", 128, "", "fatal: not a git repository\n");

        ProviderException e = assertThrows(ProviderException.class, () -> new RevisionRange(git, repo).resolve("HEAD", "v2"));
        assertTrue(e.getMessage().contains("not a git repository"), e.getMessage());
    }

    @Test void baseLogFailureIsAProviderError() {
        FakeProcessRunner git = new FakeProcessRunner()
                .on("git rev-parse --verify --quiet v1^{commit}", 0, A + "\n", "")
                .on("git rev-parse --verify --quiet HEAD^{commit}", 0, C + "\n", "")
                .on("git log -1 --format=%H%x1f%P%x1f%s " + A, 128, "", "fatal: bad object\n");

        ProviderException e = assertThrows(ProviderException.class, () -> new RevisionRange(git, repo).resolve("v1", "HEAD"));
        assertTrue(e.getMessage().contains("bad object"), e.getMessage());
    }

    @Test void rangeLogFailureIsAProviderError() {
        FakeProcessRunner git = new FakeProcessRunner()
                .on("git rev-parse --verify --quiet v1^{commit}", 0, A + "\n", "")
                .on("git rev-parse --verify --quiet HEAD^{commit}", 0, C + "\n", "")
                .on("git log -1 --format=%H%x1f%P%x1f%s " + A, 0, A + "\u001f\u001fbaseline\n", "")
                .on("git log --reverse --first-parent --format=%H%x1f%P%x1f%s " + A + ".." + C, 128, "",
                        "fatal: bad revision range\n");

        ProviderException e = assertThrows(ProviderException.class, () -> new RevisionRange(git, repo).resolve("v1", "HEAD"));
        assertTrue(e.getMessage().contains("bad revision range"), e.getMessage());
    }

    // String.lines() treats a bare \r as a line terminator too; a commit subject that happens to contain one must
    // not be split into a spurious extra "row" that then fails to parse as sha\x1fparents\x1fsubject.
    @Test void subjectContainingCarriageReturnIsNotSplitIntoAnExtraRow() throws Exception {
        FakeProcessRunner git = new FakeProcessRunner()
                .on("git rev-parse --verify --quiet v1^{commit}", 0, A + "\n", "")
                .on("git rev-parse --verify --quiet HEAD^{commit}", 0, C + "\n", "")
                .on("git log -1 --format=%H%x1f%P%x1f%s " + A, 0, A + "\u001f\u001fbaseline\n", "")
                .on("git log --reverse --first-parent --format=%H%x1f%P%x1f%s " + A + ".." + C, 0,
                        B + "\u001f" + A + "\u001fsome\rtitle\n" + C + "\u001f" + B + "\u001ffinal\n", "");

        List<Commit> commits = new RevisionRange(git, repo).resolve("v1", "HEAD");

        assertEquals(List.of(new Commit(A, "baseline"), new Commit(B, "some\rtitle"), new Commit(C, "final")), commits);
    }

    @Test void emptyRangeIsAUsageError() {
        FakeProcessRunner git = new FakeProcessRunner()
                .on("git rev-parse --verify --quiet A^{commit}", 0, A + "\n", "")
                .on("git rev-parse --verify --quiet B^{commit}", 0, A + "\n", "")
                .on("git log -1 --format=%H%x1f%P%x1f%s " + A, 0, A + "\u001f\u001fbaseline\n", "")
                .on("git log --reverse --first-parent --format=%H%x1f%P%x1f%s " + A + ".." + A, 0, "", "");

        UsageException e = assertThrows(UsageException.class, () -> new RevisionRange(git, repo).resolve("A", "B"));
        assertTrue(e.getMessage().contains("no commits"), e.getMessage());
    }

    @Test void gitNotRunnableIsAProviderError() {
        FakeProcessRunner git = new FakeProcessRunner()
                .failWith("git rev-parse", ProcessException.cannotStart(List.of("git"), new java.io.IOException("No such file")));

        assertThrows(ProviderException.class, () -> new RevisionRange(git, repo).resolve("A", "B"));
    }

    @Test void malformedLogLineIsAProviderErrorNamingTheLine() {
        FakeProcessRunner git = new FakeProcessRunner()
                .on("git rev-parse --verify --quiet v1^{commit}", 0, A + "\n", "")
                .on("git rev-parse --verify --quiet HEAD^{commit}", 0, C + "\n", "")
                .on("git log -1 --format=%H%x1f%P%x1f%s " + A, 0, A + "\u001f\u001fbaseline\n", "")
                .on("git log --reverse --first-parent --format=%H%x1f%P%x1f%s " + A + ".." + C, 0,
                        "not-a-valid-sha\u001f" + A + "\u001fwhatever\n", "");

        ProviderException e = assertThrows(ProviderException.class, () -> new RevisionRange(git, repo).resolve("v1", "HEAD"));
        assertTrue(e.getMessage().contains("not-a-valid-sha"), e.getMessage());
    }

    // A side-branch commit merged into 'to' is not an ancestor of 'from' on the first-parent line: the first row's
    // own first parent must equal the resolved 'from', not just appear somewhere in 'to's history.
    @Test void sideBranchStartIsRejected() {
        FakeProcessRunner git = new FakeProcessRunner()
                .on("git rev-parse --verify --quiet v1^{commit}", 0, A + "\n", "")
                .on("git rev-parse --verify --quiet HEAD^{commit}", 0, C + "\n", "")
                .on("git log -1 --format=%H%x1f%P%x1f%s " + A, 0, A + "\u001f\u001fbaseline\n", "")
                .on("git log --reverse --first-parent --format=%H%x1f%P%x1f%s " + A + ".." + C, 0,
                        // B's first parent is D, not A: 'from' (A) is not on 'to's first-parent line.
                        B + "\u001f" + D + "\u001fside branch work\n" + C + "\u001f" + B + "\u001fmerge\n", "");

        UsageException e = assertThrows(UsageException.class, () -> new RevisionRange(git, repo).resolve("v1", "HEAD"));
        assertTrue(e.getMessage().contains(B) && e.getMessage().contains(A), e.getMessage());
    }

    // A root commit's %P is the empty string; parsing it must not crash, and it correctly fails the chain check
    // (an empty parent can never equal the 40-hex sha it is compared against) rather than being silently accepted.
    @Test void rootCommitWithEmptyParentFieldIsRejectedNotCrashed() {
        FakeProcessRunner git = new FakeProcessRunner()
                .on("git rev-parse --verify --quiet v1^{commit}", 0, A + "\n", "")
                .on("git rev-parse --verify --quiet HEAD^{commit}", 0, C + "\n", "")
                .on("git log -1 --format=%H%x1f%P%x1f%s " + A, 0, A + "\u001f\u001fbaseline\n", "")
                .on("git log --reverse --first-parent --format=%H%x1f%P%x1f%s " + A + ".." + C, 0,
                        C + "\u001f\u001froot commit\n", "");

        UsageException e = assertThrows(UsageException.class, () -> new RevisionRange(git, repo).resolve("v1", "HEAD"));
        assertTrue(e.getMessage().contains(C), e.getMessage());
    }
}
```

`tools/tracer/src/test/java/io/joeyang/tracer/cli/WorktreePoolTest.java`:
```java
package io.joeyang.tracer.cli;

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertEquals;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertFalse;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertThrows;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertTrue;

import java.io.ByteArrayOutputStream;
import java.io.IOException;
import java.io.PrintStream;
import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;
import java.nio.file.Files;
import java.nio.file.Path;
import java.time.Duration;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.concurrent.TimeUnit;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Assumptions;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.io.TempDir;

class WorktreePoolTest {
    static final Commit C1 = new Commit("1".repeat(40), "one");
    @TempDir Path tmp;
    final Path repo = Path.of("/repo");

    FakeProcessRunner happyGit() {
        return new FakeProcessRunner()
                .on("git worktree prune", 0, "", "")
                .on("git worktree add --detach", 0, "", "")
                .on("git worktree remove --force", 0, "", "");
    }

    @Test void prunesOnStartAndAddsDetachedWorktreeUnderRoot() throws Exception {
        FakeProcessRunner git = happyGit();
        try (WorktreePool pool = new WorktreePool(git, repo, tmp.resolve("wts"), false)) {
            Path dir = pool.checkout(C1);
            assertEquals(tmp.resolve("wts").resolve("wt-1111111"), dir);
            assertEquals(List.of("git", "worktree", "prune"), git.calls.get(0));
            assertEquals(List.of("git", "worktree", "add", "--detach", dir.toString(), C1.sha()), git.calls.get(1));
        }
    }

    @Test void closeRemovesEveryWorktree() throws Exception {
        FakeProcessRunner git = happyGit();
        Path dir;
        try (WorktreePool pool = new WorktreePool(git, repo, tmp.resolve("wts"), false)) {
            dir = pool.checkout(C1);
        }
        assertTrue(git.ran("git worktree remove --force " + dir));
    }

    @Test void keepWorktreesSkipsRemoval() throws Exception {
        FakeProcessRunner git = happyGit();
        try (WorktreePool pool = new WorktreePool(git, repo, tmp.resolve("wts"), true)) {
            pool.checkout(C1);
        }
        assertFalse(git.ran("git worktree remove"));
    }

    // Ctrl-C must leave --keep-worktrees' worktrees exactly where they were put, not wipe them via the shutdown
    // hook; registering the hook unconditionally (keep or not) would delete them on an interrupted run anyway.
    @Test void keepWorktreesRegistersNoShutdownHook() throws Exception {
        List<Thread> registered = new ArrayList<>();
        try (WorktreePool pool = new WorktreePool(happyGit(), repo, tmp.resolve("wts"), true, System.err, registered::add)) {
            pool.checkout(C1);
        }
        assertTrue(registered.isEmpty(), "keep=true must not register a shutdown hook: " + registered);
    }

    @Test void notKeepingWorktreesRegistersAShutdownHook() throws Exception {
        List<Thread> registered = new ArrayList<>();
        try (WorktreePool pool = new WorktreePool(happyGit(), repo, tmp.resolve("wts"), false, System.err, registered::add)) {
            pool.checkout(C1);
        }
        assertEquals(1, registered.size());
    }

    @Test void pruneFailureIsAProviderError() {
        FakeProcessRunner git = new FakeProcessRunner()
                .on("git worktree prune", 1, "", "fatal: prune failed\n");

        ProviderException e = assertThrows(ProviderException.class,
                () -> new WorktreePool(git, repo, tmp.resolve("wts"), false));
        assertTrue(e.getMessage().contains("prune failed"), e.getMessage());
    }

    @Test void releaseOfAnUntrackedPathIsRejected() throws Exception {
        try (WorktreePool pool = new WorktreePool(happyGit(), repo, tmp.resolve("wts"), false)) {
            Path untracked = tmp.resolve("wts").resolve("wt-9999999");
            assertThrows(IllegalArgumentException.class, () -> pool.release(untracked));
        }
    }

    @Test void worktreeOperationsUseTheirOwnTwoMinuteTimeout() throws Exception {
        FakeProcessRunner git = happyGit();
        try (WorktreePool pool = new WorktreePool(git, repo, tmp.resolve("wts"), false)) {
            pool.checkout(C1);
        }
        assertTrue(!git.timeouts.isEmpty() && git.timeouts.stream().allMatch(t -> t.equals(Duration.ofMinutes(2))),
                git.timeouts.toString());
    }

    // A failed removal must be visible, not silently swallowed: the warnings stream is injected (rather than
    // always System.err) exactly so a test can see it without capturing the real stream.
    @Test void removeFailureWarningNamesTheDirAndReason() throws Exception {
        FakeProcessRunner git = new FakeProcessRunner()
                .on("git worktree prune", 0, "", "")
                .on("git worktree add --detach", 0, "", "")
                .on("git worktree remove --force", 1, "", "fatal: not a working tree\n");
        ByteArrayOutputStream captured = new ByteArrayOutputStream();
        PrintStream warnings = new PrintStream(captured, true, StandardCharsets.UTF_8);
        Path dir;
        try (WorktreePool pool = new WorktreePool(git, repo, tmp.resolve("wts"), false, warnings, Runtime.getRuntime()::addShutdownHook)) {
            dir = pool.checkout(C1);
        }
        String output = captured.toString(StandardCharsets.UTF_8);
        assertTrue(output.contains(dir.toString()) && output.contains("not a working tree"), output);
    }

    @Test void sweepIgnoresEntriesThatDoNotLookLikeAWorktreeDirectory() throws Exception {
        Path root = tmp.resolve("wts");
        Files.createDirectories(root);
        Files.writeString(root.resolve("wt-README.txt"), "not a dir, even though the name matches the prefix");
        Files.createDirectories(root.resolve("wt-tooshort")); // dir, but not 7 hex chars
        Files.createDirectories(root.resolve("other"));       // dir, wrong prefix entirely

        try (WorktreePool pool = new WorktreePool(happyGit(), repo, root, false)) {
            assertTrue(Files.exists(root.resolve("wt-README.txt")));
            assertTrue(Files.exists(root.resolve("wt-tooshort")));
            assertTrue(Files.exists(root.resolve("other")));
        }
    }

    @Test void staleDirectoryIsDeletedBeforeAdd() throws Exception {
        Path stale = tmp.resolve("wts").resolve("wt-1111111");
        Files.createDirectories(stale);
        Files.writeString(stale.resolve("leftover.txt"), "x");
        FakeProcessRunner git = happyGit();
        try (WorktreePool pool = new WorktreePool(git, repo, tmp.resolve("wts"), false)) {
            pool.checkout(C1);
            assertFalse(Files.exists(stale.resolve("leftover.txt")));
        }
    }

    @Test void staleWorktreeDirsFromKilledRunsAreSweptAtStart() throws Exception {
        Path stale = tmp.resolve("wts").resolve("wt-0000000");
        Files.createDirectories(stale);
        Files.writeString(stale.resolve("leftover.txt"), "x");
        try (WorktreePool pool = new WorktreePool(happyGit(), repo, tmp.resolve("wts"), false)) {
            assertFalse(Files.exists(stale));
        }
    }

    @Test void addFailureIsAProviderErrorNamingTheCommit() throws Exception {
        FakeProcessRunner git = new FakeProcessRunner()
                .on("git worktree prune", 0, "", "")
                .on("git worktree add --detach", 128, "", "fatal: invalid reference\n");
        try (WorktreePool pool = new WorktreePool(git, repo, tmp.resolve("wts"), false)) {
            ProviderException e = assertThrows(ProviderException.class, () -> pool.checkout(C1));
            assertTrue(e.getMessage().contains("1111111"), e.getMessage());
            assertTrue(e.getMessage().contains("invalid reference"), e.getMessage());
        }
    }

    @Test void removalFailuresDoNotEscapeClose() throws Exception {
        FakeProcessRunner git = new FakeProcessRunner()
                .on("git worktree prune", 0, "", "")
                .on("git worktree add --detach", 0, "", "")
                .failWith("git worktree remove", ProcessException.timeout(List.of("git"), java.time.Duration.ofSeconds(1)));
        WorktreePool pool = new WorktreePool(git, repo, tmp.resolve("wts"), false);
        pool.checkout(C1);
        pool.close(); // must not throw
    }

    // A directory left behind mid-run (not caught by the constructor's one-time sweep) is still registered with
    // git; deleting it without 'git worktree remove' first would leave a dangling registration that makes the
    // next 'git worktree add' of the same path fail with "is a missing but already registered worktree".
    @Test void checkoutUnregistersBeforeDeletingAPreexistingDirectory() throws Exception {
        FakeProcessRunner git = happyGit();
        try (WorktreePool pool = new WorktreePool(git, repo, tmp.resolve("wts"), false)) {
            Path dir = tmp.resolve("wts").resolve("wt-1111111");
            Files.createDirectories(dir);
            Files.writeString(dir.resolve("leftover.txt"), "x");

            pool.checkout(C1);

            int removeIndex = git.calls.indexOf(List.of("git", "worktree", "remove", "--force", dir.toString()));
            int addIndex = git.calls.indexOf(List.of("git", "worktree", "add", "--detach", dir.toString(), C1.sha()));
            assertTrue(removeIndex >= 0 && removeIndex < addIndex,
                    "must unregister the stale worktree before re-adding it: " + git.calls);
            assertFalse(Files.exists(dir.resolve("leftover.txt")));
        }
    }

    // The fake cannot model git's own worktree registration bookkeeping, so this exercises the real binary: with
    // the old prune-before-sweep order, a dir left behind by a --keep-worktrees run is still "registered" when the
    // next pool's sweep deletes it (prune, run first, saw nothing missing yet), and the following 'worktree add'
    // of the same sha then fails with "is a missing but already registered worktree" (exit 128).
    @Test void reopeningThePoolCanReuseACommitKeptByThePreviousRun() throws Exception {
        Assumptions.assumeTrue(gitIsOnPath(), "git is not on PATH; skipping the real-git regression test");
        ProcessRunner git = realGit();
        Path gitRepo = tmp.resolve("repo");
        Files.createDirectories(gitRepo);
        run(git, gitRepo, "git", "init", "-q");
        run(git, gitRepo, "git", "-c", "user.name=t", "-c", "user.email=t@example.com",
                "commit", "--allow-empty", "-q", "-m", "first");
        String sha = run(git, gitRepo, "git", "rev-parse", "HEAD").stdout().strip();
        Commit commit = new Commit(sha, "first");
        Path root = tmp.resolve("wts");

        try (WorktreePool pool1 = new WorktreePool(git, gitRepo, root, true)) {
            pool1.checkout(commit);
        } // keep=true: the worktree is left registered and on disk for the next run to find.

        try (WorktreePool pool2 = new WorktreePool(git, gitRepo, root, true)) {
            Path dir = pool2.checkout(commit);
            assertTrue(Files.isDirectory(dir));
        }
    }

    private static boolean gitIsOnPath() {
        try {
            return new ProcessBuilder("git", "--version").start().waitFor(10, TimeUnit.SECONDS);
        } catch (IOException e) {
            return false;
        } catch (InterruptedException e) {
            Thread.currentThread().interrupt();
            return false;
        }
    }

    private static ProcessRunner realGit() {
        return (cwd, timeout, command) -> {
            Process process;
            try {
                process = new ProcessBuilder(command).directory(cwd.toFile()).start();
            } catch (IOException e) {
                throw ProcessException.cannotStart(command, e);
            }
            String out, err;
            try {
                out = new String(process.getInputStream().readAllBytes(), StandardCharsets.UTF_8);
                err = new String(process.getErrorStream().readAllBytes(), StandardCharsets.UTF_8);
                if (!process.waitFor(timeout.toSeconds(), TimeUnit.SECONDS)) {
                    process.destroyForcibly();
                    throw ProcessException.timeout(command, timeout);
                }
            } catch (IOException e) {
                throw ProcessException.cannotStart(command, e);
            } catch (InterruptedException e) {
                Thread.currentThread().interrupt();
                throw ProcessException.interrupted(command);
            }
            return new ProcessRunner.Result(process.exitValue(), out, err);
        };
    }

    private static ProcessRunner.Result run(ProcessRunner git, Path cwd, String... command) throws ProcessException {
        ProcessRunner.Result r = git.run(cwd, Duration.ofSeconds(30), List.of(command));
        if (!r.ok()) {
            throw new AssertionError("command failed: " + String.join(" ", command) + ": " + r.stderr());
        }
        return r;
    }
}
```

`tools/tracer/src/main/java/io/joeyang/tracer/cli/RevisionRange.java`:
```java
package io.joeyang.tracer.cli;

import java.nio.file.Path;
import java.time.Duration;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Objects;

/** Turns {@code --from/--to} into an ordered commit list: the base commit first, then every first-parent commit in from..to. */
public final class RevisionRange {
    private static final Duration TIMEOUT = Duration.ofSeconds(30);
    // %P is every parent sha, space-separated; for a root commit it is empty. Only the first token (the first
    // parent) matters here, since the walk follows --first-parent.
    private static final String FORMAT = "--format=%H%x1f%P%x1f%s";

    private final ProcessRunner git;
    private final Path repo;

    public RevisionRange(ProcessRunner git, Path repo) {
        this.git = Objects.requireNonNull(git, "git must not be null");
        this.repo = Objects.requireNonNull(repo, "repo must not be null");
    }

    public List<Commit> resolve(String from, String to) throws UsageException, ProviderException {
        String fromSha = revParse(from);
        String toSha = revParse(to);
        List<Commit> commits = new ArrayList<>();
        ProcessRunner.Result base = run("git", "log", "-1", FORMAT, fromSha);
        if (!base.ok()) {
            throw new ProviderException("git log failed for '" + fromSha + "': " + base.firstErrorLine());
        }
        commits.add(parse(base.stdout().strip()));
        ProcessRunner.Result log = run("git", "log", "--reverse", "--first-parent", FORMAT, fromSha + ".." + toSha);
        if (!log.ok()) {
            throw new ProviderException("git log failed: " + log.firstErrorLine());
        }
        // 'from' must be an ancestor of 'to' on its first-parent line: a side-branch commit merged into 'to' (or
        // any other commit not actually on that line) would otherwise pair up with an unrelated predecessor and
        // silently compare the wrong build inputs.
        String previousSha = fromSha;
        // split("\n") only: String.lines() also treats a bare \r as a line terminator, and %s keeps whatever
        // characters the subject has, including one, so a bare \r would otherwise split one row into two.
        for (String line : log.stdout().split("\n", -1)) {
            if (line.isBlank()) {
                continue;
            }
            Row row = parseRow(line);
            if (!row.firstParent().equals(previousSha)) {
                throw new UsageException("commit '" + row.commit().sha() + "' is not a first-parent descendant of '"
                        + previousSha + "'; 'from' must be an ancestor of 'to' on its first-parent history");
            }
            commits.add(row.commit());
            previousSha = row.commit().sha();
        }
        if (commits.size() == 1) {
            throw new UsageException("no commits between '" + from + "' and '" + to + "'");
        }
        return commits;
    }

    private String revParse(String rev) throws UsageException, ProviderException {
        ProcessRunner.Result r = run("git", "rev-parse", "--verify", "--quiet", rev + "^{commit}");
        // Under --quiet, real git exits 1 (with no stderr) for a revision it simply cannot resolve. Any other
        // non-zero exit (128 for "fatal: not a git repository", a detached/broken worktree, ...) is a tool
        // failure unrelated to whether the revision itself is valid.
        if (r.exitCode() == 1) {
            throw new UsageException("unknown revision '" + rev + "'");
        }
        if (!r.ok()) {
            throw new ProviderException("git rev-parse failed for '" + rev + "': " + r.firstErrorLine());
        }
        return r.stdout().strip();
    }

    private ProcessRunner.Result run(String... command) throws ProviderException {
        try {
            return git.run(repo, TIMEOUT, List.of(command));
        } catch (ProcessException e) {
            throw new ProviderException(e.getMessage(), e);
        }
    }

    /** One parsed {@code git log} row: the commit plus its first parent, used only to validate the first-parent chain. */
    private record Row(Commit commit, String firstParent) {}

    // Amendment (code review): Commit's compact constructor throws an unchecked IllegalArgumentException for a sha
    // that isn't 40 lowercase hex chars. The CLI's walk (Task 13) catches only UsageException and ProviderException,
    // so a SHA-256 repo or a stray `git log` output line would otherwise escape as a stack trace with exit code 1
    // (the usage-error code) instead of the ProviderException exit code. Translate it here, naming the bad line.
    private static Commit parse(String line) throws ProviderException {
        return parseRow(line).commit();
    }

    private static Row parseRow(String line) throws ProviderException {
        String[] parts = line.split("\u001f", 3);
        if (parts.length < 3) {
            throw new ProviderException("unexpected git log line: '" + line + "': expected sha, parents, subject");
        }
        String firstParent = parts[1].isBlank() ? "" : parts[1].split(" ", 2)[0];
        try {
            return new Row(new Commit(parts[0], parts[2]), firstParent);
        } catch (IllegalArgumentException e) {
            throw new ProviderException("unexpected git log line: '" + line + "': " + e.getMessage(), e);
        }
    }
}
```

`tools/tracer/src/main/java/io/joeyang/tracer/cli/WorktreePool.java`:
```java
package io.joeyang.tracer.cli;

import java.io.IOException;
import java.io.PrintStream;
import java.nio.file.Files;
import java.nio.file.Path;
import java.time.Duration;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.Collections;
import java.util.Comparator;
import java.util.LinkedHashSet;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Objects;
import java.util.Set;
import java.util.function.Consumer;
import java.util.regex.Pattern;
import java.util.stream.Stream;

/**
 * Materialises commits as detached git worktrees under a scratch root and removes them again.
 * Removal happens on {@link #close()} and from a JVM shutdown hook, so Ctrl-C leaves nothing behind, unless
 * {@code keep} is set.
 */
public final class WorktreePool implements AutoCloseable {
    private static final Duration TIMEOUT = Duration.ofMinutes(2);
    /**
     * A worktree directory's own name: {@code wt-} plus a 7-hex-char short sha, nothing else.
     * Package-private: {@link KeyComparator} reuses this to strip the worktree root off a culprit path before
     * naming it, since the two commits' worktrees otherwise differ by this prefix. If this naming convention ever
     * changes, {@link KeyComparator}'s stripping must change with it.
     */
    static final Pattern WT_DIR_NAME = Pattern.compile("wt-[0-9a-f]{7}");

    private final ProcessRunner git;
    private final Path repo;
    private final Path root;
    private final boolean keep;
    private final PrintStream warnings;
    private final Set<Path> created = Collections.synchronizedSet(new LinkedHashSet<>());
    /** Null when {@code keep} is true: Ctrl-C must leave kept worktrees exactly where {@code --keep-worktrees} put them. */
    private final Thread shutdownHook;

    public WorktreePool(ProcessRunner git, Path repo, Path root, boolean keep) throws ProviderException {
        this(git, repo, root, keep, System.err, Runtime.getRuntime()::addShutdownHook);
    }

    /** Package-private seam: lets tests capture warnings and observe hook registration without touching real I/O or the JVM hook list. */
    WorktreePool(ProcessRunner git, Path repo, Path root, boolean keep, PrintStream warnings, Consumer<Thread> hookRegistrar)
            throws ProviderException {
        this.git = Objects.requireNonNull(git, "git must not be null");
        this.repo = Objects.requireNonNull(repo, "repo must not be null");
        this.root = Objects.requireNonNull(root, "root must not be null");
        this.keep = keep;
        this.warnings = Objects.requireNonNull(warnings, "warnings must not be null");
        Objects.requireNonNull(hookRegistrar, "hookRegistrar must not be null");
        // Sweep before prune: 'git worktree prune' only forgets a worktree whose directory is already missing.
        // After --keep-worktrees or a killed run the directory is still there, so pruning first is a no-op; the
        // sweep then deletes the directory out from under git, leaving a dangling registration that makes the
        // next 'worktree add' of the same path fail with "is a missing but already registered worktree".
        sweepStaleDirectories();
        ProcessRunner.Result pruneResult = run("git", "worktree", "prune");
        if (!pruneResult.ok()) {
            throw new ProviderException("git worktree prune failed: " + pruneResult.firstErrorLine());
        }
        try {
            Files.createDirectories(root);
        } catch (IOException e) {
            throw new ProviderException("cannot create worktree root " + root + ": " + e.getMessage(), e);
        }
        this.shutdownHook = keep ? null : new Thread(this::removeAll, "worktree-cleanup");
        if (shutdownHook != null) {
            hookRegistrar.accept(shutdownHook);
        }
    }

    /** A killed run (or a kept one) leaves {@code wt-*} directories behind; delete them before prune forgets them. */
    private void sweepStaleDirectories() {
        if (!Files.isDirectory(root)) {
            return;
        }
        try (Stream<Path> entries = Files.list(root)) {
            List<Path> stale = entries.filter(Files::isDirectory)
                    .filter(d -> WT_DIR_NAME.matcher(d.getFileName().toString()).matches())
                    .toList();
            stale.forEach(WorktreePool::deleteRecursively);
            if (!stale.isEmpty()) {
                warnings.println("trace: removed " + stale.size() + " stale worktree dir(s) under " + root);
            }
        } catch (IOException ignored) {
            // best effort; checkout() deletes a same-named leftover anyway
        }
    }

    public Path checkout(Commit commit) throws ProviderException {
        Path dir = root.resolve("wt-" + commit.shortSha());
        if (Files.exists(dir)) {
            // Unregister before deleting: if this directory is still a registered worktree (left behind between
            // the constructor's one-time sweep and this call), deleting it without 'git worktree remove' first
            // would leave git thinking it still exists, and the 'add' below would fail as "missing but already
            // registered". Best effort: if it was never registered, this fails harmlessly and is ignored.
            try {
                git.run(repo, TIMEOUT, List.of("git", "worktree", "remove", "--force", dir.toString()));
            } catch (ProcessException ignored) {
                // see above
            }
            deleteRecursively(dir);
        }
        ProcessRunner.Result r = run("git", "worktree", "add", "--detach", dir.toString(), commit.sha());
        if (!r.ok()) {
            throw new ProviderException("git worktree add failed for " + commit.shortSha() + ": " + r.firstErrorLine());
        }
        created.add(dir);
        return dir;
    }

    /** Removes one worktree early to bound disk usage during long ranges. */
    public void release(Path dir) {
        if (!created.contains(dir)) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("not a tracked worktree: " + dir);
        }
        remove(dir);
    }

    @Override
    public void close() {
        if (!keep) {
            removeAll();
        }
        if (shutdownHook != null) {
            try {
                Runtime.getRuntime().removeShutdownHook(shutdownHook);
            } catch (IllegalStateException alreadyShuttingDown) {
                // the hook itself is running; nothing to do
            }
        }
    }

    private void removeAll() {
        List<Path> snapshot;
        synchronized (created) {
            snapshot = new ArrayList<>(created);
        }
        for (Path dir : snapshot) {
            remove(dir);
        }
    }

    private void remove(Path dir) {
        try {
            ProcessRunner.Result r = git.run(repo, TIMEOUT, List.of("git", "worktree", "remove", "--force", dir.toString()));
            if (!r.ok()) {
                warnings.println("trace: could not remove worktree " + dir + " (" + r.firstErrorLine() + ")");
            }
        } catch (ProcessException e) {
            warnings.println("trace: could not remove worktree " + dir + " (" + e.getMessage() + ")");
        }
        deleteRecursively(dir);
        created.remove(dir);
    }

    private ProcessRunner.Result run(String... command) throws ProviderException {
        try {
            return git.run(repo, TIMEOUT, List.of(command));
        } catch (ProcessException e) {
            throw new ProviderException(e.getMessage(), e);
        }
    }

    private static void deleteRecursively(Path dir) {
        if (!Files.exists(dir)) {
            return;
        }
        try (Stream<Path> paths = Files.walk(dir)) {
            paths.sorted(Comparator.reverseOrder()).forEach(p -> {
                try {
                    Files.deleteIfExists(p);
                } catch (IOException ignored) {
                    // best effort; git worktree remove already did the real work
                }
            });
        } catch (IOException ignored) {
            // best effort
        }
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Document and commit**

Append to `docs/tracer.md`:
```markdown
## Git layer
- `RevisionRange.resolve(from, to)` → `[from, …from..to oldest first]`, following the first-parent line so adjacent rows are parent and child even across merges. Unknown revision or empty range → `UsageException`; git not runnable → `ProviderException`. A `git log` line that does not parse into a well-formed `Commit` (e.g. a repo on SHA-256 object format, or a stray output line) is also a `ProviderException` naming the offending line, rather than an uncaught `IllegalArgumentException` from `Commit`'s constructor.
- `WorktreePool` → `git worktree prune`, sweep of stale `wt-*` directories under the root (leftovers of a killed run), then `git worktree add --detach <root>/wt-<sha7> <sha>` per commit. `close()` and a shutdown hook run `git worktree remove --force`; `--keep-worktrees` disables removal.
```

```bash
git commit -m "feat(tracer): walk the first-parent commit range

Commits are listed oldest-first with the base commit in front so adjacent
pairs can be compared. Following first-parent means adjacent rows stay
parent and child even across merges. A git log line that doesn't parse
into a well-formed Commit (SHA-256 repo, stray output line) is translated
into a ProviderException naming the line, instead of escaping as an
uncaught IllegalArgumentException that the CLI's walk would misreport as
a usage error."

git add tools/tracer/src/main/java/io/joeyang/tracer/cli/WorktreePool.java \
        tools/tracer/src/test/java/io/joeyang/tracer/cli/WorktreePoolTest.java \
        docs/tracer.md
git commit -m "feat(tracer): manage one detached worktree per commit

Every commit in the walk needs its own checkout so Gradle/Bazel can run
against it without disturbing the caller's working tree or each other.
WorktreePool checks out a detached worktree per commit under a scratch
root, sweeps wt-* leftovers from a killed prior run (git has already
forgotten them after worktree prune), and removes everything on close
or JVM shutdown so Ctrl-C leaves nothing behind."
```

```java
package io.joeyang.tracer.cli;

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertEquals;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertTrue;

import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.Optional;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;

class KeyComparatorTest {
    static final Commit C = new Commit("c".repeat(40), "some change");
    static final String TASK = ":app:appA:compileJava";

    static TaskKey key(String hash, Map<String, Map<String, String>> files, Map<String, String> values, String impl) {
        return new TaskKey(TASK, hash, files, values, impl);
    }

    @Test void firstCommitIsBase() {
        TaskKey k = key("k1", Map.of(), Map.of(), "i");
        Verdict v = KeyComparator.verdict(C, Optional.empty(), Optional.of(k), Optional.empty(), BazelStatus.NONE);
        assertEquals(Status.BASE, v.status());
        assertEquals(List.of(), v.culprits());
    }

    @Test void sameKeyIsClean() {
        TaskKey a = key("k1", Map.of("classpath", Map.of("/x/core.jar", "aa")), Map.of(), "i");
        TaskKey b = key("k1", Map.of("classpath", Map.of("/y/core.jar", "aa")), Map.of(), "i");
        Verdict v = KeyComparator.verdict(C, Optional.of(a), Optional.of(b), Optional.empty(), BazelStatus.CLEAN);
        assertEquals(Status.CLEAN, v.status());
    }

    @Test void changedJarIsNamedByFileName() {
        TaskKey a = key("k1", Map.of("classpath", Map.of("/w1/core/build/libs/core.jar", "aa", "/w1/lib/build/libs/lib.jar", "bb")), Map.of(), "i");
        TaskKey b = key("k2", Map.of("classpath", Map.of("/w2/core/build/libs/core.jar", "a2", "/w2/lib/build/libs/lib.jar", "bb")), Map.of(), "i");
        Verdict v = KeyComparator.verdict(C, Optional.of(a), Optional.of(b), Optional.empty(), BazelStatus.IMPACTED);
        assertEquals(Status.CHANGED, v.status());
        assertEquals(List.of("core.jar"), v.culprits());
    }

    @Test void changedSourceIsNamedRelativeToItsModule() {
        TaskKey a = key("k1", Map.of("stableSources", Map.of("/w1/app/appA/src/main/java/io/joeyang/tracer/app/a/AppA.java", "aa")), Map.of(), "i");
        TaskKey b = key("k2", Map.of("stableSources", Map.of("/w2/app/appA/src/main/java/io/joeyang/tracer/app/a/AppA.java", "a2")), Map.of(), "i");
        Verdict v = KeyComparator.verdict(C, Optional.of(a), Optional.of(b), Optional.empty(), BazelStatus.IMPACTED);
        assertEquals(List.of("appA/src/main/java/io/joeyang/tracer/app/a/AppA.java"), v.culprits());
    }

    @Test void classFilesUnderOneOutputDirCollapseToModuleClasses() {
        TaskKey a = key("k1", Map.of("classpath", Map.of(
                "/w1/core/build/classes/java/main/io/joeyang/tracer/core/Greeter.class", "aa",
                "/w1/core/build/classes/java/main/io/joeyang/tracer/core/Other.class", "bb")), Map.of(), "i");
        TaskKey b = key("k2", Map.of("classpath", Map.of(
                "/w2/core/build/classes/java/main/io/joeyang/tracer/core/Greeter.class", "aa",
                "/w2/core/build/classes/java/main/io/joeyang/tracer/core/Other.class", "b2")), Map.of(), "i");
        Verdict v = KeyComparator.verdict(C, Optional.of(a), Optional.of(b), Optional.empty(), BazelStatus.IMPACTED);
        assertEquals(List.of("core classes"), v.culprits());
    }

    @Test void addedAndRemovedFilesCount() {
        TaskKey a = key("k1", Map.of("classpath", Map.of("/w1/libs/gson-2.10.1.jar", "aa")), Map.of(), "i");
        TaskKey b = key("k2", Map.of("classpath", Map.of("/w2/libs/gson-2.11.0.jar", "bb")), Map.of(), "i");
        Verdict v = KeyComparator.verdict(C, Optional.of(a), Optional.of(b), Optional.empty(), BazelStatus.IMPACTED);
        assertEquals(List.of("gson-2.10.1.jar", "gson-2.11.0.jar"), v.culprits());
    }

    @Test void moduleCacheJarsAreNamedByCoordinatesSoABumpIsOneCulprit() {
        TaskKey a = key("k1", Map.of("runtimeClasspath", Map.of(
                "/home/u/.gradle/caches/modules-2/files-2.1/com.google.code.gson/gson/2.10.1/abc/gson-2.10.1.jar", "aa")), Map.of(), "i");
        TaskKey b = key("k2", Map.of("runtimeClasspath", Map.of(
                "/home/u/.gradle/caches/modules-2/files-2.1/com.google.code.gson/gson/2.11.0/def/gson-2.11.0.jar", "bb")), Map.of(), "i");
        Verdict v = KeyComparator.verdict(C, Optional.of(a), Optional.of(b), Optional.empty(), BazelStatus.IMPACTED);
        assertEquals(List.of("com.google.code.gson:gson"), v.culprits());

    @Test void changedValueAndImplementationAreNamed() {
        TaskKey a = key("k1", Map.of(), Map.of("options.compilerArgs", "v1"), "impl1");
        TaskKey b = key("k2", Map.of(), Map.of("options.compilerArgs", "v2"), "impl2");
        Verdict v = KeyComparator.verdict(C, Optional.of(a), Optional.of(b), Optional.empty(), BazelStatus.IMPACTED);
        assertEquals(List.of("build logic", "options.compilerArgs"), v.culprits());

    @Test void culpritsAreCappedAtFour() {
        Map<String, String> many1 = Map.of("/a.jar", "1", "/b.jar", "1", "/c.jar", "1", "/d.jar", "1", "/e.jar", "1", "/f.jar", "1");
        Map<String, String> many2 = Map.of("/a.jar", "2", "/b.jar", "2", "/c.jar", "2", "/d.jar", "2", "/e.jar", "2", "/f.jar", "2");
        Verdict v = KeyComparator.verdict(C, Optional.of(key("k1", Map.of("classpath", many1), Map.of(), "i")),
                Optional.of(key("k2", Map.of("classpath", many2), Map.of(), "i")), Optional.empty(), BazelStatus.IMPACTED);
        assertEquals(5, v.culprits().size());
        assertEquals("+2 more", v.culprits().get(4));
    }

    @Test void keyChangedButNoFingerprintDifferenceStillNamesSomething() {
        TaskKey a = key("k1", Map.of(), Map.of(), "i");
        TaskKey b = key("k2", Map.of(), Map.of(), "i");
        Verdict v = KeyComparator.verdict(C, Optional.of(a), Optional.of(b), Optional.empty(), BazelStatus.IMPACTED);
        assertEquals(List.of("inputs changed (no fingerprint detail)"), v.culprits());
    }

    @Test void missingKeyIsBuildFailedWithTheError() {
        Verdict v = KeyComparator.verdict(C, Optional.of(key("k1", Map.of(), Map.of(), "i")), Optional.empty(),
                Optional.of("Could not resolve com.example:missing:1.0"), BazelStatus.NOT_AVAILABLE);
        assertEquals(Status.BUILD_FAILED, v.status());
        assertTrue(v.error().orElseThrow().contains("missing:1.0"));
    }

    @Test void changedResourceIsNamedByModuleResources() {
        TaskKey a = key("k1", Map.of("runtimeClasspath", Map.of("/w1/lib/build/resources/main/data.txt", "aa")), Map.of(), "i");
        TaskKey b = key("k2", Map.of("runtimeClasspath", Map.of("/w2/lib/build/resources/main/data.txt", "a2")), Map.of(), "i");
        Verdict v = KeyComparator.verdict(C, Optional.of(a), Optional.of(b), Optional.empty(), BazelStatus.IMPACTED);
        assertEquals(List.of("lib resources"), v.culprits());
    }

    @Test void rootProjectSourceHasNoModulePrefix() {
        TaskKey a = key("k1", Map.of("stableSources", Map.of("/scratch/wt-aaaaaaa/src/main/java/Root.java", "aa")), Map.of(), "i");
        TaskKey b = key("k2", Map.of("stableSources", Map.of("/scratch/wt-bbbbbbb/src/main/java/Root.java", "a2")), Map.of(), "i");
        Verdict v = KeyComparator.verdict(C, Optional.of(a), Optional.of(b), Optional.empty(), BazelStatus.IMPACTED);
        assertEquals(List.of("src/main/java/Root.java"), v.culprits());
    }

    @Test void rootProjectClassDirHasNoLeadingSpace() {
        TaskKey a = key("k1", Map.of("classpath", Map.of("/scratch/wt-aaaaaaa/build/classes/java/main/pkg/Foo.class", "aa")), Map.of(), "i");
        TaskKey b = key("k2", Map.of("classpath", Map.of("/scratch/wt-bbbbbbb/build/classes/java/main/pkg/Foo.class", "a2")), Map.of(), "i");
        Verdict v = KeyComparator.verdict(C, Optional.of(a), Optional.of(b), Optional.empty(), BazelStatus.IMPACTED);
        assertEquals(List.of("classes"), v.culprits());
    }

    @Test void worktreeRootContainingSrcDoesNotPolluteDisplayName() {
        TaskKey a = key("k1", Map.of("classpath",
                Map.of("/home/u/src/tmp/gradle-tracer/wt-aaaaaaa/core/build/libs/core.jar", "aa")), Map.of(), "i");
        TaskKey b = key("k2", Map.of("classpath",
                Map.of("/home/u/src/tmp/gradle-tracer/wt-bbbbbbb/core/build/libs/core.jar", "a2")), Map.of(), "i");
        Verdict v = KeyComparator.verdict(C, Optional.of(a), Optional.of(b), Optional.empty(), BazelStatus.IMPACTED);
        assertEquals(List.of("core.jar"), v.culprits());
    }

    @Test void nestedSrcSegmentUsesTheFirstOccurrenceAsTheModuleBoundary() {
        TaskKey a = key("k1", Map.of("stableSources",
                Map.of("/scratch/wt-aaaaaaa/core/src/main/resources/src/x.txt", "aa")), Map.of(), "i");
        TaskKey b = key("k2", Map.of("stableSources",
                Map.of("/scratch/wt-bbbbbbb/core/src/main/resources/src/x.txt", "a2")), Map.of(), "i");
        Verdict v = KeyComparator.verdict(C, Optional.of(a), Optional.of(b), Optional.empty(), BazelStatus.IMPACTED);
        assertEquals(List.of("core/src/main/resources/src/x.txt"), v.culprits());
    }

    @Test void dirOrMissingFingerprintValuesAreComparedLikeAnyOtherHash() {
        TaskKey a = key("k1", Map.of("classpath", Map.of(
                "/w1/core/build/classes/java/main/io/joeyang/tracer/core/Greeter.class", "DIR")), Map.of(), "i");
        TaskKey b = key("k2", Map.of("classpath", Map.of(
                "/w2/core/build/classes/java/main/io/joeyang/tracer/core/Greeter.class", "MISSING")), Map.of(), "i");
        Verdict v = KeyComparator.verdict(C, Optional.of(a), Optional.of(b), Optional.empty(), BazelStatus.IMPACTED);
        assertEquals(List.of("core classes"), v.culprits());
    }

    @Test void exactlyFiveCulpritsAreAllShownWithoutATruncationMarker() {
        Map<String, String> many1 = Map.of("/a.jar", "1", "/b.jar", "1", "/c.jar", "1", "/d.jar", "1", "/e.jar", "1");
        Map<String, String> many2 = Map.of("/a.jar", "2", "/b.jar", "2", "/c.jar", "2", "/d.jar", "2", "/e.jar", "2");
        Verdict v = KeyComparator.verdict(C, Optional.of(key("k1", Map.of("classpath", many1), Map.of(), "i")),
                Optional.of(key("k2", Map.of("classpath", many2), Map.of(), "i")), Optional.empty(), BazelStatus.IMPACTED);
        assertEquals(5, v.culprits().size());
        assertTrue(v.culprits().stream().noneMatch(c -> c.startsWith("+")), v.culprits().toString());
    }

    @Test void addedOrRemovedPropertiesAreDetectedAsCulprits() {
        TaskKey a = key("k1", Map.of("classpath", Map.of("/w1/core/build/libs/core.jar", "aa")), Map.of(), "i");
        TaskKey b = key("k2", Map.of(), Map.of("options.compilerArgs", "v2"), "i");
        Verdict v = KeyComparator.verdict(C, Optional.of(a), Optional.of(b), Optional.empty(), BazelStatus.IMPACTED);
        assertTrue(v.culprits().contains("core.jar"), v.culprits().toString());
        assertTrue(v.culprits().contains("options.compilerArgs"), v.culprits().toString());
    }

    @Test void noCacheKeyObtainedIsTheDefaultErrorMessage() {
        Verdict v = KeyComparator.verdict(C, Optional.empty(), Optional.empty(), Optional.empty(), BazelStatus.NOT_AVAILABLE);
        assertEquals(Status.BUILD_FAILED, v.status());
        assertEquals("no cache key obtained", v.error().orElseThrow());
    }

    @Test void buildLogicCulpritIsAlwaysFirst() {
        TaskKey a = key("k1", Map.of("classpath", Map.of("/w1/z/build/libs/z.jar", "aa")),
                Map.of("options.compilerArgs", "v1"), "impl1");
        TaskKey b = key("k2", Map.of("classpath", Map.of("/w2/z/build/libs/z.jar", "a2")),
                Map.of("options.compilerArgs", "v2"), "impl2");
        Verdict v = KeyComparator.verdict(C, Optional.of(a), Optional.of(b), Optional.empty(), BazelStatus.IMPACTED);
        assertEquals("build logic", v.culprits().get(0));
        assertTrue(v.culprits().containsAll(List.of("build logic", "z.jar", "options.compilerArgs")));
    }
}
```

```java
package io.joeyang.tracer.cli;

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertEquals;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertTrue;

import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.Optional;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;

class TableRendererTest {
    static TaskKey key(String k) { return new TaskKey(":app:appA:compileJava", k, Map.of(), Map.of(), "i"); }
    static Commit commit(char c, String subject) { return new Commit(String.valueOf(c).repeat(40), subject); }

    final List<Verdict> rows = List.of(
            new Verdict(commit('a', "baseline"), Status.BASE, Optional.of(key("3f9c0a1b2c3d4e5f6a7b8c9d0e1f2a3be1")), BazelStatus.NONE, List.of(), Optional.empty()),
            new Verdict(commit('b', "utils: body-only edit"), Status.CLEAN, Optional.of(key("3f9c0a1b2c3d4e5f6a7b8c9d0e1f2a3be1")), BazelStatus.IMPACTED, List.of(), Optional.empty()),
            new Verdict(commit('c', "core: add Greeter.shout() with a very long subject line that keeps going"), Status.CHANGED,
                    Optional.of(key("2bb40a1b2c3d4e5f6a7b8c9d0e1f2a3b77")), BazelStatus.IMPACTED, List.of("core.jar"), Optional.empty()),
            new Verdict(commit('d', "broken"), Status.BUILD_FAILED, Optional.empty(), BazelStatus.NOT_AVAILABLE, List.of(),
                    Optional.of("Could not resolve com.example:missing:1.0")));

    @Test void rendersHeaderRowsAndSummary() {
        String out = TableRenderer.render(":app:appA:compileJava", "//app/appA:appA", rows);
        String[] lines = out.split("\n");
        assertEquals(9, lines.length);
        // Exact lines, not startsWith/contains: these pin the fixed column widths (9/37/12/12/14) byte for byte.
        assertEquals("Tracing inputs of :app:appA:compileJava (gradle) and //app/appA:appA (bazel-diff) over 3 commits", lines[0]);
        assertEquals("", lines[1]);
        assertEquals("commit   subject                              gradle key  bazel-diff  verdict       culprit", lines[2]);
        assertEquals("aaaaaaa  baseline                             3f9c…e1     -           base", lines[3]);
        assertEquals("bbbbbbb  utils: body-only edit                3f9c…e1     impacted    clean         bazel≠gradle", lines[4]);
        assertEquals("ccccccc  core: add Greeter.shout() with a ve… 2bb4…77     impacted    CHANGED       core.jar", lines[5]);
        assertEquals("ddddddd  broken                               -           n/a         BUILD-FAILED  Could not resolve com.example:missing:1.0", lines[6]);
        assertEquals("", lines[7]);
        assertEquals("1 of 3 commits changed compile inputs of appA. First: ccccccc. 1 commit could not be built.", lines[8]);
    }

    @Test void subjectIsTruncatedToThirtySixColumns() {
        String out = TableRenderer.render(":app:appA:compileJava", "//app/appA:appA", rows);
        String row = out.split("\n")[5];
        String subject = row.substring(9, 9 + 36);
        assertTrue(subject.endsWith("…"), "'" + subject + "'");
    }

    @Test void tsvHasOneRowPerCommitAndNoHeader() {
        String tsv = TableRenderer.renderTsv(rows);
        String[] lines = tsv.split("\n");
        assertEquals(4, lines.length);
        assertEquals("c".repeat(40) + "\tcore: add Greeter.shout() with a very long subject line that keeps going\t2bb40a1b2c3d4e5f6a7b8c9d0e1f2a3b77\timpacted\tCHANGED\tcore.jar", lines[2]);
        assertEquals("d".repeat(40) + "\tbroken\t\tn/a\tBUILD-FAILED\tCould not resolve com.example:missing:1.0", lines[3]);
    }

    @Test void summaryWhenNothingChanged() {
        String out = TableRenderer.render(":app:appB:fingerprint", null, rows.subList(0, 2));
        assertTrue(out.startsWith("Tracing inputs of :app:appB:fingerprint (gradle) over 1 commit\n"), out);
        assertTrue(out.endsWith("No commit changed runtime inputs of appB.\n"), out);
    }

    @Test void tsvEscapesTabsAndNewlinesSoColumnsCannotShift() {
        List<Verdict> withControlChars = List.of(
                new Verdict(commit('a', "baseline"), Status.BASE, Optional.of(key("k1")), BazelStatus.NONE, List.of(), Optional.empty()),
                new Verdict(commit('e', "fix: a\tbroken\r\nsubject"), Status.CHANGED, Optional.of(key("k2")), BazelStatus.IMPACTED,
                        List.of("weird\tcrash\r\nreport"), Optional.empty()));
        String tsv = TableRenderer.renderTsv(withControlChars);
        String[] lines = tsv.split("\n", -1);
        assertEquals(3, lines.length); // 2 rows + trailing empty string after the final newline
        // every TSV line must have exactly 5 tabs (6 columns); a stray tab/CR/LF inside a field would shift this.
        for (String line : lines) {
            if (line.isEmpty()) {
                continue;
            }
            assertEquals(5, line.chars().filter(c -> c == '\t').count(), line);
        }
        assertTrue(tsv.contains("fix: a broken  subject"), tsv);
        assertTrue(tsv.contains("weird crash  report"), tsv);
    }

    @Test void truncateAndPadAreSafeForSurrogatePairs() {
        // U+1F600 (grinning face) is one code point but two UTF-16 chars; truncating by char index would split it
        // and print a lone low surrogate as '?'.
        String emoji = "😀";
        List<Verdict> withEmoji = List.of(
                new Verdict(commit('a', "baseline"), Status.BASE, Optional.of(key("k1")), BazelStatus.NONE, List.of(), Optional.empty()),
                new Verdict(commit('e', emoji.repeat(40)), Status.CLEAN, Optional.of(key("k2")), BazelStatus.NONE, List.of(), Optional.empty()));
        String out = TableRenderer.render(":app:appA:compileJava", null, withEmoji);
        String row = out.split("\n")[4];
        assertNoLoneSurrogates(row);
        String subjectColumn = row.substring(9, row.offsetByCodePoints(9, 37));
        assertEquals(37, subjectColumn.codePointCount(0, subjectColumn.length()), subjectColumn);
    }

    private static void assertNoLoneSurrogates(String s) {
        for (int i = 0; i < s.length(); i++) {
            char c = s.charAt(i);
            if (Character.isHighSurrogate(c)) {
                assertTrue(i + 1 < s.length() && Character.isLowSurrogate(s.charAt(i + 1)), "lone high surrogate in: " + s);
                i++;
            } else {
                assertTrue(!Character.isLowSurrogate(c), "lone low surrogate in: " + s);
            }
        }
    }

    @Test void rowsWithNoCulpritHaveNoTrailingSpaces() {
        String out = TableRenderer.render(":app:appA:compileJava", null, rows.subList(0, 1));
        String row = out.split("\n")[3];
        assertEquals(row, row.stripTrailing());
    }

    @Test void summaryFallsBackToTheFullTaskPathWhenThereIsNoAppSegment() {
        String out = TableRenderer.render(":compileJava", null, rows.subList(0, 2));
        assertTrue(out.contains("No commit changed compile inputs of :compileJava."), out);
    }

    @Test void bazelCleanOnAChangedRowGetsTheDisagreementSuffix() {
        List<Verdict> row = List.of(new Verdict(commit('a', "x"), Status.CHANGED, Optional.of(key("k1")), BazelStatus.CLEAN,
                List.of("core.jar"), Optional.empty()));
        String out = TableRenderer.render(":app:appA:compileJava", null, row);
        String line = out.split("\n")[3];
        assertTrue(line.endsWith("core.jar (bazel≠gradle)"), line);
    }
}
```

`tools/tracer/src/main/java/io/joeyang/tracer/cli/KeyComparator.java`:
```java
package io.joeyang.tracer.cli;

import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.Arrays;
import java.util.LinkedHashMap;
import java.util.LinkedHashSet;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.Objects;
import java.util.Optional;
import java.util.Set;
import java.util.TreeSet;
import java.util.stream.Collectors;

/** Pure logic: turns two adjacent keys into a verdict and a short list of culprits. */
public final class KeyComparator {
    static final int MAX_CULPRITS = 4;

    private KeyComparator() {}

    /**
     * @param previous the last successfully obtained key before this commit; empty for the base commit
     * @param current the key at this commit; empty when the build failed
     * @param error the failure reason when {@code current} is empty
     */
    public static Verdict verdict(Commit commit, Optional<TaskKey> previous, Optional<TaskKey> current,
                                  Optional<String> error, BazelStatus bazel) {
        Objects.requireNonNull(commit, "commit must not be null");
        Objects.requireNonNull(previous, "previous must not be null");
        Objects.requireNonNull(current, "current must not be null");
        Objects.requireNonNull(error, "error must not be null");
        Objects.requireNonNull(bazel, "bazel must not be null");
        if (current.isEmpty()) {
            return new Verdict(commit, Status.BUILD_FAILED, Optional.empty(), bazel, List.of(),
                    Optional.of(error.orElse("no cache key obtained")));
        }
        if (previous.isEmpty()) {
            return new Verdict(commit, Status.BASE, current, bazel, List.of(), Optional.empty());
        }
        if (previous.get().key().equals(current.get().key())) {
            return new Verdict(commit, Status.CLEAN, current, bazel, List.of(), Optional.empty());
        }
        return new Verdict(commit, Status.CHANGED, current, bazel, culprits(previous.get(), current.get()), Optional.empty());
    }

    static List<String> culprits(TaskKey before, TaskKey after) {
        // "build logic" goes first unconditionally, ahead of file/value names, so it is never pushed past the
        // MAX_CULPRITS cap and hidden behind "+N more": a build-logic change is usually the most actionable one.
        List<String> names = new ArrayList<>();
        if (!before.implementation().equals(after.implementation())) {
            names.add("build logic");
        }
        Set<String> rest = new LinkedHashSet<>();
        for (String property : union(before.fileFingerprints().keySet(), after.fileFingerprints().keySet())) {
            Map<String, String> a = before.fileFingerprints().getOrDefault(property, Map.of());
            Map<String, String> b = after.fileFingerprints().getOrDefault(property, Map.of());
            Map<String, String> aByName = byDisplayName(a);
            Map<String, String> bByName = byDisplayName(b);
            for (String name : union(aByName.keySet(), bByName.keySet())) {
                if (!Objects.equals(aByName.get(name), bByName.get(name))) {
                    rest.add(name);
                }
            }
        }
        for (String property : union(before.valueFingerprints().keySet(), after.valueFingerprints().keySet())) {
            if (!Objects.equals(before.valueFingerprints().get(property), after.valueFingerprints().get(property))) {
                rest.add(property);
            }
        }
        names.addAll(rest);
        if (names.isEmpty()) {
            return List.of("inputs changed (no fingerprint detail)");
        }
        if (names.size() <= MAX_CULPRITS + 1) {
            return names;
        }
        List<String> capped = new ArrayList<>(names.subList(0, MAX_CULPRITS));
        capped.add("+" + (names.size() - MAX_CULPRITS) + " more");
        return capped;
    }

    /**
     * Paths differ between worktrees, so compare by a location-independent display name. Several paths can share a
     * name (every class file under {@code core/build/classes/...} is "core classes"), so the value is the sorted
     * join of all their hashes: it changes if any one of them changes.
     */
    private static Map<String, String> byDisplayName(Map<String, String> byPath) {
        Map<String, List<String>> grouped = new LinkedHashMap<>();
        byPath.forEach((path, hash) -> grouped.computeIfAbsent(displayName(path), k -> new ArrayList<>()).add(hash));
        Map<String, String> out = new LinkedHashMap<>();
        grouped.forEach((name, hashes) -> out.put(name, hashes.stream().sorted().collect(Collectors.joining("+"))));
        return out;
    }

    /**
     * {@code .../core/build/libs/core.jar → core.jar}; {@code .../app/appA/src/main/java/X.java → appA/src/main/java/X.java};
     * {@code .../appA/build/classes/java/main/... → appA classes}; {@code .../appA/build/resources/main/... → appA resources};
     * {@code ~/.gradle/caches/modules-2/files-2.1/com.google.code.gson/gson/2.11.0/<sha>/gson-2.11.0.jar → com.google.code.gson:gson}
     * (so a version bump is one culprit, not a removed plus an added jar).
     *
     * <p>The worktree root (e.g. {@code .../wt-a1b2c3d/}) is stripped first, so a root path that itself happens to
     * contain {@code /src/} (a user's checkout under {@code ~/src/...}) never leaks into the name, and two commits'
     * worktrees — which differ only by that prefix — compare equal. What remains is matched against the module's
     * own {@code /src/}, using its first occurrence so a resource that happens to be nested under another
     * {@code src} directory (e.g. {@code core/src/main/resources/src/x.txt}) is still named relative to its real
     * module ({@code core/src/...}), not the inner one.
     */
    static String displayName(String path) {
        String p = stripWorktreeRoot(path.replace('\\', '/'));
        int classes = p.lastIndexOf("/build/classes/");
        if (classes >= 0) {
            String module = lastSegmentBefore(p, classes);
            return module.isEmpty() ? "classes" : module + " classes";
        }
        int resources = p.lastIndexOf("/build/resources/");
        if (resources >= 0) {
            String module = lastSegmentBefore(p, resources);
            return module.isEmpty() ? "resources" : module + " resources";
        }
        int cache = p.indexOf("/modules-2/files-2.1/");
        if (cache >= 0) {
            String[] parts = p.substring(cache + "/modules-2/files-2.1/".length()).split("/");
            if (parts.length >= 2) {
                return parts[0] + ":" + parts[1];
            }
        }
        int src = p.indexOf("/src/");
        if (src >= 0) {
            String module = lastSegmentBefore(p, src);
            return module.isEmpty() ? p.substring(src + 1) : module + p.substring(src);
        }
        return p.substring(p.lastIndexOf('/') + 1);
    }

    /**
     * Strips everything up to and including the last {@code wt-<sha7>} path segment, i.e. {@link WorktreePool}'s
     * worktree directory naming ({@link WorktreePool#WT_DIR_NAME}); a path with no such segment is returned as is.
     * The result keeps its leading slash so the rest of {@link #displayName} can treat it uniformly.
     */
    private static String stripWorktreeRoot(String p) {
        String[] segments = p.split("/");
        for (int i = segments.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
            if (WorktreePool.WT_DIR_NAME.matcher(segments[i]).matches()) {
                return "/" + String.join("/", Arrays.asList(segments).subList(i + 1, segments.length));
            }
        }
        return p;
    }

    private static String lastSegmentBefore(String p, int index) {
        int from = p.lastIndexOf('/', index - 1);
        return from < 0 ? "" : p.substring(from + 1, index);
    }

    private static Set<String> union(Set<String> a, Set<String> b) {
        Set<String> u = new TreeSet<>(a);
        u.addAll(b);
        return u;
    }
}
```

```java
package io.joeyang.tracer.cli;

import java.util.List;
import java.util.stream.Collectors;

/** Fixed-width table for humans and TSV for scripts. */
public final class TableRenderer {
    static final int SUBJECT_WIDTH = 36;

    private TableRenderer() {}

    /** @param bazelTarget null when the bazel column was disabled */
    public static String render(String taskPath, String bazelTarget, List<Verdict> rows) {
        StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder();
        int commits = Math.max(0, rows.size() - 1);
        sb.append("Tracing inputs of ").append(taskPath).append(" (gradle)");
        if (bazelTarget != null) {
            sb.append(" and ").append(bazelTarget).append(" (bazel-diff)");
        }
        sb.append(" over ").append(commits).append(commits == 1 ? " commit" : " commits").append("\n\n");
        sb.append(pad("commit", 9)).append(pad("subject", SUBJECT_WIDTH + 1)).append(pad("gradle key", 12))
                .append(pad("bazel-diff", 12)).append(pad("verdict", 14)).append("culprit\n");
        for (Verdict v : rows) {
            String row = pad(v.commit().shortSha(), 9)
                    + pad(truncate(v.commit().subject(), SUBJECT_WIDTH), SUBJECT_WIDTH + 1)
                    + pad(v.key().map(TaskKey::shortKey).orElse("-"), 12)
                    + pad(v.bazel().label(), 12)
                    + pad(v.status().label(), 14)
                    + culpritColumn(v);
            sb.append(row.stripTrailing()).append("\n");
        }
        sb.append("\n").append(summary(taskPath, rows)).append("\n");
        return sb.toString();
    }

    public static String renderTsv(List<Verdict> rows) {
        return rows.stream().map(v -> String.join("\t",
                v.commit().sha(), tsvSafe(v.commit().subject()), v.key().map(TaskKey::key).orElse(""),
                v.bazel().label(), v.status().label(),
                tsvSafe(v.status() == Status.BUILD_FAILED ? v.error().orElse("") : String.join(", ", v.culprits()))))
                .collect(Collectors.joining("\n", "", "\n"));
    }

    /** A tab, CR or LF inside a field would shift the TSV's columns, so each is collapsed to a single space. */
    private static String tsvSafe(String s) {
        return s.replaceAll("[\\t\\r\\n]", " ");
    }

    private static String culpritColumn(Verdict v) {
        if (v.status() == Status.BUILD_FAILED) {
            return v.error().orElse("");
        }
        String culprits = String.join(", ", v.culprits());
        if (v.bazelDisagrees()) {
            return culprits.isEmpty() ? "bazel≠gradle" : culprits + " (bazel≠gradle)";
        }
        return culprits;
    }

    private static String summary(String taskPath, List<Verdict> rows) {
        String app = appName(taskPath);
        String kind = taskPath.endsWith(":fingerprint") ? "runtime inputs" : taskPath.endsWith(":compileJava") ? "compile inputs" : "inputs";
        long changed = rows.stream().filter(v -> v.status() == Status.CHANGED).count();
        long failed = rows.stream().filter(v -> v.status() == Status.BUILD_FAILED).count();
        int commits = Math.max(0, rows.size() - 1);
        StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder();
        if (changed == 0) {
            sb.append("No commit changed ").append(kind).append(" of ").append(app).append(".");
        } else {
            String first = rows.stream().filter(v -> v.status() == Status.CHANGED).findFirst().orElseThrow().commit().shortSha();
            sb.append(changed).append(" of ").append(commits).append(commits == 1 ? " commit" : " commits")
                    .append(" changed ").append(kind).append(" of ").append(app).append(". First: ").append(first).append(".");
        }
        if (failed > 0) {
            sb.append(" ").append(failed).append(failed == 1 ? " commit" : " commits").append(" could not be built.");
        }
        return sb.toString();
    }

    /** {@code :app:appA:compileJava → appA}; falls back to the whole path when there is no app segment to name. */
    static String appName(String taskPath) {
        String[] parts = taskPath.split(":");
        if (parts.length >= 2 && !parts[parts.length - 2].isBlank()) {
            return parts[parts.length - 2];
        }
        return taskPath;
    }

    /**
     * Truncates by code point, not {@code char}, so a surrogate pair (an emoji, mostly) is never split into a lone
     * surrogate followed by the ellipsis.
     */
    private static String truncate(String s, int width) {
        if (s.codePointCount(0, s.length()) <= width) {
            return s;
        }
        int cut = s.offsetByCodePoints(0, width - 1);
        return s.substring(0, cut) + "…";
    }

    /** Pads by code point count, matching {@link #truncate}, so a surrogate pair is counted as one column. */
    private static String pad(String s, int width) {
        int codePoints = s.codePointCount(0, s.length());
        return codePoints >= width ? s + " " : s + " ".repeat(width - codePoints);
    }
}
```

```markdown
## Comparison
`KeyComparator.verdict(commit, previousGoodKey, currentKey, error, bazel)`: no current key → `BUILD-FAILED`; no previous → `base`;
equal keys → `clean`; otherwise `CHANGED` with culprits = file fingerprints that differ (compared by location-independent display
name: `core.jar`, `appA/src/main/java/.../AppA.java`, `appA classes`, and `group:artifact` for jars from the Gradle module cache so a version bump is one culprit), value properties that differ, and `build logic` when the task
```

```bash
Two adjacent TaskKeys differ by worktree-local paths even when nothing
meaningful changed, so culprits are derived by diffing per-input
fingerprints under a location-independent display name (jar/source/file
name, or group:artifact for module-cache jars) rather than raw paths.
```
