```java
package io.joeyang.tracer.cli;

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertEquals;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertTrue;

import java.io.ByteArrayOutputStream;
import java.io.IOException;
import java.io.PrintStream;
import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;
import java.nio.file.Files;
import java.nio.file.Path;
import java.nio.file.StandardCopyOption;
import java.time.Duration;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.stream.Stream;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.io.TempDir;

/** Real git, real Gradle, no Bazel: appA's compile key must change when core gains a public method; appC's must not. */
class EndToEndTest {
    static final List<String> COPY = List.of("settings.gradle.kts", "gradle.properties", "gradle", "gradlew", "build-logic", "utils", "core", "lib", "app");
    @TempDir Path tmp;

    @Test
    void publicMethodInCoreChangesAppAButNotAppC() throws Exception {
        Path source = Path.of(System.getProperty("tracer.repoRoot"));
        Path fixture = tmp.resolve("fixture");
        for (String entry : COPY) {
            copyTree(source.resolve(entry), fixture.resolve(entry));
        }
        Files.writeString(fixture.resolve("settings.gradle.kts"),
                Files.readString(fixture.resolve("settings.gradle.kts")).replace("include(\"tools:tracer\")", ""));
        ProcessRunner runner = new DefaultProcessRunner();
        git(runner, fixture, "init", "-q", "-b", "main");
        git(runner, fixture, "-c", "user.email=e2e@example.com", "-c", "user.name=e2e", "add", "-A");
        git(runner, fixture, "-c", "user.email=e2e@example.com", "-c", "user.name=e2e", "commit", "-q", "-m", "baseline");
        Path greeter = fixture.resolve("core/src/main/java/io/joeyang/tracer/core/Greeter.java");
        String src = Files.readString(greeter);
        int brace = src.lastIndexOf('}');
        Files.writeString(greeter, src.substring(0, brace) + "\n    public String shout(String name) {\n        return greet(name).toUpperCase();\n    }\n}\n");
        git(runner, fixture, "-c", "user.email=e2e@example.com", "-c", "user.name=e2e", "commit", "-q", "-am", "core: add shout");

        KeyProvider keys = new GradleKeyProvider(runner, false, Duration.ofMinutes(10));
        Cli cli = new Cli(runner, fixture, tmp.resolve("scratch"), keys, null);

        String[] appA = trace(cli, "appA");
        assertEquals("CHANGED", appA[4], String.join("|", appA));
        assertTrue(appA[5].contains("core classes"), "compile classpath consumes project deps as class dirs: " + appA[5]);

        String[] appC = trace(cli, "appC");
        assertEquals("clean", appC[4], String.join("|", appC));
    }

    private static String[] trace(Cli cli, String app) throws Exception {
        ByteArrayOutputStream out = new ByteArrayOutputStream();
        ByteArrayOutputStream err = new ByteArrayOutputStream();
        int code = cli.run(Cli.Options.parse(new String[] {app, "--from", "HEAD~1", "--to", "HEAD", "--no-bazel", "--tsv"}),
                new PrintStream(out, true, StandardCharsets.UTF_8), new PrintStream(err, true, StandardCharsets.UTF_8));
        String text = out.toString(StandardCharsets.UTF_8);
        assertTrue(code == Cli.EXIT_OK || code == Cli.EXIT_CHANGED, "exit " + code + "\n" + err + "\n" + text);
        String[] rows = text.split("\n");
        assertEquals(2, rows.length, text);
        return rows[1].split("\t", -1);
    }

    private static void git(ProcessRunner runner, Path cwd, String... args) throws Exception {
        List<String> cmd = new java.util.ArrayList<>(List.of("git"));
        cmd.addAll(List.of(args));
        ProcessRunner.Result r = runner.run(cwd, Duration.ofMinutes(1), cmd);
        assertTrue(r.ok(), String.join(" ", cmd) + " failed: " + r.firstErrorLine());
    }

    /** True when any path segment is a Gradle output dir (`build`, `.gradle`); `build.gradle.kts` and `build-logic` are kept. */
    private static boolean isBuildOutput(Path rel) {
        for (Path segment : rel) {
            String name = segment.toString();
            if (name.equals("build") || name.equals(".gradle")) {
                return true;
            }
        }
        return false;
    }

    private static void copyTree(Path from, Path to) throws IOException {
        try (Stream<Path> paths = Files.walk(from)) {
            for (Path p : paths.toList()) {
                Path rel = from.relativize(p);
                if (isBuildOutput(rel)) {
                    continue;
                }
                Path target = to.resolve(rel);
                if (Files.isDirectory(p)) {
                    Files.createDirectories(target);
                } else {
                    Files.createDirectories(target.getParent());
                    Files.copy(p, target, StandardCopyOption.COPY_ATTRIBUTES);
                }
            }
        }
    }
}
```

```bash

./gradlew :tools:tracer:e2eTest
```

Expected: `BUILD SUCCESSFUL` after roughly one to three minutes (two commits × two apps × a Gradle run each). The culprit is `core classes` because Gradle feeds project dependencies to `compileJava` as class directories, not jars (the fingerprint task sees `core.jar`). If `lib classes` appears as well, that is correct — lib recompiled against the new core ABI — loosen only the contains check, never the verdict.

### Task 15: Scenario script, docs/scenarios.md, README final

**Files:**
- Create: `tools/scenarios.sh` (executable)
- Create: `docs/scenarios.md`
- Modify: `README.md`

The script needs network (dependency bumps re-resolve; `bazel run @maven//:pin` re-pins). `NO_BAZEL=1` skips Bazel entirely. It leaves the user's checkout untouched: all edits happen in a temporary worktree on a temporary branch that is deleted at the end (`KEEP_BRANCH=1` keeps it).

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the script**

`tools/scenarios.sh`:
```bash
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# Replays the teaching commits from docs/scenarios.md on a temporary branch (in a temporary worktree),
# runs ./trace for every app in both flavours, prints the tables and asserts the expected verdicts.
# Env: NO_BAZEL=1 skips the bazel-diff column; KEEP_BRANCH=1 keeps the demo branch.
set -euo pipefail

ROOT="$(cd "$(dirname "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}")/.." && pwd)"
cd "$ROOT"
BRANCH="demo/scenarios-$(date +%Y%m%d-%H%M%S)"
WT="$(mktemp -d "${TMPDIR:-/tmp}/gradle-tracer-scenarios.XXXXXX")"
NO_BAZEL="${NO_BAZEL:-0}"
TRACE_FLAGS=()
[[ "$NO_BAZEL" == 1 ]] && TRACE_FLAGS+=(--no-bazel)
PROJECTS=(utils core lib app:appA app:appB app:appC tools:tracer)
FAILURES=0

cleanup() {
  git -C "$ROOT" worktree remove --force "$WT" >/dev/null 2>&1 || true
  rm -rf "$WT"
  [[ "${KEEP_BRANCH:-0}" == 1 ]] || git -C "$ROOT" branch -D "$BRANCH" >/dev/null 2>&1 || true
}
trap cleanup EXIT

say() { printf '\n== %s\n' "$*"; }

# edit FILE OLD NEW — exact replacement; fails loudly unless OLD occurs exactly once.
edit() {
  python3 - "$1" "$2" "$3" <<'PY'
import pathlib, sys
path, old, new = sys.argv[1:4]
p = pathlib.Path(path)
text = p.read_text()
n = text.count(old)
if n != 1:
    sys.exit(f"scenarios: expected exactly one occurrence of {old!r} in {path}, found {n}")
p.write_text(text.replace(old, new))
PY
}

relock() {
  local tasks=()
  for p in "${PROJECTS[@]}"; do tasks+=(":$p:dependencies"); done
  (cd "$WT" && ./gradlew -q "${tasks[@]}" --write-locks >/dev/null)
  [[ "$NO_BAZEL" == 1 ]] || (cd "$WT" && REPIN=1 bazel run --ui_event_filters=-info,-stdout --noshow_progress @maven//:pin >/dev/null)
}

commit() { git -C "$WT" add -A >/dev/null && git -C "$WT" commit -q -m "$1" && git -C "$WT" rev-parse HEAD; }

say "creating $BRANCH in $WT"
git worktree add -q -b "$BRANCH" "$WT" HEAD
BASE="$(git -C "$WT" rev-parse HEAD)"

say "S1 utils: body-only edit"
edit "$WT/utils/src/main/java/io/joeyang/tracer/utils/Formatter.java" \
'        return text + " ".repeat(width - text.length());   // SCENARIO S1 edits this line (body only)' \
'        StringBuilder padded = new StringBuilder(text);
        while (padded.length() < width) {
            padded.append(" ");
        }
        return padded.toString();'
commit "utils: pad with a loop (body only)" >/dev/null

say "S2 core: add a public method"
edit "$WT/core/src/main/java/io/joeyang/tracer/core/Greeter.java" \
'        return Formatter.pad("Hello,", column) + name + "!";
    }
}' \
'        return Formatter.pad("Hello,", column) + name + "!";
    }

    public String shout(String name) {
        return greet(name).toUpperCase();
    }
}'
commit "core: add Greeter.shout()" >/dev/null

say "S3 lib: commons-lang3 3.18.0 -> 3.20.0 (relocking; needs network)"
edit "$WT/gradle/libs.versions.toml" 'commons-lang3 = "3.18.0"' 'commons-lang3 = "3.20.0"'
edit "$WT/MODULE.bazel" '"org.apache.commons:commons-lang3:3.18.0"' '"org.apache.commons:commons-lang3:3.20.0"'
relock
commit "deps: commons-lang3 3.18.0 -> 3.20.0" >/dev/null

say "S4 appC: gson 2.10.1 -> 2.11.0 (relocking; needs network)"
edit "$WT/gradle/libs.versions.toml" 'gson = "2.10.1"' 'gson = "2.11.0"'
edit "$WT/MODULE.bazel" '"com.google.code.gson:gson:2.10.1"' '"com.google.code.gson:gson:2.11.0"'
relock
commit "deps: gson 2.10.1 -> 2.11.0" >/dev/null

say "S5 core: comment-only edit"
edit "$WT/core/src/main/java/io/joeyang/tracer/core/Greeter.java" '    // Greets by name.' '    // Greets the given name.'
commit "core: reword a comment" >/dev/null

say "S6 build logic: -Xlint:all in both builds"
edit "$WT/build-logic/src/main/java/io/joeyang/tracer/buildlogic/JavaBaseConventionPlugin.java" \
'            task.getOptions().setEncoding("UTF-8");' \
'            task.getOptions().setEncoding("UTF-8");
            task.getOptions().getCompilerArgs().add("-Xlint:all");'
edit "$WT/tools/defs.bzl" 'JAVACOPTS = ["-Xlint:deprecation"]' 'JAVACOPTS = ["-Xlint:deprecation", "-Xlint:all"]'
TIP="$(commit "build: enable -Xlint:all in both builds")"

# Expected gradle verdicts for S1..S6 (C = CHANGED, - = clean), per app and task.
declare -A EXPECT=(
  [appA:compileJava]="- C - - - C"  [appA:fingerprint]="C C C - - C"
  [appB:compileJava]="- C - - - C"  [appB:fingerprint]="C C - - - C"
  [appC:compileJava]="- - - C - C"  [appC:fingerprint]="C - - C - C"
)
# Expected bazel-diff column for S1..S6 (checked on the compileJava pass only; bazel-diff hashes all transitive inputs regardless of task).
declare -A EXPECT_BZ=( [appA]="C C C - C C" [appB]="C C - - C C" [appC]="C - - C - C" )

check() {  # check LABEL EXPECTED ACTUAL
  if [[ "$2" == "$3" ]]; then printf 'OK   %-22s %s\n' "$1" "$3"; else printf 'FAIL %-22s expected [%s] got [%s]\n' "$1" "$2" "$3"; FAILURES=$((FAILURES + 1)); fi
}

for app in appA appB appC; do
  for task in compileJava fingerprint; do
    flags=("${TRACE_FLAGS[@]}")
    # bazel-diff does not depend on the Gradle task, so one Bazel pass per app (the compileJava run) is enough.
    [[ "$task" == fingerprint ]] && flags+=(--no-bazel)
    say "./trace $app --from ${BASE:0:7} --to ${TIP:0:7} --task $task"
    TSV="$WT/.trace-$app-$task.tsv"
    ./trace "$app" --from "$BASE" --to "$TIP" --task "$task" --tsv-out "$TSV" "${flags[@]}" || true
    gradle_actual="$(awk -F'\t' 'NR>1 { printf "%s%s", (NR>2 ? " " : ""), ($5=="CHANGED" ? "C" : ($5=="clean" ? "-" : "?")) }' "$TSV")"
    check "$app $task" "${EXPECT[$app:$task]}" "$gradle_actual"
    if [[ "$NO_BAZEL" != 1 && "$task" == compileJava ]]; then
      bazel_actual="$(awk -F'\t' 'NR>1 { printf "%s%s", (NR>2 ? " " : ""), ($4=="impacted" ? "C" : ($4=="clean" ? "-" : "?")) }' "$TSV")"
      check "$app bazel-diff" "${EXPECT_BZ[$app]}" "$bazel_actual"
    fi
  done
done

cat <<'LEGEND'

Why the columns disagree (see docs/scenarios.md):
  S1 body-only edit      compile: ABI unchanged, nothing recompiles · fingerprint/bazel: bytes/sources changed
  S3 commons-lang3 bump  compile: implementation dep of lib, not on appA's compile classpath · fingerprint/bazel: changed
  S5 comment-only edit   gradle: identical bytecode · bazel-diff: hashes sources, so "impacted"
  S6 build logic         every Gradle task key includes the build-logic jar; every Bazel target hashes its javacopts
LEGEND

if [[ "$FAILURES" -eq 0 ]]; then say "all expectations held"; else say "$FAILURES expectation(s) failed"; fi
exit "$FAILURES"
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Run it**

```bash
chmod +x tools/scenarios.sh
tools/scenarios.sh
```
Expected: six tables, then `OK` lines for every app/task pair (and three `OK ... bazel-diff` lines), `all expectations held`, exit 0. Budget 20–40 minutes: two relocks plus 21 bazel-diff analyses (3 apps × 7 commits) dominate.

If a cell disagrees with the expectation, do not edit `EXPECT` to match. Reproduce the single case by hand (`./trace <app> --from <base> --to <tip> --task <task>` and `./trace <app> --at <sha> --task <task>` on both sides), understand which fingerprint moved, and record the finding in `docs/scenarios.md`. Only then change the expectation, with the reason next to it.

- [ ] **Step 3: docs/scenarios.md**

`docs/scenarios.md`:
```markdown
# Scenarios

`tools/scenarios.sh` replays these commits on a throwaway branch and runs `./trace` for every app in both flavours.
`cj` = `compileJava` key, `fp` = `fingerprint` key, `bz` = bazel-diff. `C` changed/impacted, `-` clean.

| # | commit | appA cj/fp/bz | appB cj/fp/bz | appC cj/fp/bz | lesson |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| S1 | utils: body-only edit | - / C / C | - / C / C | - / C / C | ABI unchanged ⇒ no recompiles; runtime bytes differ everywhere |
| S2 | core: add public method | C / C / C | C / C / C | - / - / - | ABI change reaches appA through `api`; appC untouched |
| S3 | lib: commons-lang3 3.18.0 → 3.20.0 | - / C / C | - / - / - | - / - / - | `implementation` dep: runtime-only input for appA |
| S4 | appC: gson 2.10.1 → 2.11.0 | - / - / - | - / - / - | C / C / C | direct dep whose ABI differs |
| S5 | core: comment-only edit | - / - / C | - / - / C | - / - / - | bazel-diff hashes sources; Gradle hashes bytecode |
| S6 | build logic: `-Xlint:all` in both builds | C / C / C | C / C / C | C / C / C | every Gradle task key includes the build-logic jar (implementation hash); every Bazel target hashes its `javacopts` |

## Findings
(Record here anything the run showed that differs from the table, with the fingerprint that moved.)
```

## Self-review checklist (run after writing, before handing over)

- Spec §5 CLI: `--from/--to`, `--at`, `--task`, `--no-bazel`, `--offline`, `--keep-worktrees` → Tasks 12–13; `./trace` wrapper → Task 14; `--tsv`/`--tsv-out` added for the scenario script (spec §14).
- Spec §6 components: every row has a task (ProcessRunner 6/11, RevisionRange + WorktreePool 9, GradleKeyProvider + CacheDebugLog 7, BazelDiffProvider 8, KeyComparator + TableRenderer 10, Cli 12–13, wrapper + e2e 14, FingerprintTask 5, convention plugins 1, Bazel build 4).
- Spec §8 scenarios: Task 15 asserts the table; S6 runtime column corrected to `C` (build-logic jar is part of every task's implementation hash).
- Spec §9 failure handling: unknown rev/app/empty range (9, 12, 13), stale worktrees and scratch files (9, 13), Gradle failure per commit (13), bazel missing (8, 13), `--offline` (7, 8, 13), hang (11), Ctrl-C (9), unrecognised debug output (7).
- Spec §10 tests: all present. Spec §11 docs: README (1, 14, 15), gradle-build (1, 5), bazel-build (4, 8), tracer (6, 7, 8, 9, 10, 12, 13, 14), scenarios (15).
